# Beatmap Service Rewrite Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Rewrite the osu! beatmap service in the style of `~/Projects/dpp-processor-backend-new` (DI, interfaces at every boundary, unit/integration/e2e tests), keeping its HTTP contract, adding validation, stored beatmapsets, managed API keys, UTC dates, and dump-based cache population.

**Architecture:** One package. Plumbing copied from dpp's `@dpp/server` into `src/server/`. Express 5 controllers call services (`BeatmapService`, `BeatmapsetService`, `BeatmapFileService`), which use repositories (Drizzle/Postgres), a file store, and the ported `OsuGateway`/`RateLimiter`. A CLI (`dist/cli.mjs`) manages keys, baselines the database, populates the cache, and fixes dates.

**Tech Stack:** Node 24, TypeScript 7 (typecheck), tsdown (build), Express 5, tsyringe, zod 4, Drizzle ORM 0.45 + `pg`, `p-queue`, `express-rate-limit`, vitest 5, supertest, PGlite, oxlint, oxfmt.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-rewrite-design.md` (read it before starting any task).

## Global Constraints

- Work on branch `rewrite`. Node 24 (`engines: ">=24"`), pnpm 12.
- Imports carry no `.js` extension. Imports that would climb a directory (`../`) use `@/…` (`src/`) or `@test/…` (`tests/`); `./` imports stay relative. Exception: files run directly by Node (`scripts/*.ts`, `tests/fixtures/**`, `tests/golden/cases.ts`, `tests/utils/stubOsu.ts`) import each other with explicit `.ts` extensions and use only erasable TypeScript syntax (no enums, no parameter properties), because `scripts/record-golden.ts` runs them with Node's type stripping.
- Every error body is `{ error: string }`. Unknown routes keep Express's default HTML 404 (never a JSON `{ error }` body).
- A `404` with `{ error: string }` means only "osu! has no such beatmap/set/file". osu! unavailable → `503` + `Retry-After` + `{ error }`. Database read failure → `503 { error: "Database unavailable" }` + `Retry-After: 5`.
- Env variable names from the spec are exact: `EXPRESS_PORT`, `POSTGRES_HOSTNAME`, `POSTGRES_PORT`, `POSTGRES_DB_NAME`, `POSTGRES_USERNAME`, `POSTGRES_PASSWORD`, `OSU_API_KEY`, `INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY`, `BULK_SERVER_KEY`, `OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT` (55), `OSU_FILE_RATE_LIMIT` (55), `OSU_TOTAL_RATE_LIMIT` (55), `BULK_SHARE` (0.5), `PRIORITY_MAX_WAIT_MS` (8000), `BULK_MAX_WAIT_MS` (5000), `OSU_API_BASE_URL` (`https://osu.ppy.sh/api`), `OSU_FILE_BASE_URL` (`https://osu.ppy.sh/osu`), `BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY` (`beatmaps`), `RECHECK_QUALIFIED_MINUTES` (15), `RECHECK_PENDING_MINUTES` (60), `RECHECK_GRAVEYARD_MINUTES` (1440), `BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE` (10000), `BEATMAPSET_CACHE_SIZE` (2000), `ANON_RATE_LIMIT` (30), `KEY_RELOAD_INTERVAL_SECONDS` (30), `TRUST_PROXY` (`false`).
- Memory cache entries live 15 minutes.
- Log prefix `[beatmap-service]`. Never log API keys (osu! or ours); `OsuGateway` already redacts `k=`.
- Never stop test servers with `pkill -f`; stop the recorded PID or dispose the handle.
- Naming: method and function names start with a verb (`getBeatmap`, `buildApiBeatmap`); fields and properties are nouns named after what they hold (`osuGateway`, `beatmapRepository`, `pendingLookups`); booleans read as a question (`isBusy`, `shouldRestart`). Code copied unchanged from dpp (`src/server/**`, the broker's CLI helpers) keeps dpp's names.
- Tests run on at most two threads (`maxWorkers: 2` in `vitest.config.mts`; the e2e config uses one). Never raise it or pass a larger `--maxWorkers`.
- Formatting: oxfmt with dpp's `.oxfmtrc.json` (4 spaces, width 80). Code blocks in this plan are not pre-formatted; run `pnpm format` before each commit.
- Before each commit: `pnpm typecheck && pnpm lint && pnpm test` pass (from Task 2 on).

## Review Focus

1. **The same uncached beatmap requested at once by both lanes** should reach osu! once. Test: `BeatmapService` unit test "shares one lookup across lanes" (Task 11); `BeatmapFileService` "shares one download across lanes" (Task 13).
2. **Postgres down while osu! is up** should answer 503 without spending osu! budget. Test: `BeatmapService` unit test "answers DatabaseUnavailableError without asking osu!" (Task 11), and the controller integration test "maps a database outage to 503" (Task 15).
3. **Odd query strings** (`id=1&id=2`, `id=+5`, `id=0`, `id=2147483648`, ` 75`, uppercase hash, `hash` with 31 characters) should get 400, never a crash or a NaN lookup. Test: validation unit tests (Task 6).
4. **A cached `.osu` file that is truncated or corrupt** should be replaced, not served. Test: `BeatmapFileService` "replaces a stored file whose MD5 does not match" (Task 13).
5. **An unwritable beatmap directory** should still serve the downloaded file and log the failure. Test: `BeatmapFileService` "serves the file when storing it fails" (Task 13).

Task 8 creates `src/dependencies/tokens.ts`; each later task adds its own entries. The final file is:

```ts
import type { IKeyService } from "@/access/IKeyService";
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { OsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapFileStore";
import type { IBeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapRepository";
import type { IBeatmapsetRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapsetRepository";
import type { ICursorRepository } from "@/repositories/ICursorRepository";
import type { IKeyRepository } from "@/repositories/IKeyRepository";
import type { IBeatmapFileService } from "@/services/IBeatmapFileService";
import type { IBeatmapService } from "@/services/IBeatmapService";
import type { IBeatmapsetService } from "@/services/IBeatmapsetService";
import type { InjectionToken } from "tsyringe";

/**
 * Tokens for dependency injection. `Symbol.for(...) as InjectionToken<T>` is how tsyringe gives a symbol token its type.
 */
export const dependencyTokens = {
    config: Symbol.for("config") as InjectionToken<Config>,
    db: Symbol.for("db") as InjectionToken<Database>,
    osuGatewayOptions: Symbol.for("osuGatewayOptions") as InjectionToken<OsuGatewayOptions>,
    osuGateway: Symbol.for("osuGateway") as InjectionToken<IOsuGateway>,
    beatmapRepository: Symbol.for("beatmapRepository") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapRepository>,
    beatmapsetRepository: Symbol.for("beatmapsetRepository") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapsetRepository>,
    cursorRepository: Symbol.for("cursorRepository") as InjectionToken<ICursorRepository>,
    keyRepository: Symbol.for("keyRepository") as InjectionToken<IKeyRepository>,
    beatmapFileStore: Symbol.for("beatmapFileStore") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapFileStore>,
    beatmapService: Symbol.for("beatmapService") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapService>,
    beatmapsetService: Symbol.for("beatmapsetService") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapsetService>,
    beatmapFileService: Symbol.for("beatmapFileService") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapFileService>,
    keyService: Symbol.for("keyService") as InjectionToken<IKeyService>,
} satisfies Record<string, InjectionToken<unknown>>;
```

```ts
// Run directly by Node in scripts/record-golden.ts: erasable syntax only, `.ts` import extensions.
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";
import type { FakeOsuState } from "./fakeOsu.ts";

export function computeFixtureMD5(data: Buffer | string): string {
    return createHash("md5").update(data).digest("hex");
}

/**
 * A small valid osu!standard `.osu` file with two circles (max combo 2). Different arguments give different MD5s.
 */
export function buildOsuFile(id: number, version: string): Buffer {
    return Buffer.from(
        [
            "osu file format v14",
            "",
            "[General]",
            "AudioFilename: audio.mp3",
            "Mode: 0",
            "",
            "[Metadata]",
            "Title:DISCO PRINCE",
            "Artist:Kenji Ninuma",
            "Creator:peppy",
            `Version:${version}`,
            `BeatmapID:${id.toString()}`,
            "BeatmapSetID:1",
            "",
            "[Difficulty]",
            "HPDrainRate:6",
            "CircleSize:4",
            "OverallDifficulty:6",
            "ApproachRate:6",
            "SliderMultiplier:1.4",
            "SliderTickRate:1",
            "",
            "[TimingPoints]",
            "0,500,4,2,0,100,1,0",
            "",
            "[HitObjects]",
            "256,192,1000,1,0,0:0:0:0:",
            "320,192,1500,1,0,0:0:0:0:",
            "",
        ].join("\r\n"),
    );
}

/**
 * An osu! API v1 `get_beatmaps` entry. Values are plain decimals so that they survive `real` columns unchanged.
 */
export function buildApiBeatmap(
    overrides: Partial<OsuAPIResponse> = {},
): OsuAPIResponse {
    return {
        approved: "1",
        submit_date: "2007-10-06 17:46:31",
        approved_date: "2007-10-06 17:46:31",
        last_update: "2007-10-06 17:46:31",
        artist: "Kenji Ninuma",
        beatmap_id: "75",
        beatmapset_id: "1",
        bpm: "119.5",
        creator: "peppy",
        creator_id: "2",
        difficultyrating: "2.5",
        diff_aim: "1.25",
        diff_speed: "1.125",
        diff_size: "4",
        diff_overall: "6",
        diff_approach: "6",
        diff_drain: "6",
        hit_length: "108",
        source: "",
        genre_id: "2",
        language_id: "3",
        title: "DISCO PRINCE",
        total_length: "142",
        version: "Normal",
        file_md5: "00000000000000000000000000000000",
        mode: "0",
        tags: "katamari",
        favourite_count: "100",
        rating: "8.5",
        playcount: "1000",
        passcount: "500",
        count_normal: "160",
        count_slider: "30",
        count_spinner: "4",
        max_combo: "314",
        storyboard: "0",
        video: "0",
        download_unavailable: "0",
        audio_unavailable: "0",
        packs: "S1,T44",
        ...overrides,
    };
}

/**
 * Beatmapset 1: beatmap 75 (standard, with a file), 76 (standard, osu! has no file), and 1001 (taiko, with a file).
 */
export function createGoldenOsuState(): FakeOsuState {
    const file75 = buildOsuFile(75, "Normal");
    const file1001 = buildOsuFile(1001, "Taiko");

    return {
        beatmaps: [
            buildApiBeatmap({ file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(file75) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({
                beatmap_id: "76",
                version: "Hard",
                file_md5: "11111111111111111111111111111111",
                max_combo: null,
            }),
            buildApiBeatmap({
                beatmap_id: "1001",
                version: "Taiko",
                mode: "1",
                file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(file1001),
            }),
        ],
        files: new Map([
            [75, file75],
            [1001, file1001],
        ]),
        rateLimited: false,
    };
}
```

`tests/fixtures/osu/fakeOsu.ts`:

```ts
// Run directly by Node in scripts/record-golden.ts: erasable syntax only.
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import type { IncomingMessage, ServerResponse } from "node:http";

export interface FakeOsuState {
    beatmaps: OsuAPIResponse[];
    files: Map<number, Buffer>;
    /** Answer every request with 429 and `Retry-After: 30`. */
    rateLimited: boolean;
}

/**
 * Serves `GET /api/get_beatmaps` (`b`, `h`, `s`, or `since` with `m` and `limit`) and `GET /osu/<id>` from `state`.
 * `since` is inclusive and orders by approval date, then beatmap ID.
 */
export function createFakeOsuHandler(
    state: FakeOsuState,
): (req: IncomingMessage, res: ServerResponse) => void {
    return (req, res) => {
        const url = new URL(req.url ?? "", "http://fake-osu");

        if (state.rateLimited) {
            res.writeHead(429, { "Retry-After": "30" }).end();
            return;
        }

        if (url.pathname === "/api/get_beatmaps") {
            const params = url.searchParams;
            let list = state.beatmaps;
            const id = params.get("b");
            const hash = params.get("h");
            const set = params.get("s");
            const since = params.get("since");

            if (id !== null) {
                list = list.filter((b) => b.beatmap_id === id);
            }

            if (hash !== null) {
                list = list.filter((b) => b.file_md5 === hash);
            }

            if (set !== null) {
                list = list.filter((b) => b.beatmapset_id === set);
            }

            if (since !== null) {
                const mode = params.get("m");

                list = list
                    .filter(
                        (b) =>
                            b.approved_date !== null &&
                            b.approved_date >= since &&
                            (mode === null || b.mode === mode),
                    )
                    .sort(
                        (a, b) =>
                            (a.approved_date ?? "").localeCompare(
                                b.approved_date ?? "",
                            ) || Number(a.beatmap_id) - Number(b.beatmap_id),
                    )
                    .slice(0, Number(params.get("limit") ?? "500"));
            }

            res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "application/json" }).end(
                JSON.stringify(list),
            );
            return;
        }

        const file = /^\/osu\/(\d+)$/.exec(url.pathname);

        if (file) {
            const data = state.files.get(Number(file[1]));

            if (data) {
                res.writeHead(200, { "Content-Type": "text/plain" }).end(data);
            } else {
                res.writeHead(404).end();
            }

            return;
        }

        res.writeHead(404).end();
    };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the golden cases**

`tests/golden/cases.ts`:

```ts
// Run directly by Node in scripts/record-golden.ts: erasable syntax only.
import { computeFixtureMD5, buildOsuFile } from "../fixtures/osu/beatmaps.ts";

export interface GoldenCase {
    readonly name: string;
    /** Path and query, relative to the server. */
    readonly path: string;
    /** Make osu! answer 429 before this request. */
    readonly rateLimited?: boolean;
    /** Why the rewrite answers differently; the contract test checks the new answer explicitly. */
    readonly changeReason?: string;
}

export interface GoldenResponse {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly status: number;
    /** Only `content-type`, `content-disposition`, and `retry-after`, when present. */
    readonly headers: Record<string, string>;
    /** Parsed JSON for JSON responses, otherwise the text. */
    readonly body: unknown;
}

export const priorityKey = "golden-priority-key";
export const bulkKey = "golden-bulk-key";

const hash75 = computeFixtureMD5(buildOsuFile(75, "Normal"));

/**
 * Run in this order: earlier requests fill the caches later ones rely on, and the rate-limited case comes last.
 */
export const goldenCases: readonly GoldenCase[] = [
    { name: "getbeatmap by id", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=75&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmap by hash", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?hash=${hash75}&key=${bulkKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmap not found", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=999&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmapfile by id", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?id=75&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmapfile by hash", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?hash=${hash75}&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmapfile without a file", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?id=76&key=${priorityKey}` },
    {
        name: "getbeatmapfile not found",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?id=999&key=${priorityKey}`,
        changeReason: "metadata is resolved first, so an unknown beatmap is 404 Beatmap not found",
    },
    {
        name: "getbeatmapfile of a taiko beatmap",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?id=1001&key=${priorityKey}`,
        changeReason: "metadata is resolved first, so a non-standard beatmap is 404 Beatmap not found",
    },
    { name: "getbeatmaps", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmaps?id=1&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "getbeatmaps not found", path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmaps?id=999&key=${priorityKey}` },
    { name: "wrong key", path: "/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=75&key=wrong" },
    {
        name: "getbeatmap without parameters",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?key=${priorityKey}`,
        changeReason: "message is Missing id or hash",
    },
    {
        name: "getbeatmapfile without parameters",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?key=${priorityKey}`,
        changeReason: "message is Missing id or hash",
    },
    {
        name: "getbeatmaps without parameters",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmaps?key=${priorityKey}`,
        changeReason: "message is Missing id",
    },
    {
        name: "no key",
        path: "/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=75",
        changeReason: "requests without a key are anonymous (bulk lane, limited per IP)",
    },
    {
        name: "osu! rate limited",
        path: `/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=77&key=${priorityKey}`,
        rateLimited: true,
    },
];
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the recorder**

`scripts/record-golden.ts`:

```ts
// Records the responses of the pre-rewrite service (`dist/index.js`, built with `tsc`) into
// tests/golden/responses.json. It was run once, in Task 1 of the rewrite plan, before the old code was removed;
// it cannot run against the rewritten service.
//
// Run with `node scripts/record-golden.ts` (Node strips the types).
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { PGLiteSocketServer } from "@electric-sql/pglite-socket";
import { spawn } from "node:child_process";
import { mkdir, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { createServer } from "node:net";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { createGoldenOsuState } from "../tests/fixtures/osu/beatmaps.ts";
import { createFakeOsuHandler } from "../tests/fixtures/osu/fakeOsu.ts";
import {
    bulkKey,
    goldenCases,
    priorityKey,
    type GoldenResponse,
} from "../tests/golden/cases.ts";
import { startStubOsu } from "../tests/utils/stubOsu.ts";

const root = fileURLToPath(new URL("..", import.meta.url));

function findFreePort(): Promise<number> {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const server = createServer().listen(0, "127.0.0.1", () => {
            const address = server.address();

            server.close(() =>
                typeof address === "object" && address
                    ? resolve(address.port)
                    : reject(new Error("no port")),
            );
        });
    });
}

const recordedHeaders = ["content-type", "content-disposition", "retry-after"];

async function main(): Promise<void> {
    const work = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "golden-"));
    const beatmapDirectory = join(work, "beatmaps");

    await mkdir(beatmapDirectory);

    const database = new PGlite();

    await database.exec(
        await readFile(join(root, "tests/fixtures/production-schema.sql"), "utf8"),
    );

    const postgresPort = await findFreePort();
    const postgres = new PGLiteSocketServer({
        db: database,
        host: "127.0.0.1",
        port: postgresPort,
        maxConnections: 10,
    });

    await postgres.start();

    const state = createGoldenOsuState();
    const osu = await startStubOsu();

    osu.handle(createFakeOsuHandler(state));

    const port = await findFreePort();
    // The cwd is the temporary directory, so the old service's `dotenv` finds no `.env`.
    const child = spawn("node", [join(root, "dist/index.js")], {
        cwd: work,
        env: {
            PATH: process.env.PATH,
            EXPRESS_PORT: port.toString(),
            POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: "127.0.0.1",
            POSTGRES_PORT: postgresPort.toString(),
            POSTGRES_DB_NAME: "postgres",
            POSTGRES_USERNAME: "postgres",
            POSTGRES_PASSWORD: "postgres",
            OSU_API_KEY: "golden-osu-key",
            INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: priorityKey,
            BULK_SERVER_KEY: bulkKey,
            OSU_API_BASE_URL: `${osu.url}/api`,
            OSU_FILE_BASE_URL: `${osu.url}/osu`,
            BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY: beatmapDirectory,
        },
        stdio: "inherit",
    });

    try {
        const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port.toString()}`;
        const deadline = Date.now() + 20_000;

        while (!(await fetch(`${base}/api/beatmap/getbeatmap`).then(() => true, () => false))) {
            if (Date.now() > deadline || child.exitCode !== null) {
                throw new Error("The old service did not start");
            }

            await sleep(200);
        }

        const responses: GoldenResponse[] = [];

        for (const golden of goldenCases) {
            state.rateLimited = golden.rateLimited ?? false;

            const response = await fetch(`${base}${golden.path}`);
            const headers: Record<string, string> = {};

            for (const name of recordedHeaders) {
                const value = response.headers.get(name);

                if (value !== null) {
                    headers[name] = value;
                }
            }

            const text = await response.text();
            const body: unknown = headers["content-type"]?.startsWith(
                "application/json",
            )
                ? JSON.parse(text)
                : text;

            responses.push({ name: golden.name, status: response.status, headers, body });
            console.log(`${response.status.toString()} ${golden.name}`);
        }

        await writeFile(
            join(root, "tests/golden/responses.json"),
            `${JSON.stringify(responses, null, 4)}\n`,
        );
    } finally {
        child.kill();
        await osu[Symbol.asyncDispose]();
        await postgres.stop();
        await database.close();
        await rm(work, { recursive: true, force: true });
    }
}

await main();
```

```ts
import { cp } from "node:fs/promises";
import { builtinModules } from "node:module";
import { defineConfig } from "tsdown";

/**
 * Packages left out of the bundle. `pg` optionally requires `pg-native`, which is not installed.
 */
const externalPackages = ["pg-native"];

function isExternal(id: string): boolean {
    return (
        id.startsWith("node:") ||
        builtinModules.includes(id) ||
        externalPackages.some(
            (name) => id === name || id.startsWith(`${name}/`),
        )
    );
}

// `src/polyfills.ts` must be the first import of both entries: the bundler evaluates reflect-metadata lazily, so a
// later import lets tsyringe load before the Reflect polyfill exists.
export default defineConfig({
    entry: { index: "src/index.ts", cli: "src/cli.ts" },
    format: "esm",
    platform: "node",
    tsconfig: "tsconfig.build.json",
    outDir: "dist",
    clean: true,
    deps: {
        neverBundle: externalPackages,
        alwaysBundle: (id) => !isExternal(id),
        onlyBundle: false,
    },
    onSuccess: async () => {
        await cp("drizzle", "dist/drizzle", { recursive: true });
    },
});
```

```ts
import {
    APIError,
    BadRequestError,
    ServiceUnavailableError,
    UnauthorizedError,
} from "@/server/errors";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("API errors", () => {
    it("answers with the message as the error body", () => {
        const error = new APIError(418, "I am a teapot");

        expect(error.statusCode).toBe(418);
        expect(error.toResponse()).toEqual({ error: "I am a teapot" });
        expect(error.headers).toEqual({});
        expect(error.name).toBe("APIError");
    });

    it("has the conventional subclasses", () => {
        expect(new BadRequestError("Invalid id").statusCode).toBe(400);
        expect(new UnauthorizedError().toResponse()).toEqual({
            error: "Unauthorized",
        });
        expect(new UnauthorizedError("API key required").message).toBe(
            "API key required",
        );

        const unavailable = new ServiceUnavailableError("Database unavailable", 5);

        expect(unavailable.statusCode).toBe(503);
        expect(unavailable.headers).toEqual({ "Retry-After": "5" });
        expect(unavailable.name).toBe("ServiceUnavailableError");
    });
});
```

```ts
import { ServiceUnavailableError } from "@/server/errors";
import { BaseController } from "@/server/http/BaseController";
import { errorHandler } from "@/server/http/errorHandlers";
import express, { type Response } from "express";
import request from "supertest";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

class TestController extends BaseController {
    fail(res: Response, error: unknown): void {
        this.handleError(res, error);
    }
}

describe("errorHandler", () => {
    it("answers API errors with their status, headers, and body", async () => {
        const app = express()
            .get("/", () => {
                throw new ServiceUnavailableError("Database unavailable", 5);
            })
            .use(errorHandler);

        const response = await request(app).get("/");

        expect(response.status).toBe(503);
        expect(response.headers["retry-after"]).toBe("5");
        expect(response.body).toEqual({ error: "Database unavailable" });
    });

    it("answers anything else with 500 and logs it", async () => {
        const log = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
        const app = express()
            .get("/", () => {
                throw new Error("boom");
            })
            .use(errorHandler);

        const response = await request(app).get("/");

        expect(response.status).toBe(500);
        expect(response.body).toEqual({ error: "Internal server error" });
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it("leaves unknown routes to Express's HTML 404", async () => {
        const response = await request(express().use(errorHandler)).get("/nope");

        expect(response.status).toBe(404);
        expect(response.headers["content-type"]).toMatch(/^text\/html/);
    });
});

describe("BaseController.handleError", () => {
    it("answers API errors and hides unexpected ones", async () => {
        vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);

        const controller = new TestController();
        const app = express()
            .get("/api", (_req, res) =>
                controller.fail(res, new ServiceUnavailableError("down", 7)),
            )
            .get("/other", (_req, res) => controller.fail(res, new Error("x")));

        const api = await request(app).get("/api");

        expect(api.status).toBe(503);
        expect(api.headers["retry-after"]).toBe("7");
        expect(api.body).toEqual({ error: "down" });

        const other = await request(app).get("/other");

        expect(other.status).toBe(500);
        expect(other.body).toEqual({ error: "Internal server error" });
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace the four adapted files**

`src/server/errors.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The body of every error response.
 */
export interface ErrorResponse {
    readonly error: string;
}

/**
 * An error that maps to an HTTP response with the body `{ error: message }`.
 */
export class APIError extends Error {
    constructor(
        readonly statusCode: number,
        message: string,
        readonly headers: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {},
    ) {
        super(message);
        this.name = new.target.name;
    }

    /**
     * Builds the response body for this error.
     */
    toResponse(): ErrorResponse {
        return { error: this.message };
    }
}

export class BadRequestError extends APIError {
    constructor(message: string) {
        super(400, message);
    }
}

export class UnauthorizedError extends APIError {
    constructor(message = "Unauthorized") {
        super(401, message);
    }
}

export class ServiceUnavailableError extends APIError {
    constructor(message: string, retryAfterSeconds: number) {
        super(503, message, { "Retry-After": retryAfterSeconds.toString() });
    }
}
```

`src/server/http/BaseController.ts`:

```ts
import { APIError, type ErrorResponse } from "../errors";
import type { Response } from "express";

/**
 * Base class for controllers.
 */
export abstract class BaseController {
    /**
     * Sends the response for an error thrown by a handler. Unexpected errors are logged and reported as a 500
     * without details.
     */
    protected handleError(res: Response, error: unknown): void {
        if (error instanceof APIError) {
            res.status(error.statusCode)
                .set(error.headers)
                .json(error.toResponse());

            return;
        }

        console.error("[Unhandled Error]:", error);

        res.status(500).json({
            error: "Internal server error",
        } satisfies ErrorResponse);
    }
}
```

`src/server/http/errorHandlers.ts`:

```ts
import { APIError, type ErrorResponse } from "../errors";
import type { ErrorRequestHandler } from "express";

// There is deliberately no JSON not-found handler: a `404` with a `{ error: string }` body means "beatmap not
// found" to the dpp processors, so unknown routes keep Express's default HTML 404.

/**
 * Responds to errors that reach Express: API errors with their status, anything else with a 500.
 */
export const errorHandler: ErrorRequestHandler = (error, _req, res, next) => {
    if (res.headersSent) {
        next(error);

        return;
    }

    if (error instanceof APIError) {
        res.status(error.statusCode).set(error.headers).json(error.toResponse());

        return;
    }

    console.error("[Unhandled Error]:", error);

    res.status(500).json({
        error: "Internal server error",
    } satisfies ErrorResponse);
};
```

```ts
export * from "./http/errorHandlers";
export * from "./http/router";
export * from "./runtime/GracefulServer";
export * from "./runtime/logging";
export * from "./runtime/shutdown";
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const required = {
    POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: "localhost",
    POSTGRES_DB_NAME: "beatmaps",
    POSTGRES_USERNAME: "service",
    OSU_API_KEY: "osu-key",
};

describe("loadConfig", () => {
    it("applies the defaults", () => {
        const config = loadConfig(required);

        expect(config).toEqual({
            port: 3000,
            database: {
                host: "localhost",
                port: 5432,
                name: "beatmaps",
                user: "service",
                password: "",
            },
            osu: {
                apiKey: "osu-key",
                apiBaseUrl: "https://osu.ppy.sh/api",
                fileBaseUrl: "https://osu.ppy.sh/osu",
            },
            builtInKeys: { priority: undefined, bulk: undefined },
            ratesPerMinute: { api: 55, file: 55, total: 55 },
            bulkShare: 0.5,
            maxWaitMs: { priority: 8000, bulk: 5000 },
            beatmapFileDirectory: resolve("beatmaps"),
            recheckMinutes: { qualified: 15, pending: 60, graveyard: 1440 },
            cacheSize: { beatmaps: 10_000, beatmapsets: 2000 },
            anonymousRateLimit: 30,
            keyReloadIntervalSeconds: 30,
            trustProxy: false,
        });
    });

    it("reads every variable", () => {
        const config = loadConfig({
            ...required,
            EXPRESS_PORT: "4000",
            POSTGRES_PORT: "5433",
            POSTGRES_PASSWORD: "secret",
            INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: "internal",
            BULK_SERVER_KEY: "bulk",
            OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT: "40",
            OSU_FILE_RATE_LIMIT: "30",
            OSU_TOTAL_RATE_LIMIT: "50",
            BULK_SHARE: "0.25",
            PRIORITY_MAX_WAIT_MS: "100",
            BULK_MAX_WAIT_MS: "0",
            OSU_API_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:9/api/",
            OSU_FILE_BASE_URL: "http://127.0.0.1:9/osu",
            BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY: "/srv/beatmaps",
            RECHECK_QUALIFIED_MINUTES: "1",
            RECHECK_PENDING_MINUTES: "2",
            RECHECK_GRAVEYARD_MINUTES: "3",
            BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE: "5",
            BEATMAPSET_CACHE_SIZE: "6",
            ANON_RATE_LIMIT: "0",
            KEY_RELOAD_INTERVAL_SECONDS: "7",
            TRUST_PROXY: "loopback",
        });

        expect(config).toMatchObject({
            port: 4000,
            database: { port: 5433, password: "secret" },
            osu: { apiBaseUrl: "http://127.0.0.1:9/api" },
            builtInKeys: { priority: "internal", bulk: "bulk" },
            ratesPerMinute: { api: 40, file: 30, total: 50 },
            bulkShare: 0.25,
            maxWaitMs: { priority: 100, bulk: 0 },
            beatmapFileDirectory: "/srv/beatmaps",
            recheckMinutes: { qualified: 1, pending: 2, graveyard: 3 },
            cacheSize: { beatmaps: 5, beatmapsets: 6 },
            anonymousRateLimit: 0,
            keyReloadIntervalSeconds: 7,
            trustProxy: "loopback",
        });
    });

    it.each([
        ["true", true],
        ["false", false],
        ["2", 2],
        ["10.0.0.0/8", "10.0.0.0/8"],
    ])("parses TRUST_PROXY=%s", (value, expected) => {
        expect(loadConfig({ ...required, TRUST_PROXY: value }).trustProxy).toBe(
            expected,
        );
    });

    it("treats empty variables as unset", () => {
        expect(
            loadConfig({ ...required, EXPRESS_PORT: "", INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: "" }),
        ).toMatchObject({ port: 3000, builtInKeys: { priority: undefined } });
    });

    it("names every missing or invalid variable, never a value", () => {
        let error: unknown;

        try {
            loadConfig({
                OSU_API_KEY: "osu-key",
                BULK_SHARE: "2",
                OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT: "0",
                BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE: "0",
            });
        } catch (e) {
            error = e;
        }

        expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ConfigError);

        const message = (error as ConfigError).message;

        for (const name of [
            "POSTGRES_HOSTNAME",
            "POSTGRES_DB_NAME",
            "POSTGRES_USERNAME",
            "BULK_SHARE",
            "OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT",
            "BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE",
        ]) {
            expect(message).toContain(name);
        }

        expect(message).not.toContain("osu-key");
    });

    it("rejects the same key for both lanes", () => {
        expect(() =>
            loadConfig({
                ...required,
                INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: "same",
                BULK_SERVER_KEY: "same",
            }),
        ).toThrow(/BULK_SERVER_KEY must differ from INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY/);
    });
});

describe("loadDatabaseConfig", () => {
    it("reads only the database variables", () => {
        expect(
            loadDatabaseConfig({
                POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: "db",
                POSTGRES_DB_NAME: "beatmaps",
                POSTGRES_USERNAME: "service",
            }),
        ).toEqual({
            host: "db",
            port: 5432,
            name: "beatmaps",
            user: "service",
            password: "",
        });
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/config/config.ts`:

```ts
import type { DatabaseSettings } from "@/server";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { z } from "zod";

/**
 * A value for Express's `trust proxy` setting: `true`, `false`, a hop count, or addresses and subnets.
 */
export type TrustProxySetting = boolean | number | string;

/**
 * Settings read from environment variables.
 */
export interface Config {
    readonly port: number;
    readonly database: DatabaseSettings;
    readonly osu: {
        readonly apiKey: string;
        readonly apiBaseUrl: string;
        readonly fileBaseUrl: string;
    };

    /**
     * Keys from the environment that work like unlimited database keys: `priority` (`INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY`) for the
     * priority lane, `bulk` (`BULK_SERVER_KEY`) for the bulk lane.
     */
    readonly builtInKeys: {
        readonly priority: string | undefined;
        readonly bulk: string | undefined;
    };
    readonly ratesPerMinute: {
        readonly api: number;
        readonly file: number;
        readonly total: number;
    };

    /**
     * The fraction of each osu! budget the bulk lane may use, in (0, 1].
     */
    readonly bulkShare: number;
    readonly maxWaitMs: { readonly priority: number; readonly bulk: number };

    /**
     * The absolute path of the directory where `.osu` files are stored.
     */
    readonly beatmapFileDirectory: string;

    /**
     * How long a cached beatmap of each status is trusted before its status is re-checked with osu!. Ranked,
     * approved, and loved beatmaps are never re-checked.
     */
    readonly recheckMinutes: {
        readonly qualified: number;
        /** Pending and WIP beatmaps. */
        readonly pending: number;
        readonly graveyard: number;
    };

    /**
     * Entry limits of the memory caches: `beatmaps` for each of the ID and hash caches, `beatmapsets` for sets.
     */
    readonly cacheSize: {
        readonly beatmaps: number;
        readonly beatmapsets: number;
    };

    /**
     * Requests per minute per IP without a key; 0 requires a key.
     */
    readonly anonymousRateLimit: number;
    readonly keyReloadIntervalSeconds: number;
    readonly trustProxy: TrustProxySetting;
}

/**
 * Thrown for missing or invalid environment variables. Names the variables, never their values.
 */
export class ConfigError extends Error {
    constructor(message: string) {
        super(message);
        this.name = "ConfigError";
    }
}

const port = z.coerce.number().int().min(0).max(65535);
const perMinute = z.coerce.number().int().min(1);
const positive = z.coerce.number().int().min(1);
const milliseconds = z.coerce.number().int().min(0);
const baseUrl = z.url().transform((url) => url.replace(/\/+$/, ""));

const databaseShape = {
    POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: z.string(),
    POSTGRES_PORT: port.default(5432),
    POSTGRES_DB_NAME: z.string(),
    POSTGRES_USERNAME: z.string(),
    POSTGRES_PASSWORD: z.string().default(""),
};

const databaseSchema = z.object(databaseShape);

const schema = z
    .object({
        ...databaseShape,
        EXPRESS_PORT: port.default(3000),
        OSU_API_KEY: z.string(),
        INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: z.string().optional(),
        BULK_SERVER_KEY: z.string().optional(),
        OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT: perMinute.default(55),
        OSU_FILE_RATE_LIMIT: perMinute.default(55),
        OSU_TOTAL_RATE_LIMIT: perMinute.default(55),
        BULK_SHARE: z.coerce.number().gt(0).lte(1).default(0.5),
        PRIORITY_MAX_WAIT_MS: milliseconds.default(8000),
        BULK_MAX_WAIT_MS: milliseconds.default(5000),
        OSU_API_BASE_URL: baseUrl.default("https://osu.ppy.sh/api"),
        OSU_FILE_BASE_URL: baseUrl.default("https://osu.ppy.sh/osu"),
        BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY: z.string().default("beatmaps"),
        RECHECK_QUALIFIED_MINUTES: positive.default(15),
        RECHECK_PENDING_MINUTES: positive.default(60),
        RECHECK_GRAVEYARD_MINUTES: positive.default(1440),
        BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE: positive.default(10_000),
        BEATMAPSET_CACHE_SIZE: positive.default(2000),
        ANON_RATE_LIMIT: z.coerce.number().int().min(0).default(30),
        KEY_RELOAD_INTERVAL_SECONDS: positive.default(30),
        TRUST_PROXY: z.string().default("false").transform(parseTrustProxy),
    })
    .refine(
        (values) =>
            values.BULK_SERVER_KEY === undefined ||
            values.BULK_SERVER_KEY !== values.INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY,
        {
            message: "BULK_SERVER_KEY must differ from INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY",
            path: ["BULK_SERVER_KEY"],
        },
    );

function parseTrustProxy(value: string): TrustProxySetting {
    if (value === "true") {
        return true;
    }

    if (value === "false") {
        return false;
    }

    return /^\d+$/.test(value) ? Number(value) : value;
}

function parse<T extends z.ZodType>(
    target: T,
    env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv,
): z.infer<T> {
    // An empty variable (`EXPRESS_PORT=`) counts as unset, instead of being coerced to 0.
    const present = Object.fromEntries(
        Object.entries(env).filter(([, value]) => value !== ""),
    );
    const result = target.safeParse(present);

    if (!result.success) {
        // Issue messages name the variable (path) and the rule, never the value.
        const problems = result.error.issues.map((issue) =>
            issue.path.length > 0
                ? `${issue.path.join(".")}: ${issue.message}`
                : issue.message,
        );

        throw new ConfigError(
            `Invalid configuration:\n  ${problems.join("\n  ")}`,
        );
    }

    return result.data;
}

function buildDatabaseSettings(
    values: z.infer<typeof databaseSchema>,
): DatabaseSettings {
    return {
        host: values.POSTGRES_HOSTNAME,
        port: values.POSTGRES_PORT,
        name: values.POSTGRES_DB_NAME,
        user: values.POSTGRES_USERNAME,
        password: values.POSTGRES_PASSWORD,
    };
}

/**
 * Reads and validates the configuration. Empty variables count as unset.
 *
 * @throws {ConfigError} If any variable is missing or invalid.
 */
export function loadConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): Config {
    const values = parse(schema, env);

    return {
        port: values.EXPRESS_PORT,
        database: buildDatabaseSettings(values),
        osu: {
            apiKey: values.OSU_API_KEY,
            apiBaseUrl: values.OSU_API_BASE_URL,
            fileBaseUrl: values.OSU_FILE_BASE_URL,
        },
        builtInKeys: {
            priority: values.INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY,
            bulk: values.BULK_SERVER_KEY,
        },
        ratesPerMinute: {
            api: values.OSU_API_RATE_LIMIT,
            file: values.OSU_FILE_RATE_LIMIT,
            total: values.OSU_TOTAL_RATE_LIMIT,
        },
        bulkShare: values.BULK_SHARE,
        maxWaitMs: {
            priority: values.PRIORITY_MAX_WAIT_MS,
            bulk: values.BULK_MAX_WAIT_MS,
        },
        beatmapFileDirectory: resolve(values.BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY),
        recheckMinutes: {
            qualified: values.RECHECK_QUALIFIED_MINUTES,
            pending: values.RECHECK_PENDING_MINUTES,
            graveyard: values.RECHECK_GRAVEYARD_MINUTES,
        },
        cacheSize: {
            beatmaps: values.BEATMAP_CACHE_SIZE,
            beatmapsets: values.BEATMAPSET_CACHE_SIZE,
        },
        anonymousRateLimit: values.ANON_RATE_LIMIT,
        keyReloadIntervalSeconds: values.KEY_RELOAD_INTERVAL_SECONDS,
        trustProxy: values.TRUST_PROXY,
    };
}

/**
 * Reads only the database variables, for CLI commands that need nothing else.
 *
 * @throws {ConfigError} If any database variable is missing or invalid.
 */
export function loadDatabaseConfig(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv): DatabaseSettings {
    return buildDatabaseSettings(parse(databaseSchema, env));
}
```

### Task 5: Database schema, migrations, baseline, and rollback

**Files:**
- Create: `src/database/schema.ts`, `src/database/types.ts`, `src/database/errors.ts`, `src/database/baseline.ts`, `drizzle/0000_baseline.sql`, `drizzle/0001_rewrite.sql`, `drizzle/meta/*` (generated), `docs/rollback/0001_rewrite.down.sql`, `tests/utils/database.ts`, `tests/integration/database/migrations.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `beatmapTable`, `beatmapsetTable`, `cliCursorTable`, `apiKeysTable`, `schema` (all four), `Database` (Drizzle type that both node-postgres and PGlite satisfy), `BeatmapRow = typeof beatmapTable.$inferSelect`, `extractRows<T>(result: unknown): T[]`, `DatabaseUnavailableError(cause)` (a `ServiceUnavailableError`: 503, `"Database unavailable"`, `Retry-After: 5`), `baselineDatabase(db, migrationsFolder): Promise<void>`, `assertMigrationHistory(db): Promise<void>`, `BaselineError`, `migrationsFolder` and `createTestDatabase()` in `tests/utils/database.ts`.

`BeatmapRow` fields (camelCase; column names in parentheses only where they differ by more than case): `approved`, `submitDate`, `approvedDate`, `lastUpdate`, `artist`, `beatmapId`, `beatmapsetId`, `bpm`, `creator`, `creatorId`, `difficultyRating` (`difficultyrating`), `diffAim`, `diffSpeed`, `diffSize`, `diffOverall`, `diffApproach`, `diffDrain`, `hitLength`, `source`, `genreId`, `languageId`, `title`, `totalLength`, `version`, `fileMd5` (`file_md5`), `tags`, `favouriteCount`, `rating`, `playcount`, `passcount`, `countNormal`, `countSlider`, `countSpinner`, `maxCombo`, `storyboard`, `video`, `downloadUnavailable`, `audioUnavailable`, `packs`, `lastChecked`.

**How Drizzle's migrator tracks migrations** (dpp's broker, processor, and score service work the same way; see `apps/broker/drizzle/meta/_journal.json` there):
- `drizzle-kit generate` writes each migration as `drizzle/<tag>.sql` and lists it in `drizzle/meta/_journal.json` (`idx`, `when` in milliseconds, `tag`, `breakpoints`), next to a schema snapshot per migration. The migrator (`readMigrationFiles` in `drizzle-orm/migrator.js`) reads only the journal, then each listed `.sql` file, and hashes it with SHA-256. The snapshots are for `drizzle-kit generate` alone.
- `migrate()` records applied migrations in `drizzle.__drizzle_migrations` (`id`, `hash`, `created_at` = the journal's `when`), creating the `drizzle` schema and table if missing (`drizzle-orm/pg-core/dialect.js`). It applies every journal entry whose `when` is newer than the newest recorded `created_at`.
- So `db-baseline` inserts the `0000_baseline` row by hand on the production database, whose tables already exist; `migrate()` then skips `0000` and applies only `0001_rewrite`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the baseline schema (production as it is)**

Every column is named explicitly, so Drizzle's snake_case conversion never guesses (it would turn `fileMd5` into `file_md_5`).

`src/database/schema.ts` (temporary content for this step):

```ts
import type {
    BeatmapGenre,
    BeatmapLanguage,
    RankedStatus,
} from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import {
    boolean,
    doublePrecision,
    index,
    integer,
    pgTable,
    real,
    smallint,
    text,
    timestamp,
    varchar,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

export const beatmapTable = pgTable(
    "beatmap",
    {
        approved: smallint("approved").$type<RankedStatus>().notNull(),
        submitDate: timestamp("submit_date").notNull(),
        approvedDate: timestamp("approved_date"),
        lastUpdate: timestamp("last_update").notNull(),
        artist: text("artist").notNull(),
        beatmapId: integer("beatmap_id").primaryKey(),
        beatmapsetId: integer("beatmapset_id").notNull(),
        bpm: real("bpm").notNull(),
        creator: text("creator").notNull(),
        creatorId: integer("creator_id").notNull(),
        difficultyRating: doublePrecision("difficultyrating"),
        diffAim: doublePrecision("diff_aim"),
        diffSpeed: doublePrecision("diff_speed"),
        diffSize: real("diff_size").notNull(),
        diffOverall: real("diff_overall").notNull(),
        diffApproach: real("diff_approach").notNull(),
        diffDrain: real("diff_drain").notNull(),
        hitLength: integer("hit_length").notNull(),
        source: text("source").notNull(),
        genreId: integer("genre_id").$type<BeatmapGenre>().notNull(),
        languageId: integer("language_id").$type<BeatmapLanguage>().notNull(),
        title: text("title").notNull(),
        totalLength: integer("total_length").notNull(),
        version: text("version").notNull(),
        fileMd5: varchar("file_md5", { length: 32 }).notNull(),
        tags: text("tags").notNull(),
        favouriteCount: integer("favourite_count").notNull(),
        rating: real("rating").notNull(),
        playcount: integer("playcount").notNull(),
        passcount: integer("passcount").notNull(),
        countNormal: integer("count_normal").notNull(),
        countSlider: integer("count_slider").notNull(),
        countSpinner: integer("count_spinner").notNull(),
        maxCombo: integer("max_combo"),
        storyboard: boolean("storyboard").notNull(),
        video: boolean("video").notNull(),
        downloadUnavailable: boolean("download_unavailable").notNull(),
        audioUnavailable: boolean("audio_unavailable").notNull(),
        packs: text("packs"),
        lastChecked: timestamp("last_checked").notNull(),
    },
    (table) => [
        index("beatmap_id_idx").on(table.beatmapId),
        index("beatmapset_id_idx").on(table.beatmapsetId),
        index("beatmap_md5_idx").on(table.fileMd5),
    ],
);
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the final schema**

Replace `src/database/schema.ts`:

```ts
import type {
    BeatmapGenre,
    BeatmapLanguage,
    RankedStatus,
} from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
    boolean,
    char,
    check,
    doublePrecision,
    index,
    integer,
    pgTable,
    real,
    serial,
    smallint,
    text,
    timestamp,
    varchar,
} from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";

/**
 * A `timestamptz` column read as a `Date`.
 */
const timestamptz = (name: string) => timestamp(name, { withTimezone: true });

/**
 * Cached beatmaps, one row per osu!standard beatmap. Column names are those of the pre-rewrite table.
 */
export const beatmapTable = pgTable(
    "beatmap",
    {
        approved: smallint("approved").$type<RankedStatus>().notNull(),
        submitDate: timestamptz("submit_date").notNull(),
        approvedDate: timestamptz("approved_date"),
        lastUpdate: timestamptz("last_update").notNull(),
        artist: text("artist").notNull(),
        beatmapId: integer("beatmap_id").primaryKey(),
        beatmapsetId: integer("beatmapset_id").notNull(),
        bpm: real("bpm").notNull(),
        creator: text("creator").notNull(),
        creatorId: integer("creator_id").notNull(),
        difficultyRating: doublePrecision("difficultyrating"),
        diffAim: doublePrecision("diff_aim"),
        diffSpeed: doublePrecision("diff_speed"),
        diffSize: real("diff_size").notNull(),
        diffOverall: real("diff_overall").notNull(),
        diffApproach: real("diff_approach").notNull(),
        diffDrain: real("diff_drain").notNull(),
        hitLength: integer("hit_length").notNull(),
        source: text("source").notNull(),
        genreId: integer("genre_id").$type<BeatmapGenre>().notNull(),
        languageId: integer("language_id").$type<BeatmapLanguage>().notNull(),
        title: text("title").notNull(),
        totalLength: integer("total_length").notNull(),
        version: text("version").notNull(),
        fileMd5: varchar("file_md5", { length: 32 }).notNull(),
        tags: text("tags").notNull(),
        favouriteCount: integer("favourite_count").notNull(),
        rating: real("rating").notNull(),
        playcount: integer("playcount").notNull(),
        passcount: integer("passcount").notNull(),
        countNormal: integer("count_normal").notNull(),
        countSlider: integer("count_slider").notNull(),
        countSpinner: integer("count_spinner").notNull(),
        maxCombo: integer("max_combo"),
        storyboard: boolean("storyboard").notNull(),
        video: boolean("video").notNull(),
        downloadUnavailable: boolean("download_unavailable").notNull(),
        audioUnavailable: boolean("audio_unavailable").notNull(),
        packs: text("packs"),
        lastChecked: timestamptz("last_checked").notNull(),
    },
    (table) => [
        index("beatmapset_id_idx").on(table.beatmapsetId),
        index("beatmap_md5_idx").on(table.fileMd5),
    ],
);

/**
 * Beatmapsets whose every osu!standard beatmap was stored at `lastChecked`. Rows alone cannot tell whether a set is
 * complete, because a single-beatmap lookup stores one difficulty.
 */
export const beatmapsetTable = pgTable("beatmapset", {
    beatmapsetId: integer("beatmapset_id").primaryKey(),
    lastChecked: timestamptz("last_checked").notNull(),
});

/**
 * Where each CLI walk resumes.
 */
export const cliCursorTable = pgTable("cli_cursor", {
    command: text("command").primaryKey(),
    cursor: timestamptz("cursor").notNull(),
});

/**
 * API keys. Only the SHA-256 hash of a key is stored.
 */
export const apiKeysTable = pgTable(
    "api_keys",
    {
        id: serial("id").primaryKey(),
        name: text("name").notNull().unique("api_keys_name_unique"),
        keyHash: char("key_hash", { length: 64 })
            .notNull()
            .unique("api_keys_key_hash_unique"),
        lane: text("lane").$type<"priority" | "bulk">().notNull(),
        rateLimitPerMinute: integer("rate_limit_per_minute"),
        createdAt: timestamptz("created_at").notNull().defaultNow(),
        revokedAt: timestamptz("revoked_at"),
    },
    (table) => [
        check("api_keys_lane_check", sql`${table.lane} in ('priority', 'bulk')`),
    ],
);

export const schema = {
    beatmapTable,
    beatmapsetTable,
    cliCursorTable,
    apiKeysTable,
};
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Generate and edit the rewrite migration**

```bash
pnpm db:generate --name rewrite
```

Edit `drizzle/0001_rewrite.sql`:

1. Postgres converts `timestamp` to `timestamptz` in the session's time zone unless told otherwise. Append ` USING "<column>" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC'` to each of the four generated statements, so they read:

```sql
ALTER TABLE "beatmap" ALTER COLUMN "submit_date" SET DATA TYPE timestamp with time zone USING "submit_date" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC';--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "beatmap" ALTER COLUMN "approved_date" SET DATA TYPE timestamp with time zone USING "approved_date" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC';--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "beatmap" ALTER COLUMN "last_update" SET DATA TYPE timestamp with time zone USING "last_update" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC';--> statement-breakpoint
ALTER TABLE "beatmap" ALTER COLUMN "last_checked" SET DATA TYPE timestamp with time zone USING "last_checked" AT TIME ZONE 'UTC';--> statement-breakpoint
```

2. Append at the end (local databases that ran the old `populateCache.ts` have this table; production does not):

```sql
--> statement-breakpoint
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS "populate";
```

The file must also contain `DROP INDEX "beatmap_id_idx";` and `CREATE TABLE` statements for `api_keys` (with the check constraint and both unique constraints), `beatmapset`, and `cli_cursor`. Do not edit `drizzle/meta/*`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the rollback script**

`docs/rollback/0001_rewrite.down.sql`:

```sql
-- Reverts drizzle/0001_rewrite.sql so that the pre-rewrite build can run against the database again. Only valid
-- while 0001_rewrite is the latest applied migration. Database API keys are lost: run `key list` first and recreate
-- them after migrating forward again. Run with: psql -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -f docs/rollback/0001_rewrite.down.sql
BEGIN;

ALTER TABLE beatmap
    ALTER COLUMN submit_date TYPE timestamp USING submit_date AT TIME ZONE 'UTC',
    ALTER COLUMN approved_date TYPE timestamp USING approved_date AT TIME ZONE 'UTC',
    ALTER COLUMN last_update TYPE timestamp USING last_update AT TIME ZONE 'UTC',
    ALTER COLUMN last_checked TYPE timestamp USING last_checked AT TIME ZONE 'UTC';

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS beatmap_id_idx ON beatmap USING btree (beatmap_id);

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS beatmapset, cli_cursor, api_keys;

-- Forget 0001 so that the next start of the new build applies it again.
DELETE FROM drizzle.__drizzle_migrations
WHERE id = (SELECT max(id) FROM drizzle.__drizzle_migrations);

COMMIT;
```

`src/database/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { PgDatabase, PgQueryResultHKT } from "drizzle-orm/pg-core";
import type { beatmapTable, schema } from "./schema";

/**
 * A Drizzle database with this service's schema. Both node-postgres (production) and PGlite (tests) databases are
 * assignable to it.
 */
export type Database = PgDatabase<PgQueryResultHKT, typeof schema>;

/**
 * A cached beatmap.
 */
export type BeatmapRow = typeof beatmapTable.$inferSelect;

/**
 * The rows of a raw `db.execute` result. node-postgres and PGlite both return `{ rows }`.
 */
export function extractRows<T>(result: unknown): T[] {
    return (result as { rows: T[] }).rows;
}
```

`src/database/errors.ts`:

```ts
import { ServiceUnavailableError } from "@/server";

/**
 * A database read failed. Answered as `503` without asking osu!: during an outage every request would otherwise
 * become an osu! call and exhaust the budget within seconds.
 */
export class DatabaseUnavailableError extends ServiceUnavailableError {
    constructor(cause: unknown) {
        super("Database unavailable", 5);
        this.cause = cause;
    }
}
```

`src/database/baseline.ts`:

```ts
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { readMigrationFiles } from "drizzle-orm/migrator";
import { extractRows, type Database } from "./types";

/**
 * The database is not in the state a baseline needs, or needs a baseline first. The message says what to do.
 */
export class BaselineError extends Error {
    constructor(message: string) {
        super(message);
        this.name = "BaselineError";
    }
}

async function hasBeatmapTable(db: Database): Promise<boolean> {
    const [row] = extractRows<{ present: boolean }>(
        await db.execute(
            sql`select to_regclass('public.beatmap') is not null as present`,
        ),
    );

    return row?.present ?? false;
}

async function countRecordedMigrations(db: Database): Promise<number> {
    const [table] = extractRows<{ present: boolean }>(
        await db.execute(
            sql`select to_regclass('drizzle.__drizzle_migrations') is not null as present`,
        ),
    );

    if (!table?.present) {
        return 0;
    }

    const [row] = extractRows<{ count: number }>(
        await db.execute(
            sql`select count(*)::int as count from drizzle.__drizzle_migrations`,
        ),
    );

    return row?.count ?? 0;
}

/**
 * Records the first migration (`0000_baseline`, the pre-rewrite schema) as applied without running it, for a database
 * that has the pre-rewrite `beatmap` table but no migration history.
 *
 * @throws {BaselineError} If there is no `beatmap` table, or migrations are already recorded.
 */
export async function baselineDatabase(
    db: Database,
    migrationsFolder: string,
): Promise<void> {
    const [baseline] = readMigrationFiles({ migrationsFolder });

    if (!baseline) {
        throw new Error(`No migrations in ${migrationsFolder}`);
    }

    if ((await countRecordedMigrations(db)) > 0) {
        throw new BaselineError(
            "Migrations are already recorded; this database needs no baseline",
        );
    }

    if (!(await hasBeatmapTable(db))) {
        throw new BaselineError(
            "There is no beatmap table to baseline; a new database needs no baseline (migrations create it)",
        );
    }

    // The same table Drizzle's migrator creates and reads.
    await db.execute(sql`create schema if not exists drizzle`);
    await db.execute(
        sql`create table if not exists drizzle.__drizzle_migrations (id serial primary key, hash text not null, created_at bigint)`,
    );
    await db.execute(
        sql`insert into drizzle.__drizzle_migrations (hash, created_at) values (${baseline.hash}, ${baseline.folderMillis})`,
    );
}

/**
 * Stops a start against a pre-rewrite database that was never baselined: migrating it would try to create the
 * `beatmap` table that already exists.
 *
 * @throws {BaselineError} If the `beatmap` table exists but no migration is recorded.
 */
export async function assertMigrationHistory(db: Database): Promise<void> {
    if ((await countRecordedMigrations(db)) === 0 && (await hasBeatmapTable(db))) {
        throw new BaselineError(
            "The database has a beatmap table but no migration history; run `pnpm db:baseline` once first",
        );
    }
}
```

```ts
import { schema } from "@/database/schema";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import { createTestDatabase as createDatabase } from "@/server/testing";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

export const migrationsFolder = fileURLToPath(
    new URL("../../drizzle", import.meta.url),
);

/**
 * An in-memory Postgres database for a test. Disposing closes it.
 */
export interface TestDatabase extends AsyncDisposable {
    readonly db: Database;
}

/**
 * Creates an in-memory Postgres (PGlite) database with every migration applied.
 */
export function createTestDatabase(): Promise<TestDatabase> {
    return createDatabase({ schema, migrationsFolder });
}
```

`tests/integration/database/migrations.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
    assertMigrationHistory,
    BaselineError,
    baselineDatabase,
} from "@/database/baseline";
import { schema } from "@/database/schema";
import { extractRows, type Database } from "@/database/types";
import { PGlite } from "@electric-sql/pglite";
import { migrationsFolder } from "@test/utils/database";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { drizzle } from "drizzle-orm/pglite";
import { migrate } from "drizzle-orm/pglite/migrator";
import { cp, mkdtemp, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const fixture = (path: string) =>
    readFile(fileURLToPath(new URL(`../../${path}`, import.meta.url)), "utf8");

const insertOldRow = `
INSERT INTO beatmap VALUES (
    1, '2007-10-06 01:46:31', '2007-10-06 01:46:31', '2007-10-06 01:46:31', 'Kenji Ninuma', 75, 1, 119.5, 'peppy', 2,
    2.5, 1.25, 1.125, 4, 6, 6, 6, 108, '', 2, 3, 'DISCO PRINCE', 142, 'Normal', '0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef',
    'katamari', 100, 8.5, 1000, 500, 160, 30, 4, 314, false, true, false, false, 'S1 T44', '2025-01-01 00:00:00'
);`;

interface Opened {
    readonly client: PGlite;
    readonly db: Database;
}

const opened: PGlite[] = [];

function open(): Opened {
    const client = new PGlite();

    opened.push(client);

    return { client, db: drizzle(client, { schema, casing: "snake_case" }) };
}

/**
 * Describes columns and indexes, so two databases can be compared.
 */
async function describeSchema(db: Database) {
    const columns = extractRows(
        await db.execute(sql`
            select table_name, column_name, data_type, is_nullable, character_maximum_length
            from information_schema.columns where table_schema = 'public'
            order by table_name, column_name`),
    );
    const indexes = extractRows(
        await db.execute(sql`
            select indexname, indexdef from pg_indexes where schemaname = 'public' order by indexname`),
    );

    return { columns, indexes };
}

/**
 * A copy of the migrations folder that contains only the baseline.
 */
async function createBaselineOnlyFolder(): Promise<string> {
    const folder = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "baseline-"));

    await cp(migrationsFolder, folder, { recursive: true });

    const journalPath = join(folder, "meta/_journal.json");
    const journal = JSON.parse(await readFile(journalPath, "utf8")) as {
        entries: unknown[];
    };

    journal.entries = journal.entries.slice(0, 1);
    await writeFile(journalPath, JSON.stringify(journal));

    return folder;
}

afterEach(async () => {
    await Promise.all(opened.splice(0).map((client) => client.close()));
});

describe("migrations", () => {
    it("starts from exactly the production schema", async () => {
        const production = open();

        await production.client.exec(
            await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"),
        );

        const migrated = open();
        const folder = await createBaselineOnlyFolder();

        try {
            await migrate(migrated.db, { migrationsFolder: folder });
        } finally {
            await rm(folder, { recursive: true, force: true });
        }

        expect(await describeSchema(migrated.db)).toEqual(
            await describeSchema(production.db),
        );
    });

    it("migrates a baselined production database without moving stored instants", async () => {
        const { client, db } = open();

        await client.exec(await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"));
        await client.exec(insertOldRow);
        await baselineDatabase(db, migrationsFolder);
        await migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });

        const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.beatmapTable);

        expect(row?.submitDate).toEqual(new Date("2007-10-06T01:46:31Z"));
        expect(row?.lastChecked).toEqual(new Date("2025-01-01T00:00:00Z"));
        expect(row?.video).toBe(true);

        const { indexes } = await describeSchema(db);

        expect(indexes.map((index) => (index as { indexname: string }).indexname)).not.toContain(
            "beatmap_id_idx",
        );
        await expect(db.select().from(schema.apiKeysTable)).resolves.toEqual([]);
        await expect(db.select().from(schema.beatmapsetTable)).resolves.toEqual([]);
        await expect(db.select().from(schema.cliCursorTable)).resolves.toEqual([]);
    });

    it("migrates an empty database from scratch", async () => {
        const { db } = open();

        await assertMigrationHistory(db);
        await migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });
        await assertMigrationHistory(db);
        await expect(db.select().from(schema.beatmapTable)).resolves.toEqual([]);
    });

    it("refuses to start on a pre-rewrite database without a baseline", async () => {
        const { client, db } = open();

        await client.exec(await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"));

        await expect(assertMigrationHistory(db)).rejects.toThrow(
            /run `pnpm db:baseline` once first/,
        );
        await baselineDatabase(db, migrationsFolder);
        await expect(assertMigrationHistory(db)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("baselines only a pre-rewrite database without history", async () => {
        const empty = open();

        await expect(
            baselineDatabase(empty.db, migrationsFolder),
        ).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(BaselineError);

        const production = open();

        await production.client.exec(
            await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"),
        );
        await baselineDatabase(production.db, migrationsFolder);
        await expect(
            baselineDatabase(production.db, migrationsFolder),
        ).rejects.toThrow(/already recorded/);
    });

    it("rolls back to a schema the old build reads, and migrates forward again", async () => {
        const { client, db } = open();

        await client.exec(await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"));
        await client.exec(insertOldRow);
        await baselineDatabase(db, migrationsFolder);
        await migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });
        await client.exec(await fixture("../docs/rollback/0001_rewrite.down.sql"));

        const production = open();

        await production.client.exec(
            await fixture("fixtures/production-schema.sql"),
        );
        expect(await describeSchema(db)).toEqual(
            await describeSchema(production.db),
        );

        const [stored] = extractRows<{ submit_date: string }>(
            await db.execute(sql`select submit_date::text from beatmap`),
        );

        expect(stored?.submit_date).toBe("2007-10-06 01:46:31");

        await migrate(db, { migrationsFolder });

        const [row] = await db.select().from(schema.beatmapTable);

        expect(row?.submitDate).toEqual(new Date("2007-10-06T01:46:31Z"));
    });
});
```

```ts
import { convertApiResponseToRow } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { buildApiBeatmap } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";

/**
 * A beatmap row built from a fixture API response, checked at `checkedAt` (default: now).
 */
export function buildBeatmapRow(
    overrides: Partial<OsuAPIResponse> = {},
    checkedAt = new Date(),
): BeatmapRow {
    return convertApiResponseToRow(buildApiBeatmap(overrides), checkedAt);
}
```

`tests/unit/beatmaps/validation.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
    parseBeatmapReference,
    parseBeatmapsetId,
} from "@/beatmaps/validation";
import { BadRequestError } from "@/server";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

function captureFailureMessage(run: () => unknown): string {
    try {
        run();
    } catch (e) {
        expect(e).toBeInstanceOf(BadRequestError);

        return (e as BadRequestError).message;
    }

    throw new Error("expected a BadRequestError");
}

const hash = "0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";

describe("parseBeatmapReference", () => {
    it("reads an id", () => {
        expect(parseBeatmapReference({ id: "75" })).toEqual({ id: 75 });
        expect(parseBeatmapReference({ id: "2147483647" })).toEqual({
            id: 2147483647,
        });
    });

    it("prefers the id when both are given", () => {
        expect(parseBeatmapReference({ id: "75", hash: "nonsense" })).toEqual({
            id: 75,
        });
    });

    it("reads a hash in any case, lowercased", () => {
        expect(parseBeatmapReference({ hash: hash.toUpperCase() })).toEqual({
            hash,
        });
    });

    it("treats empty values as missing", () => {
        expect(parseBeatmapReference({ id: "", hash })).toEqual({ hash });
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapReference({ id: "", hash: "" }))).toBe(
            "Missing id or hash",
        );
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapReference({}))).toBe(
            "Missing id or hash",
        );
    });

    it.each([
        ["abc"],
        ["0"],
        ["-1"],
        ["+5"],
        [" 75"],
        ["7.5"],
        ["1e3"],
        ["2147483648"],
        [["1", "2"]],
        [{ a: "1" }],
    ])("rejects the id %j", (id) => {
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapReference({ id }))).toBe("Invalid id");
    });

    it.each([
        ["0123456789abcdef0123456789abcde"],
        ["0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef0"],
        ["0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdeg"],
        [[hash, hash]],
    ])("rejects the hash %j", (value) => {
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapReference({ hash: value }))).toBe(
            "Invalid hash",
        );
    });
});

describe("parseBeatmapsetId", () => {
    it("reads an id", () => {
        expect(parseBeatmapsetId({ id: "1" })).toBe(1);
    });

    it("needs an id", () => {
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapsetId({}))).toBe("Missing id");
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapsetId({ id: "" }))).toBe("Missing id");
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapsetId({ hash }))).toBe("Missing id");
    });

    it("rejects an invalid id", () => {
        expect(captureFailureMessage(() => parseBeatmapsetId({ id: "x" }))).toBe("Invalid id");
    });
});
```

`tests/unit/beatmaps/recheck.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
    isRecheckDue,
    getRecheckIntervalMs,
    getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs,
} from "@/beatmaps/recheck";
import { RankedStatus } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const minutes = { qualified: 15, pending: 60, graveyard: 1440 };

describe("getRecheckIntervalMs", () => {
    it.each([
        [RankedStatus.Ranked, null],
        [RankedStatus.Approved, null],
        [RankedStatus.Loved, null],
        [RankedStatus.Qualified, 15 * 60_000],
        [RankedStatus.Pending, 60 * 60_000],
        [RankedStatus.Wip, 60 * 60_000],
        [RankedStatus.Graveyard, 1440 * 60_000],
    ])("status %d → %j", (status, expected) => {
        expect(getRecheckIntervalMs(status, minutes)).toBe(expected);
    });

    it("treats an unknown status as pending", () => {
        expect(getRecheckIntervalMs(9 as RankedStatus, minutes)).toBe(60 * 60_000);
    });
});

describe("getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs", () => {
    it("uses the most volatile status", () => {
        expect(
            getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs(
                [RankedStatus.Ranked, RankedStatus.Qualified, RankedStatus.Graveyard],
                minutes,
            ),
        ).toBe(15 * 60_000);
    });

    it("never re-checks an all-ranked set", () => {
        expect(
            getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs([RankedStatus.Ranked, RankedStatus.Loved], minutes),
        ).toBeNull();
    });

    it("re-checks a set without standard beatmaps like a graveyard one", () => {
        expect(getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs([], minutes)).toBe(1440 * 60_000);
    });
});

describe("isRecheckDue", () => {
    const now = Date.parse("2026-10-11T12:00:00Z");

    it("is due once the interval has passed", () => {
        expect(isRecheckDue(new Date(now - 61_000), 60_000, now)).toBe(true);
        expect(isRecheckDue(new Date(now - 59_000), 60_000, now)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("is never due without an interval", () => {
        expect(isRecheckDue(new Date(0), null, now)).toBe(false);
    });
});
```
