`src/beatmaps/converter.ts`:

```ts
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { MapInfo, type OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";

const pad = (value: number) => value.toString().padStart(2, "0");

/**
 * Formats a date the way osu! API v1 does: `YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS` in UTC.
 */
export function formatOsuDate(date: Date): string {
    return `${date.getUTCFullYear().toString()}-${pad(date.getUTCMonth() + 1)}-${pad(date.getUTCDate())} ${pad(date.getUTCHours())}:${pad(date.getUTCMinutes())}:${pad(date.getUTCSeconds())}`;
}

/**
 * Parses an osu! API v1 date (`YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM:SS`, UTC).
 */
export function parseOsuDate(text: string): Date {
    const [year = 0, month = 1, day = 1, hours = 0, minutes = 0, seconds = 0] =
        text.split(/[- :]/).map(Number);

    return new Date(Date.UTC(year, month - 1, day, hours, minutes, seconds));
}

/**
 * Converts an osu! API v1 beatmap into a row. Goes through osu-base's `MapInfo`, as the pre-rewrite service did, so
 * stored values (and so responses) stay identical: dates as UTC, packs space-separated, missing packs as "".
 *
 * @param checkedAt The `lastChecked` value. Defaults to now.
 */
export function convertApiResponseToRow(
    response: OsuAPIResponse,
    checkedAt = new Date(),
): BeatmapRow {
    const info = MapInfo.from(response);

    return {
        approved: info.approved,
        submitDate: info.submitDate,
        approvedDate: info.approvedDate,
        lastUpdate: info.lastUpdate,
        artist: info.artist,
        beatmapId: info.beatmapId,
        beatmapsetId: info.beatmapSetId,
        bpm: info.bpm,
        creator: info.creator,
        creatorId: info.creatorId,
        difficultyRating: info.totalDifficulty,
        diffAim: info.aimDifficulty,
        diffSpeed: info.speedDifficulty,
        diffSize: info.cs,
        diffOverall: info.od,
        diffApproach: info.ar,
        diffDrain: info.hp,
        hitLength: info.hitLength,
        source: info.source,
        genreId: info.genre,
        languageId: info.language,
        title: info.title,
        totalLength: info.totalLength,
        version: info.version,
        fileMd5: info.hash,
        tags: info.tags.join(" "),
        favouriteCount: info.favorites,
        rating: info.rating,
        playcount: info.plays,
        passcount: info.passes,
        countNormal: info.circles,
        countSlider: info.sliders,
        countSpinner: info.spinners,
        maxCombo: info.maxCombo,
        storyboard: info.storyboardAvailable,
        video: info.videoAvailable,
        downloadUnavailable: !info.downloadAvailable,
        audioUnavailable: !info.audioAvailable,
        packs: info.packs.join(" "),
        lastChecked: checkedAt,
    };
}

/**
 * Converts a row into the osu! API v1 format this service answers with.
 */
export function convertRowToApiResponse(row: BeatmapRow): OsuAPIResponse {
    return {
        approved: row.approved.toString(),
        submit_date: formatOsuDate(row.submitDate),
        approved_date: row.approvedDate ? formatOsuDate(row.approvedDate) : null,
        last_update: formatOsuDate(row.lastUpdate),
        artist: row.artist,
        beatmap_id: row.beatmapId.toString(),
        beatmapset_id: row.beatmapsetId.toString(),
        bpm: row.bpm.toString(),
        creator: row.creator,
        creator_id: row.creatorId.toString(),
        difficultyrating: row.difficultyRating?.toString() ?? null,
        diff_aim: row.diffAim?.toString() ?? null,
        diff_speed: row.diffSpeed?.toString() ?? null,
        diff_size: row.diffSize.toString(),
        diff_overall: row.diffOverall.toString(),
        diff_approach: row.diffApproach.toString(),
        diff_drain: row.diffDrain.toString(),
        hit_length: row.hitLength.toString(),
        source: row.source,
        genre_id: row.genreId.toString(),
        language_id: row.languageId.toString(),
        title: row.title,
        total_length: row.totalLength.toString(),
        version: row.version,
        file_md5: row.fileMd5,
        // Only osu!standard beatmaps are stored.
        mode: "0",
        tags: row.tags,
        favourite_count: row.favouriteCount.toString(),
        rating: row.rating.toString(),
        playcount: row.playcount.toString(),
        passcount: row.passcount.toString(),
        count_normal: row.countNormal.toString(),
        count_slider: row.countSlider.toString(),
        count_spinner: row.countSpinner.toString(),
        max_combo: row.maxCombo?.toString() ?? null,
        storyboard: row.storyboard ? "1" : "0",
        video: row.video ? "1" : "0",
        download_unavailable: row.downloadUnavailable ? "1" : "0",
        audio_unavailable: row.audioUnavailable ? "1" : "0",
        packs: row.packs ?? null,
    };
}
```

`src/beatmaps/validation.ts`:

```ts
import { BadRequestError } from "@/server";

/**
 * How a request names a beatmap.
 */
export type BeatmapReference = { readonly id: number } | { readonly hash: string };

const maxId = 2_147_483_647;

/**
 * Whether a query value was given. Empty values count as missing, as they did before the rewrite.
 */
function isPresent(value: unknown): boolean {
    return value !== undefined && value !== "";
}

function parseId(value: unknown): number {
    if (typeof value !== "string" || !/^\d+$/.test(value)) {
        throw new BadRequestError("Invalid id");
    }

    const id = Number(value);

    if (id < 1 || id > maxId) {
        throw new BadRequestError("Invalid id");
    }

    return id;
}

/**
 * Reads `id` or `hash` from a query. `id` wins when both are given.
 *
 * @throws {BadRequestError} "Missing id or hash", "Invalid id", or "Invalid hash".
 */
export function parseBeatmapReference(
    query: Record<string, unknown>,
): BeatmapReference {
    if (isPresent(query.id)) {
        return { id: parseId(query.id) };
    }

    if (isPresent(query.hash)) {
        const { hash } = query;

        if (typeof hash !== "string" || !/^[0-9a-f]{32}$/i.test(hash)) {
            throw new BadRequestError("Invalid hash");
        }

        return { hash: hash.toLowerCase() };
    }

    throw new BadRequestError("Missing id or hash");
}

/**
 * Reads a beatmapset `id` from a query.
 *
 * @throws {BadRequestError} "Missing id" or "Invalid id".
 */
export function parseBeatmapsetId(query: Record<string, unknown>): number {
    if (!isPresent(query.id)) {
        throw new BadRequestError("Missing id");
    }

    return parseId(query.id);
}
```

`src/beatmaps/recheck.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import { RankedStatus } from "@rian8337/osu-base";

/**
 * How long a cached beatmap of this status is trusted before its status is re-checked with osu!, in milliseconds.
 * Ranked, approved, and loved beatmaps cannot change or be removed, so they are never re-checked (`null`). An
 * unknown status is treated as pending.
 */
export function getRecheckIntervalMs(
    status: RankedStatus,
    minutes: Config["recheckMinutes"],
): number | null {
    switch (status) {
        case RankedStatus.Ranked:
        case RankedStatus.Approved:
        case RankedStatus.Loved:
            return null;
        case RankedStatus.Qualified:
            return minutes.qualified * 60_000;
        case RankedStatus.Graveyard:
            return minutes.graveyard * 60_000;
        default:
            return minutes.pending * 60_000;
    }
}

/**
 * The re-check interval of a set: that of its most volatile status. A set without osu!standard beatmaps (whose
 * status is unknown) is re-checked like a graveyard beatmap.
 */
export function getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs(
    statuses: readonly RankedStatus[],
    minutes: Config["recheckMinutes"],
): number | null {
    if (statuses.length === 0) {
        return minutes.graveyard * 60_000;
    }

    const intervals = statuses
        .map((status) => getRecheckIntervalMs(status, minutes))
        .filter((interval) => interval !== null);

    return intervals.length > 0 ? Math.min(...intervals) : null;
}

/**
 * Whether something last checked at `lastChecked` is due for a re-check.
 */
export function isRecheckDue(
    lastChecked: Date,
    intervalMs: number | null,
    now = Date.now(),
): boolean {
    return intervalMs !== null && lastChecked.getTime() < now - intervalMs;
}
```

```ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";

/**
 * The MD5 hash of `data` as lowercase hex, the format of osu!'s `file_md5`.
 */
export function computeMD5(data: Buffer): string {
    return createHash("md5").update(data).digest("hex");
}
```

```ts
import { InFlight } from "@/utils/InFlight";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("InFlight", () => {
    it("shares one run per key while it is pending", async () => {
        const inFlight = new InFlight<string, number>();
        let starts = 0;
        let finish = (_value: number) => {};
        const start = () => {
            starts++;

            return new Promise<number>((resolve) => (finish = resolve));
        };

        const first = inFlight.run("a", start);
        const second = inFlight.run("a", start);

        expect(starts).toBe(1);
        expect(inFlight.size).toBe(1);

        finish(5);

        await expect(first).resolves.toBe(5);
        await expect(second).resolves.toBe(5);
        expect(inFlight.size).toBe(0);
    });

    it("starts again after a run settles, including a failed one", async () => {
        const inFlight = new InFlight<string, number>();

        await expect(
            inFlight.run("a", () => Promise.reject(new Error("x"))),
        ).rejects.toThrow("x");
        await expect(inFlight.run("a", () => Promise.resolve(2))).resolves.toBe(2);
    });

    it("turns a synchronous throw into a rejection", async () => {
        const inFlight = new InFlight<string, number>();

        await expect(
            inFlight.run("a", () => {
                throw new Error("sync");
            }),
        ).rejects.toThrow("sync");
        expect(inFlight.size).toBe(0);
    });

    it("keeps keys apart", async () => {
        const inFlight = new InFlight<string, string>();

        await expect(
            Promise.all([
                inFlight.run("a", () => Promise.resolve("a")),
                inFlight.run("b", () => Promise.resolve("b")),
            ]),
        ).resolves.toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/utils/InFlight.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * Shares one pending operation per key: callers that ask for a key while its operation runs get the same promise.
 * The entry is removed once the operation settles, so a later call starts afresh.
 */
export class InFlight<K, V> {
    private readonly pendingPromises = new Map<K, Promise<V>>();

    get size(): number {
        return this.pendingPromises.size;
    }

    run(key: K, start: () => Promise<V>): Promise<V> {
        const existing = this.pendingPromises.get(key);

        if (existing) {
            return existing;
        }

        // `async` turns a synchronous throw in `start` into a rejection.
        const promise = (async () => start())();

        this.pendingPromises.set(key, promise);
        void promise
            // The caller handles a rejection; this copy only waits for the promise to settle.
            .catch(() => undefined)
            .finally(() => {
                if (this.pendingPromises.get(key) === promise) {
                    this.pendingPromises.delete(key);
                }
            });

        return promise;
    }
}
```

```ts
export interface IOsuGateway {
    /** The osu!standard beatmap, `null` if osu! has none. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    getBeatmap(lane: Lane, idOrHash: number | string): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null>;
    /** Every beatmap of the set (all modes), `null` if osu! has none. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    getBeatmapset(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null>;
    /** The `.osu` file, `null` if osu! has none. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    downloadFile(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<Buffer | null>;
    /** Like `getBeatmap`, but `undefined` at once unless the limiter is idle. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    tryGetBeatmapNow(lane: Lane, idOrHash: number | string): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null | undefined>;
    /** Like `getBeatmapset`, but `undefined` at once unless the limiter is idle. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    tryGetBeatmapsetNow(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null | undefined>;
    /** Up to 500 osu!standard beatmaps approved from `since` on, in approval order. @throws {OsuUnavailableError} */
    getBeatmapsSince(lane: Lane, since: Date): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[]>;
}
```

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { OsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import type { InjectionToken } from "tsyringe";

/**
 * Tokens for dependency injection. Each token is added by the task that introduces its interface.
 *
 * `Symbol.for(...) as InjectionToken<T>` is how tsyringe gives a symbol token its type.
 */
export const dependencyTokens = {
    config: Symbol.for("config") as InjectionToken<Config>,
    db: Symbol.for("db") as InjectionToken<Database>,
    osuGatewayOptions: Symbol.for(
        "osuGatewayOptions",
    ) as InjectionToken<OsuGatewayOptions>,
    osuGateway: Symbol.for("osuGateway") as InjectionToken<IOsuGateway>,
} satisfies Record<string, InjectionToken<unknown>>;
```

`src/osu/IOsuGateway.ts`: the interface from this task's **Interfaces** block, with the imports `import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";` and `import type { Lane } from "./errors";`, one JSDoc line per method as shown.

`src/osu/OsuGateway.ts` (the legacy file with DI and the two new methods; everything not shown changed is as in `legacy/src/osu/OsuGateway.ts`):

```ts
import { formatOsuDate } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import { OsuUnavailableError, type Lane, type RequestKind } from "./errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "./IOsuGateway";
import type { RateLimiter } from "./RateLimiter";

export interface OsuGatewayOptions {
    readonly apiKey: string;
    readonly apiBaseUrl: string;
    readonly fileBaseUrl: string;
    readonly rateLimiter: RateLimiter;

    /**
     * Defaults to 10 000.
     */
    readonly timeoutMs?: number;
}

/**
 * `Retry-After` hint for failures other than osu!'s own `429`.
 */
const failureRetryAfterSeconds = 5;

/**
 * Longest pause honored from a `Retry-After` header (larger values would overflow timers).
 */
const maxRetryAfterSeconds = 3600;

/**
 * Pause after a `429` without a `Retry-After` header.
 */
const defaultRateLimitPauseSeconds = 60;

/**
 * The most beatmaps osu! API v1 returns per `get_beatmaps` call.
 */
export const sincePageSize = 500;

type Outcome<T> =
    | { readonly kind: "ok"; readonly value: T }
    | { readonly kind: "rate-limited"; readonly retryAfterSeconds: number }
    | { readonly kind: "failed"; readonly reason: string };

function redact(url: string): string {
    return url.replace(/([?&]k=)[^&]*/, "$1<redacted>");
}

function parseRetryAfter(header: string | null): number | undefined {
    if (header === null) {
        return undefined;
    }

    const seconds = Number(header);

    return Number.isFinite(seconds) && seconds > 0
        ? Math.min(Math.ceil(seconds), maxRetryAfterSeconds)
        : undefined;
}

function isBeatmapList(body: unknown): body is OsuAPIResponse[] {
    return (
        Array.isArray(body) &&
        body.every(
            (entry) =>
                typeof entry === "object" &&
                entry !== null &&
                "beatmap_id" in entry &&
                "mode" in entry,
        )
    );
}

/**
 * The only code that calls osu!. Each operation is exactly one request through the rate limiter: no retries.
 * Concurrent calls in the same lane for the same thing share one request.
 */
@Injectable(dependencyTokens.osuGateway)
export class OsuGateway implements IOsuGateway {
    private readonly timeoutMs: number;
    private readonly inFlight = new Map<string, Promise<unknown>>();

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.osuGatewayOptions)
        private readonly options: OsuGatewayOptions,
    ) {
        this.timeoutMs = options.timeoutMs ?? 10_000;
    }

    async getBeatmap(
        lane: Lane,
        idOrHash: number | string,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null> {
        const list = await this.getBeatmapList(
            lane,
            this.beatmapQuery(idOrHash),
        );

        return OsuGateway.firstStandard(list);
    }

    async getBeatmapset(
        lane: Lane,
        id: number,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null> {
        const list = await this.getBeatmapList(lane, `s=${id.toString()}`);

        return list.length > 0 ? list : null;
    }

    getBeatmapsSince(lane: Lane, since: Date): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[]> {
        return this.getBeatmapList(
            lane,
            `since=${encodeURIComponent(formatOsuDate(since))}&m=0&limit=${sincePageSize.toString()}`,
        );
    }

    downloadFile(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        return this.shared(`${lane}:file:${id.toString()}`, () =>
            this.options.rateLimiter.schedule(lane, "file", () =>
                this.requestFile(id),
            ),
        );
    }

    async tryGetBeatmapNow(
        lane: Lane,
        idOrHash: number | string,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null | undefined> {
        const query = this.beatmapQuery(idOrHash);
        const list = await this.options.rateLimiter.tryNow(lane, "api", () =>
            this.requestBeatmapList(query),
        );

        return list === undefined ? undefined : OsuGateway.firstStandard(list);
    }

    async tryGetBeatmapsetNow(
        lane: Lane,
        id: number,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null | undefined> {
        const list = await this.options.rateLimiter.tryNow(lane, "api", () =>
            this.requestBeatmapList(`s=${id.toString()}`),
        );

        if (list === undefined) {
            return undefined;
        }

        return list.length > 0 ? list : null;
    }

    // firstStandard, beatmapQuery, getBeatmapList, requestBeatmapList, requestFile, request, unwrap, and shared:
    // copy these private members unchanged from legacy/src/osu/OsuGateway.ts.
}

export type { RequestKind };
```

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { OsuGatewayOptions } from "./OsuGateway";
import { RateLimiter } from "./RateLimiter";

/**
 * The gateway's settings and its rate limiter. A process creates this once: the limiter is the process's whole osu!
 * budget.
 */
export function createOsuGatewayOptions(config: Config): OsuGatewayOptions {
    return {
        apiKey: config.osu.apiKey,
        apiBaseUrl: config.osu.apiBaseUrl,
        fileBaseUrl: config.osu.fileBaseUrl,
        rateLimiter: new RateLimiter({
            ratesPerMinute: config.ratesPerMinute,
            bulkShare: config.bulkShare,
            maxWaitMs: config.maxWaitMs,
        }),
    };
}
```

```ts
// Reads throw DatabaseUnavailableError when the database fails; writes throw the driver's error.
export interface IBeatmapRepository {
    findById(id: number): Promise<BeatmapRow | null>;
    findByHash(hash: string): Promise<BeatmapRow | null>;
    findByIds(ids: readonly number[]): Promise<BeatmapRow[]>;
    /** The set's stored beatmaps, by beatmap ID. */
    findByBeatmapsetId(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<BeatmapRow[]>;
    /** Inserts, or replaces every column of an existing row. */
    upsert(rows: readonly BeatmapRow[]): Promise<void>;
    /** @returns Whether the row existed. */
    update(id: number, changes: Partial<Omit<BeatmapRow, "beatmapId">>): Promise<boolean>;
    delete(id: number): Promise<void>;
}

export interface IBeatmapsetRepository {
    /** When the set was last stored complete, `null` if it never was. */
    findLastChecked(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<Date | null>;
    /**
     * In one transaction: deletes the set's stored beatmaps that are not in `rows`, upserts `rows`, and marks the set
     * complete at `checkedAt`. @returns The IDs of the deleted beatmaps.
     */
    replaceBeatmapset(beatmapsetId: number, rows: readonly BeatmapRow[], checkedAt: Date): Promise<number[]>;
    /** Deletes every stored beatmap of the set and its completeness row. @returns The deleted beatmap IDs. */
    deleteBeatmapset(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<number[]>;
    /** Forgets that the set is complete, so the next set request asks osu!. */
    markIncomplete(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<void>;
}

export interface ICursorRepository {
    getCursor(command: string): Promise<Date | null>;
    setCursor(command: string, cursor: Date): Promise<void>;
    clearCursor(command: string): Promise<void>;
}
```

Also `readOrUnavailable<T>(query: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T>` in `src/database/errors.ts`, `registerDependencies(container, { config, db, osuGatewayOptions })` in `src/dependencies/register.ts`, and `createTestContainer({ db?, config?, osuGatewayOptions?, overrides? })` in `tests/utils/container.ts`.

```ts
        const beatmapsetRepository = new BeatmapsetRepository(database.db);

        await beatmapsetRepository.replaceBeatmapset(1, [buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: "75" }), buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: "76" })], new Date());
        await beatmapsetRepository.markIncomplete(1);

        expect(await beatmapsetRepository.findLastChecked(1)).toBeNull();
        expect(await beatmaps.findByBeatmapsetId(1)).toHaveLength(2);
```

```ts
/**
 * Runs a read query, turning a database failure into {@link DatabaseUnavailableError}.
 */
export async function readOrUnavailable<T>(query: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
    try {
        return await query();
    } catch (e) {
        throw new DatabaseUnavailableError(e);
    }
}
```

`src/repositories/upsertBeatmaps.ts`:

```ts
import { beatmapTable } from "@/database/schema";
import type { BeatmapRow, Database } from "@/database/types";
import { getTableColumns, sql } from "drizzle-orm";

const replaceEveryColumn = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(getTableColumns(beatmapTable))
        .filter(([, column]) => !column.primary)
        .map(([key, column]) => [key, sql.raw(`excluded."${column.name}"`)]),
);

/**
 * Inserts beatmaps, replacing every column of existing rows. Concurrent requests for the same uncached beatmap race
 * to insert it, so the latest data wins.
 *
 * @param db The database or a transaction.
 */
export async function upsertBeatmaps(
    db: Pick<Database, "insert">,
    rows: readonly BeatmapRow[],
): Promise<void> {
    if (rows.length === 0) {
        return;
    }

    await db
        .insert(beatmapTable)
        .values([...rows])
        .onConflictDoUpdate({
            target: beatmapTable.beatmapId,
            set: replaceEveryColumn,
        });
}
```

`src/repositories/BeatmapRepository.ts`:

```ts
import { readOrUnavailable } from "@/database/errors";
import { beatmapTable } from "@/database/schema";
import type { BeatmapRow, Database } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { asc, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IBeatmapRepository } from "./IBeatmapRepository";
import { upsertBeatmaps } from "./upsertBeatmaps";

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapRepository)
export class BeatmapRepository implements IBeatmapRepository {
    constructor(@inject(dependencyTokens.db) private readonly db: Database) {}

    findById(id: number): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        return readOrUnavailable(async () => {
            const [row] = await this.db
                .select()
                .from(beatmapTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapTable.beatmapId, id))
                .limit(1);

            return row ?? null;
        });
    }

    findByHash(hash: string): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        return readOrUnavailable(async () => {
            const [row] = await this.db
                .select()
                .from(beatmapTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapTable.fileMd5, hash))
                .limit(1);

            return row ?? null;
        });
    }

    findByIds(ids: readonly number[]): Promise<BeatmapRow[]> {
        if (ids.length === 0) {
            return Promise.resolve([]);
        }

        return readOrUnavailable(() =>
            this.db
                .select()
                .from(beatmapTable)
                .where(inArray(beatmapTable.beatmapId, [...ids])),
        );
    }

    findByBeatmapsetId(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<BeatmapRow[]> {
        return readOrUnavailable(() =>
            this.db
                .select()
                .from(beatmapTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId))
                .orderBy(asc(beatmapTable.beatmapId)),
        );
    }

    upsert(rows: readonly BeatmapRow[]): Promise<void> {
        return upsertBeatmaps(this.db, rows);
    }

    async update(
        id: number,
        changes: Partial<Omit<BeatmapRow, "beatmapId">>,
    ): Promise<boolean> {
        const updated = await this.db
            .update(beatmapTable)
            .set(changes)
            .where(eq(beatmapTable.beatmapId, id))
            .returning({ id: beatmapTable.beatmapId });

        return updated.length > 0;
    }

    async delete(id: number): Promise<void> {
        await this.db
            .delete(beatmapTable)
            .where(eq(beatmapTable.beatmapId, id));
    }
}
```

`src/repositories/BeatmapsetRepository.ts`:

```ts
import { readOrUnavailable } from "@/database/errors";
import { beatmapsetTable, beatmapTable } from "@/database/schema";
import type { BeatmapRow, Database } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { and, eq, notInArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IBeatmapsetRepository } from "./IBeatmapsetRepository";
import { upsertBeatmaps } from "./upsertBeatmaps";

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapsetRepository)
export class BeatmapsetRepository implements IBeatmapsetRepository {
    constructor(@inject(dependencyTokens.db) private readonly db: Database) {}

    findLastChecked(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<Date | null> {
        return readOrUnavailable(async () => {
            const [set] = await this.db
                .select({ lastChecked: beatmapsetTable.lastChecked })
                .from(beatmapsetTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapsetTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId))
                .limit(1);

            return set?.lastChecked ?? null;
        });
    }

    replaceBeatmapset(
        beatmapsetId: number,
        rows: readonly BeatmapRow[],
        checkedAt: Date,
    ): Promise<number[]> {
        return this.db.transaction(async (tx) => {
            const kept = rows.map((row) => row.beatmapId);
            const removed = await tx
                .delete(beatmapTable)
                .where(
                    and(
                        eq(beatmapTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId),
                        kept.length > 0
                            ? notInArray(beatmapTable.beatmapId, kept)
                            : undefined,
                    ),
                )
                .returning({ id: beatmapTable.beatmapId });

            await upsertBeatmaps(tx, rows);
            await tx
                .insert(beatmapsetTable)
                .values({ beatmapsetId, lastChecked: checkedAt })
                .onConflictDoUpdate({
                    target: beatmapsetTable.beatmapsetId,
                    set: { lastChecked: checkedAt },
                });

            return removed.map(({ id }) => id).sort((a, b) => a - b);
        });
    }

    deleteBeatmapset(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<number[]> {
        return this.db.transaction(async (tx) => {
            const removed = await tx
                .delete(beatmapTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId))
                .returning({ id: beatmapTable.beatmapId });

            await tx
                .delete(beatmapsetTable)
                .where(eq(beatmapsetTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId));

            return removed.map(({ id }) => id).sort((a, b) => a - b);
        });
    }

    async markIncomplete(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<void> {
        await this.db
            .delete(beatmapsetTable)
            .where(eq(beatmapsetTable.beatmapsetId, beatmapsetId));
    }
}
```

`src/repositories/CursorRepository.ts`:

```ts
import { cliCursorTable } from "@/database/schema";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { ICursorRepository } from "./ICursorRepository";

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.cursorRepository)
export class CursorRepository implements ICursorRepository {
    constructor(@inject(dependencyTokens.db) private readonly db: Database) {}

    async getCursor(command: string): Promise<Date | null> {
        const [row] = await this.db
            .select({ cursor: cliCursorTable.cursor })
            .from(cliCursorTable)
            .where(eq(cliCursorTable.command, command))
            .limit(1);

        return row?.cursor ?? null;
    }

    async setCursor(command: string, cursor: Date): Promise<void> {
        await this.db
            .insert(cliCursorTable)
            .values({ command, cursor })
            .onConflictDoUpdate({
                target: cliCursorTable.command,
                set: { cursor },
            });
    }

    async clearCursor(command: string): Promise<void> {
        await this.db
            .delete(cliCursorTable)
            .where(eq(cliCursorTable.command, command));
    }
}
```

`src/dependencies/register.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import type { OsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import { registerInjectables } from "@/server";
import type { DependencyContainer } from "tsyringe";
// Load injectable classes so their metadata is registered.
import "../osu/OsuGateway";
import "../repositories";
import { dependencyTokens } from "./tokens";

/**
 * Instances created at startup rather than by the container.
 */
export interface RuntimeInstances {
    readonly config: Config;
    readonly db: Database;
    readonly osuGatewayOptions: OsuGatewayOptions;
}

/**
 * Registers runtime instances and every `@Injectable` class to a container.
 */
export function registerDependencies(
    container: DependencyContainer,
    instances: RuntimeInstances,
): void {
    container.registerInstance(dependencyTokens.config, instances.config);
    container.registerInstance(dependencyTokens.db, instances.db);
    container.registerInstance(
        dependencyTokens.osuGatewayOptions,
        instances.osuGatewayOptions,
    );
    registerInjectables(container);
}
```

`tests/utils/container.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import { registerDependencies } from "@/dependencies/register";
import { createOsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/createOsuGatewayOptions";
import type { OsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import { testConfig } from "@test/utils/config";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@test/utils/database";
import {
    container as globalContainer,
    type DependencyContainer,
} from "tsyringe";

/**
 * A container for a test. Disposing releases the test database unless one was passed in.
 */
export interface TestContainer extends AsyncDisposable {
    readonly container: DependencyContainer;
}

/**
 * Creates a child container with real implementations and a PGlite database (unless `db` is given).
 */
export async function createTestContainer(
    options: {
        db?: Database;
        config?: Config;
        osuGatewayOptions?: OsuGatewayOptions;
        overrides?: (container: DependencyContainer) => void;
    } = {},
): Promise<TestContainer> {
    const resources = new AsyncDisposableStack();
    const db = options.db ?? resources.use(await createTestDatabase()).db;
    const config = options.config ?? testConfig;
    const container = globalContainer.createChildContainer();

    registerDependencies(container, {
        config,
        db,
        osuGatewayOptions:
            options.osuGatewayOptions ?? createOsuGatewayOptions(config),
    });
    options.overrides?.(container);

    return { container, [Symbol.asyncDispose]: () => resources.disposeAsync() };
}
```

```ts
    read(beatmapId: number): Promise<Buffer | null>;
    /** Writes atomically (temporary file, then rename), creating the directory if needed. */
    write(beatmapId: number, data: Buffer): Promise<void>;
    /** Deletes the file; a missing file is fine. */
```

```ts
import { BeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/BeatmapFileStore";
import { mkdtemp, readdir, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("BeatmapFileStore", () => {
    let root: string;

    beforeEach(async () => {
        root = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "files-"));
    });

    afterEach(() => rm(root, { recursive: true, force: true }));

    it("writes, reads, and deletes files named by beatmap ID", async () => {
        const store = new BeatmapFileStore({ beatmapFileDirectory: root });

        expect(await store.read(75)).toBeNull();

        await store.write(75, Buffer.from("osu"));

        expect(await store.read(75)).toEqual(Buffer.from("osu"));
        expect(await readdir(root)).toEqual(["75.osu"]);

        await store.delete(75);
        await store.delete(75);

        expect(await store.read(75)).toBeNull();
    });

    it("creates the directory on first write", async () => {
        const directory = join(root, "nested", "beatmaps");
        const store = new BeatmapFileStore({ beatmapFileDirectory: directory });

        await store.write(75, Buffer.from("osu"));

        expect(await readdir(directory)).toEqual(["75.osu"]);
    });

    it("leaves a whole file when writes race, and no temporary files", async () => {
        const store = new BeatmapFileStore({ beatmapFileDirectory: root });
        const a = Buffer.alloc(1 << 20, "a");
        const b = Buffer.alloc(1 << 20, "b");

        await Promise.all([store.write(75, a), store.write(75, b)]);

        const stored = await store.read(75);

        expect([a, b]).toContainEqual(stored);
        expect(await readdir(root)).toEqual(["75.osu"]);
    });
});
```

`src/repositories/BeatmapFileStore.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { randomBytes } from "node:crypto";
import { mkdir, readFile, rename, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "./IBeatmapFileStore";

function isMissing(error: unknown): boolean {
    return (
        typeof error === "object" &&
        error !== null &&
        "code" in error &&
        error.code === "ENOENT"
    );
}

/**
 * `.osu` files in `BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY`, named `<beatmap ID>.osu`.
 */
@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileStore)
export class BeatmapFileStore implements IBeatmapFileStore {
    private directoryCreation: Promise<void> | undefined;

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.config)
        private readonly config: Pick<Config, "beatmapFileDirectory">,
    ) {}

    async read(beatmapId: number): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        try {
            return await readFile(this.getFilePath(beatmapId));
        } catch (e) {
            if (isMissing(e)) {
                return null;
            }

            throw e;
        }
    }

    async write(beatmapId: number, data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
        await this.ensureDirectory();

        const target = this.getFilePath(beatmapId);
        // A reader or a crash never sees a partial file: the rename replaces the file in one step.
        const temporary = `${target}.${randomBytes(6).toString("hex")}.tmp`;

        await writeFile(temporary, data);

        try {
            await rename(temporary, target);
        } catch (e) {
            await rm(temporary, { force: true });

            throw e;
        }
    }

    async delete(beatmapId: number): Promise<void> {
        await rm(this.getFilePath(beatmapId), { force: true });
    }

    private getFilePath(beatmapId: number): string {
        return join(
            this.config.beatmapFileDirectory,
            `${beatmapId.toString()}.osu`,
        );
    }

    private ensureDirectory(): Promise<void> {
        this.directoryCreation ??= mkdir(this.config.beatmapFileDirectory, {
            recursive: true,
        }).then(
            () => undefined,
            (e: unknown) => {
                // Let a later write try again.
                this.directoryCreation = undefined;

                throw e;
            },
        );

        return this.directoryCreation;
    }
}
```

Add `export * from "./BeatmapFileStore";` to `src/repositories/index.ts` and the `beatmapFileStore` token (`Symbol.for("beatmapFileStore") as InjectionToken<IBeatmapFileStore>`).

```ts
export interface IBeatmapService {
    /**
     * The beatmap from memory, the database, or osu!, re-checked with osu! when its status's interval has passed
     * and the budget is idle. `null` if osu! has no such osu!standard beatmap.
     *
     * @throws {DatabaseUnavailableError} If the database cannot be read (osu! is not asked).
     * @throws {OsuUnavailableError} If the beatmap is not cached and osu! cannot be asked.
     */
    getBeatmap(lane: Lane, reference: BeatmapReference): Promise<BeatmapRow | null>;

    /**
     * Fetches the beatmap from osu! now (waiting for the budget) and stores it. `null` if osu! no longer has it.
     *
     * @throws {OsuUnavailableError} If osu! cannot be asked.
     */
    refreshBeatmap(lane: Lane, beatmapId: number): Promise<BeatmapRow | null>;

    /** Stores a max combo computed from the beatmap's file. A failed write is logged. */
    setMaxCombo(beatmapId: number, maxCombo: number): Promise<void>;

    /** Puts rows into the memory caches. @returns The frozen copies that were cached. */
    cacheBeatmaps(rows: readonly BeatmapRow[]): readonly BeatmapRow[];

    /** Removes a beatmap from the memory caches. */
    evictBeatmap(row: Pick<BeatmapRow, "beatmapId" | "fileMd5">): void;
}
```

`tests/fakes/FakeOsuGateway.ts`:

```ts
import { formatOsuDate } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { OsuUnavailableError, type Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";

/**
 * osu! in memory. Every call is recorded in `calls` as `"<method> <lane> <argument>"`.
 */
export class FakeOsuGateway implements IOsuGateway {
    readonly beatmaps = new Map<number, OsuAPIResponse>();
    readonly files = new Map<number, Buffer>();
    readonly calls: string[] = [];

    /** The `tryGet…Now` methods answer `undefined`, as when the limiter is busy. */
    isBusy = false;

    /** When set, every call fails with an `OsuUnavailableError` with this many seconds. */
    unavailableForSeconds: number | null = null;

    /** When set, calls wait for it before answering. */
    gate: Promise<void> | null = null;

    addBeatmaps(...responses: OsuAPIResponse[]): this {
        for (const response of responses) {
            this.beatmaps.set(Number(response.beatmap_id), response);
        }

        return this;
    }

    getBeatmap(
        lane: Lane,
        idOrHash: number | string,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null> {
        return this.answer(`getBeatmap ${lane} ${String(idOrHash)}`, () =>
            this.findBeatmap(idOrHash),
        );
    }

    getBeatmapset(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null> {
        return this.answer(`getBeatmapset ${lane} ${id.toString()}`, () =>
            this.findBeatmapset(id),
        );
    }

    downloadFile(lane: Lane, id: number): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        return this.answer(
            `downloadFile ${lane} ${id.toString()}`,
            () => this.files.get(id) ?? null,
        );
    }

    tryGetBeatmapNow(
        lane: Lane,
        idOrHash: number | string,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse | null | undefined> {
        return this.answerNow(
            `tryGetBeatmapNow ${lane} ${String(idOrHash)}`,
            () => this.findBeatmap(idOrHash),
        );
    }

    tryGetBeatmapsetNow(
        lane: Lane,
        id: number,
    ): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[] | null | undefined> {
        return this.answerNow(
            `tryGetBeatmapsetNow ${lane} ${id.toString()}`,
            () => this.findBeatmapset(id),
        );
    }

    getBeatmapsSince(lane: Lane, since: Date): Promise<OsuAPIResponse[]> {
        const from = formatOsuDate(since);

        return this.answer(`getBeatmapsSince ${lane} ${from}`, () =>
            [...this.beatmaps.values()]
                .filter(
                    (b) =>
                        b.mode === "0" &&
                        b.approved_date !== null &&
                        b.approved_date >= from,
                )
                .sort(
                    (a, b) =>
                        (a.approved_date ?? "").localeCompare(
                            b.approved_date ?? "",
                        ) || Number(a.beatmap_id) - Number(b.beatmap_id),
                )
                .slice(0, 500),
        );
    }

    private findBeatmap(idOrHash: number | string): OsuAPIResponse | null {
        const found =
            typeof idOrHash === "number"
                ? this.beatmaps.get(idOrHash)
                : [...this.beatmaps.values()].find(
                      (b) => b.file_md5 === idOrHash,
                  );

        return found?.mode === "0" ? found : null;
    }

    private findBeatmapset(id: number): OsuAPIResponse[] | null {
        const list = [...this.beatmaps.values()].filter(
            (b) => b.beatmapset_id === id.toString(),
        );

        return list.length > 0 ? list : null;
    }

    private async answer<T>(call: string, value: () => T): Promise<T> {
        this.calls.push(call);

        if (this.gate) {
            await this.gate;
        }

        if (this.unavailableForSeconds !== null) {
            throw new OsuUnavailableError(this.unavailableForSeconds);
        }

        return value();
    }

    private answerNow<T>(
        call: string,
        value: () => T,
    ): Promise<T | undefined> {
        if (this.isBusy) {
            this.calls.push(`${call} (busy)`);

            return Promise.resolve(undefined);
        }

        return this.answer(call, value);
    }
}
```

`tests/fakes/FakeBeatmapRepository.ts`:

```ts
import { DatabaseUnavailableError } from "@/database/errors";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import type { IBeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapRepository";

/**
 * The beatmap table in memory.
 */
export class FakeBeatmapRepository implements IBeatmapRepository {
    readonly rows = new Map<number, BeatmapRow>();
    shouldFailReads = false;
    shouldFailWrites = false;
    readCount = 0;

    constructor(...initial: BeatmapRow[]) {
        for (const row of initial) {
            this.rows.set(row.beatmapId, row);
        }
    }

    async findById(id: number): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        this.simulateRead();

        return this.rows.get(id) ?? null;
    }

    async findByHash(hash: string): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        this.simulateRead();

        return [...this.rows.values()].find((r) => r.fileMd5 === hash) ?? null;
    }

    async findByIds(ids: readonly number[]): Promise<BeatmapRow[]> {
        this.simulateRead();

        return ids.flatMap((id) => this.rows.get(id) ?? []);
    }

    async findByBeatmapsetId(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<BeatmapRow[]> {
        this.simulateRead();

        return [...this.rows.values()]
            .filter((r) => r.beatmapsetId === beatmapsetId)
            .sort((a, b) => a.beatmapId - b.beatmapId);
    }

    async upsert(rows: readonly BeatmapRow[]): Promise<void> {
        this.simulateWrite();

        for (const row of rows) {
            this.rows.set(row.beatmapId, { ...row });
        }
    }

    async update(
        id: number,
        changes: Partial<Omit<BeatmapRow, "beatmapId">>,
    ): Promise<boolean> {
        this.simulateWrite();

        const row = this.rows.get(id);

        if (!row) {
            return false;
        }

        this.rows.set(id, { ...row, ...changes });

        return true;
    }

    async delete(id: number): Promise<void> {
        this.simulateWrite();
        this.rows.delete(id);
    }

    /** Counts a database read, and fails it as the real repository would when `shouldFailReads` is set. */
    simulateRead(): void {
        this.readCount++;

        if (this.shouldFailReads) {
            throw new DatabaseUnavailableError(new Error("database down"));
        }
    }

    /** Fails a database write when `shouldFailWrites` is set. */
    simulateWrite(): void {
        if (this.shouldFailWrites) {
            throw new Error("database write failed");
        }
    }
}
```

`tests/fakes/FakeBeatmapsetRepository.ts`:

```ts
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import type { IBeatmapsetRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapsetRepository";
import type { FakeBeatmapRepository } from "./FakeBeatmapRepository";

/**
 * Set completeness in memory, over a {@link FakeBeatmapRepository}'s rows.
 */
export class FakeBeatmapsetRepository implements IBeatmapsetRepository {
    readonly lastCheckedById = new Map<number, Date>();

    constructor(private readonly beatmapRepository: FakeBeatmapRepository) {}

    async findLastChecked(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<Date | null> {
        this.beatmapRepository.simulateRead();

        return this.lastCheckedById.get(beatmapsetId) ?? null;
    }

    async replaceBeatmapset(
        beatmapsetId: number,
        rows: readonly BeatmapRow[],
        checkedAt: Date,
    ): Promise<number[]> {
        this.beatmapRepository.simulateWrite();

        const kept = new Set(rows.map((r) => r.beatmapId));
        const removed = [...this.beatmapRepository.rows.values()]
            .filter((r) => r.beatmapsetId === beatmapsetId && !kept.has(r.beatmapId))
            .map((r) => r.beatmapId)
            .sort((a, b) => a - b);

        for (const id of removed) {
            this.beatmapRepository.rows.delete(id);
        }

        for (const row of rows) {
            this.beatmapRepository.rows.set(row.beatmapId, { ...row });
        }

        this.lastCheckedById.set(beatmapsetId, checkedAt);

        return removed;
    }

    async deleteBeatmapset(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<number[]> {
        this.beatmapRepository.simulateWrite();

        const removed = [...this.beatmapRepository.rows.values()]
            .filter((r) => r.beatmapsetId === beatmapsetId)
            .map((r) => r.beatmapId)
            .sort((a, b) => a - b);

        for (const id of removed) {
            this.beatmapRepository.rows.delete(id);
        }

        this.lastCheckedById.delete(beatmapsetId);

        return removed;
    }

    async markIncomplete(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<void> {
        this.beatmapRepository.simulateWrite();
        this.lastCheckedById.delete(beatmapsetId);
    }
}
```

`tests/fakes/FakeBeatmapFileStore.ts`:

```ts
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapFileStore";

/**
 * `.osu` files in memory.
 */
export class FakeBeatmapFileStore implements IBeatmapFileStore {
    readonly files = new Map<number, Buffer>();
    shouldFailReads = false;
    shouldFailWrites = false;

    async read(beatmapId: number): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        if (this.shouldFailReads) {
            throw new Error("EACCES: permission denied");
        }

        return this.files.get(beatmapId) ?? null;
    }

    async write(beatmapId: number, data: Buffer): Promise<void> {
        if (this.shouldFailWrites) {
            throw new Error("EACCES: permission denied");
        }

        this.files.set(beatmapId, data);
    }

    async delete(beatmapId: number): Promise<void> {
        this.files.delete(beatmapId);
    }
}
```

```ts
import { DatabaseUnavailableError } from "@/database/errors";
import { BeatmapService } from "@/services/BeatmapService";
import { buildApiBeatmap } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { testConfig } from "@test/utils/config";
import { FakeBeatmapRepository } from "@test/fakes/FakeBeatmapRepository";
import { FakeBeatmapsetRepository } from "@test/fakes/FakeBeatmapsetRepository";
import { FakeBeatmapFileStore } from "@test/fakes/FakeBeatmapFileStore";
import { FakeOsuGateway } from "@test/fakes/FakeOsuGateway";
import { silenceConsoleErrors } from "@test/utils/silenceConsoleErrors";
import { buildBeatmapRow } from "@test/utils/rows";
import { RankedStatus } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const hashA = "aaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaaa";
const hashB = "bbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbbb";
const twoDaysAgo = () => new Date(Date.now() - 2 * 86_400_000);

function setup(...stored: ReturnType<typeof buildBeatmapRow>[]) {
    const osuGateway = new FakeOsuGateway();
    const beatmapRepository = new FakeBeatmapRepository(...stored);
    const beatmapsetRepository = new FakeBeatmapsetRepository(beatmapRepository);
    const beatmapFileStore = new FakeBeatmapFileStore();
    const service = new BeatmapService(testConfig, beatmapRepository, beatmapsetRepository, beatmapFileStore, osuGateway);

    return { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, beatmapsetRepository, beatmapFileStore, service };
}

describe("BeatmapService", () => {
    it("asks osu! for an unknown beatmap, stores it, then serves it from memory", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ file_md5: hashA }));

        const first = await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 });

        expect(first).toMatchObject({ beatmapId: 75, fileMd5: hashA });
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)).toMatchObject({ fileMd5: hashA });
        expect(Object.isFrozen(first)).toBe(true);

        await service.getBeatmap("bulk", { id: 75 });
        await service.getBeatmap("bulk", { hash: hashA });

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["getBeatmap priority 75"]);
        expect(beatmapRepository.readCount).toBe(2);
    });

    it("serves a stored beatmap without asking osu!", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup(buildBeatmapRow({ file_md5: hashA }));

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { hash: hashA })).toMatchObject({
            beatmapId: 75,
        });
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("answers null when osu! has no such beatmap", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "1001", mode: "1" }));

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 999 })).toBeNull();
        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 1001 })).toBeNull();
    });

    it("shares one lookup across lanes", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup();
        let open = () => {};

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        osuGateway.gate = new Promise((resolve) => (open = resolve));

        const lookups = [
            service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 }),
            service.getBeatmap("bulk", { id: 75 }),
        ];

        open();

        const [a, b] = await Promise.all(lookups);

        expect(a).toBe(b);
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["getBeatmap priority 75"]);
    });

    it("answers DatabaseUnavailableError without asking osu!", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        beatmapRepository.shouldFailReads = true;

        await expect(service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
            DatabaseUnavailableError,
        );
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("still serves a fetched beatmap when storing it fails", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        beatmapRepository.shouldFailWrites = true;

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).toMatchObject({
            beatmapId: 75,
        });
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it("replaces a stored beatmap that osu! now knows under a new hash", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, beatmapsetRepository, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup(
            buildBeatmapRow({ file_md5: hashA }),
        );

        beatmapFileStore.files.set(75, Buffer.from("old"));
        beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.set(1, new Date());
        await service.getBeatmap("priority", { hash: hashA });
        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ file_md5: hashB }));

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { hash: hashB })).toMatchObject({
            fileMd5: hashB,
        });
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)?.fileMd5).toBe(hashB);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.has(1)).toBe(false);
        // The old hash is no longer served from memory.
        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { hash: hashA })).toBeNull();
    });

    it("never re-checks ranked, approved, or loved beatmaps", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup(
            buildBeatmapRow({}, new Date(0)),
            buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: "76", approved: "2", file_md5: hashA }, new Date(0)),
            buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: "77", approved: "4", file_md5: hashB }, new Date(0)),
        );

        await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 });
        await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 76 });
        await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 77 });

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("re-checks a due beatmap when idle and rewrites its dates", async () => {
        const stale = {
            ...buildBeatmapRow({ approved: "-2", max_combo: "314" }, twoDaysAgo()),
            // A pre-rewrite row with UTC+8 dates.
            submitDate: new Date("2007-10-07T01:46:31Z"),
        };
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup(stale);

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ approved: "-2", max_combo: null, playcount: "2000" }));

        const checked = await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 });

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["tryGetBeatmapNow priority 75"]);
        expect(checked).toMatchObject({
            submitDate: new Date("2007-10-06T17:46:31Z"),
            playcount: 2000,
            // osu! returned no max combo; the stored one is kept.
            maxCombo: 314,
        });
        expect(checked?.lastChecked.getTime()).toBeGreaterThan(
            Date.now() - 60_000,
        );
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)).toMatchObject({
            submitDate: new Date("2007-10-06T17:46:31Z"),
            maxCombo: 314,
        });
    });

    it("serves the cached beatmap when the re-check is skipped or fails", async () => {
        const stale = buildBeatmapRow({ approved: "-2" }, twoDaysAgo());
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup(stale);

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ approved: "1" }));
        osuGateway.isBusy = true;

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).toEqual(stale);

        osuGateway.isBusy = false;
        osuGateway.unavailableForSeconds = 5;

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).toEqual(stale);
    });

    it("forgets a beatmap osu! no longer has", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, beatmapsetRepository, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup(
            buildBeatmapRow({ approved: "-2" }, twoDaysAgo()),
        );

        beatmapFileStore.files.set(75, Buffer.from("old"));
        beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.set(1, new Date());

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).toBeNull();
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["tryGetBeatmapNow priority 75"]);
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.has(75)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.has(1)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("replaces a beatmap whose re-check finds a new hash", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup(
            buildBeatmapRow({ approved: "-2", file_md5: hashA }, twoDaysAgo()),
        );

        beatmapFileStore.files.set(75, Buffer.from("old"));
        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ approved: "3", file_md5: hashB }));

        expect(await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 })).toMatchObject({
            approved: RankedStatus.Qualified,
            fileMd5: hashB,
        });
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)?.fileMd5).toBe(hashB);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("refreshes a beatmap from osu! on demand", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup(buildBeatmapRow({ file_md5: hashA }));

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap({ file_md5: hashB }));

        expect(await service.refreshBeatmap("bulk", 75)).toMatchObject({
            fileMd5: hashB,
        });
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["getBeatmap bulk 75"]);
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)?.fileMd5).toBe(hashB);
    });

    it("stores a computed max combo in memory and the database", async () => {
        const { beatmapRepository, service } = setup(buildBeatmapRow({ max_combo: null }));

        await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 });
        await service.setMaxCombo(75, 2);

        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.get(75)?.maxCombo).toBe(2);

        beatmapRepository.shouldFailReads = true;

        expect((await service.getBeatmap("priority", { id: 75 }))?.maxCombo).toBe(2);
    });
});
```

`src/services/BeatmapService.ts`:

```ts
import { convertApiResponseToRow } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { isRecheckDue, getRecheckIntervalMs } from "@/beatmaps/recheck";
import type { BeatmapReference } from "@/beatmaps/validation";
import { LruCache } from "@/cache/LruCache";
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { OsuUnavailableError, type Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapFileStore";
import type { IBeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapRepository";
import type { IBeatmapsetRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapsetRepository";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { getErrorMessage } from "@/utils/getErrorMessage";
import { InFlight } from "@/utils/InFlight";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import { cacheLifetimeMs } from "./cacheLifetime";
import type { IBeatmapService } from "./IBeatmapService";

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapService)
export class BeatmapService implements IBeatmapService {
    private readonly beatmapsById: LruCache<number, BeatmapRow>;
    private readonly beatmapsByHash: LruCache<string, BeatmapRow>;
    private readonly lookups = new InFlight<string, BeatmapRow | null>();

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.config)
        private readonly config: Pick<Config, "cacheSize" | "recheckMinutes">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapRepository)
        private readonly beatmapRepository: IBeatmapRepository,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapsetRepository)
        private readonly beatmapsetRepository: Pick<IBeatmapsetRepository, "markIncomplete">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileStore)
        private readonly beatmapFileStore: Pick<IBeatmapFileStore, "delete">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.osuGateway)
        private readonly osuGateway: Pick<IOsuGateway, "getBeatmap" | "tryGetBeatmapNow">,
    ) {
        this.beatmapsById = new LruCache(config.cacheSize.beatmaps, cacheLifetimeMs);
        this.beatmapsByHash = new LruCache(config.cacheSize.beatmaps, cacheLifetimeMs);
    }

    getBeatmap(lane: Lane, reference: BeatmapReference): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        const key =
            "id" in reference
                ? `id:${reference.id.toString()}`
                : `hash:${reference.hash}`;

        // Shared regardless of lane: a second caller waits for the first instead of spending budget.
        return this.pendingLookups.run(key, () => this.lookup(lane, reference));
    }

    async refreshBeatmap(lane: Lane, beatmapId: number): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        const fetched = await this.fetch(lane, beatmapId);

        if (!fetched) {
            const known =
                this.beatmapsById.get(beatmapId) ??
                (await this.beatmapRepository.findById(beatmapId).catch(() => null));

            if (known) {
                await this.removeBeatmap(known);
            }
        }

        return fetched;
    }

    async setMaxCombo(beatmapId: number, maxCombo: number): Promise<void> {
        const cached = this.beatmapsById.get(beatmapId);

        if (cached) {
            this.cacheBeatmaps([{ ...cached, maxCombo }]);
        }

        await this.write(`store the max combo of beatmap ${beatmapId.toString()}`, () =>
            this.beatmapRepository.update(beatmapId, { maxCombo }),
        );
    }

    cacheBeatmaps(rows: readonly BeatmapRow[]): readonly BeatmapRow[] {
        return rows.map((row) => {
            const frozen = Object.isFrozen(row) ? row : Object.freeze({ ...row });

            this.beatmapsById.set(frozen.beatmapId, frozen);
            this.beatmapsByHash.set(frozen.fileMd5, frozen);

            return frozen;
        });
    }

    evictBeatmap(row: Pick<BeatmapRow, "beatmapId" | "fileMd5">): void {
        this.beatmapsById.delete(row.beatmapId);
        this.beatmapsByHash.delete(row.fileMd5);
    }

    private async lookup(
        lane: Lane,
        reference: BeatmapReference,
    ): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        // A failed database read throws DatabaseUnavailableError here, before osu! is asked.
        const cached =
            ("id" in reference
                ? this.beatmapsById.get(reference.id)
                : this.beatmapsByHash.get(reference.hash)) ??
            ("id" in reference
                ? await this.beatmapRepository.findById(reference.id)
                : await this.beatmapRepository.findByHash(reference.hash));

        if (!cached) {
            return this.fetch(
                lane,
                "id" in reference ? reference.id : reference.hash,
            );
        }

        const current = this.needsRecheck(cached)
            ? await this.recheck(lane, cached)
            : cached;

        return current ? (this.cacheBeatmaps([current])[0] ?? null) : null;
    }

    private async fetch(
        lane: Lane,
        idOrHash: number | string,
    ): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        const response = await this.osuGateway.getBeatmap(lane, idOrHash);

        if (!response) {
            return null;
        }

        const row = convertApiResponseToRow(response);
        const previous =
            this.beatmapsById.get(row.beatmapId) ??
            (await this.beatmapRepository.findById(row.beatmapId).catch((e: unknown) => {
                console.error(
                    `Failed to read the stored beatmap ${row.beatmapId.toString()}:`,
                    getErrorMessage(e),
                );

                return null;
            }));

        if (previous && previous.fileMd5 !== row.fileMd5) {
            await this.invalidateBeatmap(previous);
        }

        await this.write(`store beatmap ${row.beatmapId.toString()}`, () =>
            this.beatmapRepository.upsert([row]),
        );

        return this.cacheBeatmaps([row])[0] ?? null;
    }

    /**
     * The optional status re-check. It never fails a lookup: when the limiter is busy or osu! is unavailable, the
     * cached beatmap is served unchanged.
     */
    private async recheck(
        lane: Lane,
        cached: BeatmapRow,
    ): Promise<BeatmapRow | null> {
        let response;

        try {
            response = await this.osuGateway.tryGetBeatmapNow(lane, cached.beatmapId);
        } catch (e) {
            if (e instanceof OsuUnavailableError) {
                return cached;
            }

            throw e;
        }

        if (response === undefined) {
            return cached;
        }

        if (response === null) {
            await this.removeBeatmap(cached);

            return null;
        }

        const fresh = convertApiResponseToRow(response);

        if (fresh.fileMd5 !== cached.fileMd5) {
            await this.invalidateBeatmap(cached);
            await this.write(`store beatmap ${fresh.beatmapId.toString()}`, () =>
                this.beatmapRepository.upsert([fresh]),
            );

            return fresh;
        }

        // Every column comes from osu!'s answer (this is how rows with UTC+8 dates heal), except a max combo that
        // osu! does not know and the file provided.
        const updated = { ...fresh, maxCombo: fresh.maxCombo ?? cached.maxCombo };

        await this.write(`update beatmap ${fresh.beatmapId.toString()}`, () =>
            this.beatmapRepository.upsert([updated]),
        );

        return updated;
    }

    /**
     * Drops what is cached about a beatmap whose file changeReason: memory, its file, and its set's completeness.
     */
    private async invalidateBeatmap(previous: BeatmapRow): Promise<void> {
        const id = previous.beatmapId.toString();

        this.evictBeatmap(previous);
        await this.write(`delete the file of beatmap ${id}`, () =>
            this.beatmapFileStore.delete(previous.beatmapId),
        );
        await this.write(`mark beatmapset ${previous.beatmapsetId.toString()} incomplete`, () =>
            this.beatmapsetRepository.markIncomplete(previous.beatmapsetId),
        );
    }

    private async removeBeatmap(row: BeatmapRow): Promise<void> {
        await this.invalidateBeatmap(row);
        await this.write(`delete beatmap ${row.beatmapId.toString()}`, () =>
            this.beatmapRepository.delete(row.beatmapId),
        );
    }

    private needsRecheck(row: BeatmapRow): boolean {
        return isRecheckDue(
            row.lastChecked,
            getRecheckIntervalMs(row.approved, this.config.recheckMinutes),
        );
    }

    /**
     * Runs a write. A failed write is logged; the request is still served from the data in hand.
     */
    private async write(what: string, action: () => Promise<unknown>): Promise<void> {
        try {
            await action();
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(`Failed to ${what}:`, getErrorMessage(e));
        }
    }
}
```

```ts
/**
 * A short description of a caught value for a log line.
 */
export function getErrorMessage(error: unknown): string {
    return error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
}
```

```ts
    /**
     * The set's osu!standard beatmaps by beatmap ID (possibly none), from memory, a complete stored set, or osu!.
     * `null` if osu! has no such set.
     *
     * @throws {DatabaseUnavailableError} If the database cannot be read (osu! is not asked).
     * @throws {OsuUnavailableError} If the set is not stored complete and osu! cannot be asked.

    getBeatmapset(lane: Lane, beatmapsetId: number): Promise<readonly BeatmapRow[] | null>;
}
```

```ts
import { DatabaseUnavailableError } from "@/database/errors";
import { BeatmapService } from "@/services/BeatmapService";
import { BeatmapsetService } from "@/services/BeatmapsetService";
import { buildApiBeatmap } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";

const ids = (rows: readonly { beatmapId: number }[] | null) =>

describe("BeatmapsetService", () => {

        );

        expect([...beatmaps.rows.keys()].sort()).toEqual([75, 76]);

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["getBeatmapset priority 1"]);

        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.has(76)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(76)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(true);

        expect(ids(await service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1))).toEqual([75]);

        osuGateway.isBusy = false;
        osuGateway.unavailableForSeconds = 5;

        expect(ids(await service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1))).toEqual([75]);
    });

    it("never re-checks an all-ranked set", async () => {
        const beatmapRepository = new FakeBeatmapRepository(buildBeatmapRow({}, new Date(0)));
        const { osuGateway, beatmapsetRepository, service } = setup(beatmapRepository);

        beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.set(1, new Date(0));

        expect(ids(await service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1))).toEqual([75]);
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("deletes a set osu! no longer has", async () => {
        const beatmapRepository = new FakeBeatmapRepository(buildBeatmapRow({ approved: "-2" }, twoDaysAgo()));
        const { beatmapsetRepository, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup(beatmapRepository);

        beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.set(1, twoDaysAgo());
        beatmapFileStore.files.set(75, Buffer.from("old"));

        expect(await service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1)).toBeNull();
        expect(beatmapRepository.rows.size).toBe(0);
        expect(beatmapsetRepository.lastCheckedById.has(1)).toBe(false);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("shares one lookup across lanes", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup();
        let open = () => {};

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        osuGateway.gate = new Promise((resolve) => (open = resolve));

        const lookups = [service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1), service.getBeatmapset("bulk", 1)];

        open();
        await Promise.all(lookups);

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["getBeatmapset priority 1"]);
    });

    it("answers DatabaseUnavailableError without asking osu!", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        beatmapRepository.shouldFailReads = true;

        await expect(service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
            DatabaseUnavailableError,
        );
        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([]);
    });

    it("still serves a fetched set when storing it fails", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        const { osuGateway, beatmapRepository, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.addBeatmaps(buildApiBeatmap());
        beatmapRepository.shouldFailWrites = true;

        expect(ids(await service.getBeatmapset("priority", 1))).toEqual([75]);
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
});
```
