`src/services/IBeatmapsetService.ts`: the interface above, with `import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";` and `import type { Lane } from "@/osu/errors";`.

`src/services/BeatmapsetService.ts`:

```ts
import { convertApiResponseToRow } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { isRecheckDue, getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs } from "@/beatmaps/recheck";
import { LruCache } from "@/cache/LruCache";
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { OsuUnavailableError, type Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapFileStore";
import type { IBeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapRepository";
import type { IBeatmapsetRepository } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapsetRepository";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { getErrorMessage } from "@/utils/getErrorMessage";
import { InFlight } from "@/utils/InFlight";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import { cacheLifetimeMs } from "./cacheLifetime";
import type { IBeatmapService } from "./IBeatmapService";
import type { IBeatmapsetService } from "./IBeatmapsetService";

interface CachedSet {
    readonly rows: readonly BeatmapRow[];
    readonly lastChecked: Date;
}

const byBeatmapId = (a: BeatmapRow, b: BeatmapRow) => a.beatmapId - b.beatmapId;

/**
 * Whole beatmapsets. The memory cache may serve a set for up to its lifetime after a single-beatmap lookup found one
 * of its beatmaps changed; the stored set is marked incomplete at once, so the database never serves it stale.
 */
@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapsetService)
export class BeatmapsetService implements IBeatmapsetService {
    private readonly beatmapsetCache: LruCache<number, CachedSet>;
    private readonly lookups = new InFlight<number, readonly BeatmapRow[] | null>();

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.config)
        private readonly config: Pick<Config, "cacheSize" | "recheckMinutes">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapsetRepository)
        private readonly beatmapsetRepository: IBeatmapsetRepository,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapRepository)
        private readonly beatmapRepository: Pick<IBeatmapRepository, "findByBeatmapsetId">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapService)
        private readonly beatmapService: Pick<IBeatmapService, "cacheBeatmaps" | "evictBeatmap">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileStore)
        private readonly beatmapFileStore: Pick<IBeatmapFileStore, "delete">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.osuGateway)
        private readonly osuGateway: Pick<IOsuGateway, "getBeatmapset" | "tryGetBeatmapsetNow">,
    ) {
        this.beatmapsetCache = new LruCache(config.cacheSize.beatmapsets, cacheLifetimeMs);
    }

    getBeatmapset(lane: Lane, beatmapsetId: number): Promise<readonly BeatmapRow[] | null> {
        return this.pendingLookups.run(beatmapsetId, () => this.lookup(lane, beatmapsetId));
    }

    private async lookup(
        lane: Lane,
        beatmapsetId: number,
    ): Promise<readonly BeatmapRow[] | null> {
        // A failed database read throws DatabaseUnavailableError here, before osu! is asked.
        const cached =
            this.beatmapsetCache.get(beatmapsetId) ?? (await this.loadFromDatabase(beatmapsetId));

        if (!cached) {
            return this.storeBeatmapset(
                beatmapsetId,
                await this.osuGateway.getBeatmapset(lane, beatmapsetId),
                null,
            );
        }

        if (!this.needsRecheck(cached)) {
            this.beatmapsetCache.set(beatmapsetId, cached);

            return cached.rows;
        }

        let responses: OsuAPIResponse[] | null | undefined;

        try {
            responses = await this.osuGateway.tryGetBeatmapsetNow(lane, beatmapsetId);
        } catch (e) {
            if (!(e instanceof OsuUnavailableError)) {
                throw e;
            }
        }

        if (responses === undefined) {
            // Busy or unavailable: serve the stored set unchanged.
            this.beatmapsetCache.set(beatmapsetId, cached);

            return cached.rows;
        }

        return this.storeBeatmapset(beatmapsetId, responses, cached.rows);
    }

    private async loadFromDatabase(beatmapsetId: number): Promise<CachedSet | null> {
        const lastChecked = await this.beatmapsetRepository.findLastChecked(beatmapsetId);

        if (!lastChecked) {
            return null;
        }

        const rows = await this.beatmapRepository.findByBeatmapsetId(beatmapsetId);

        return { rows: this.beatmapService.cacheBeatmaps(rows), lastChecked };
    }

    /**
     * Stores osu!'s answer for a set, or deletes the set when osu! no longer has it.
     *
     * @param previous The rows served so far, when re-checking.
     */
    private async storeBeatmapset(
        beatmapsetId: number,
        responses: OsuAPIResponse[] | null,
        previous: readonly BeatmapRow[] | null,
    ): Promise<readonly BeatmapRow[] | null> {
        this.beatmapsetCache.delete(beatmapsetId);

        if (responses === null) {
            if (previous) {
                await this.drop(beatmapsetId, previous);
            }

            return null;
        }

        const checkedAt = new Date();
        const rows = responses
            .filter((response) => response.mode === "0")
            .map((response) => convertApiResponseToRow(response, checkedAt))
            .sort(byBeatmapId);

        try {
            const before = previous ?? (await this.beatmapRepository.findByBeatmapsetId(beatmapsetId));
            const removed = new Set(
                await this.beatmapsetRepository.replaceBeatmapset(beatmapsetId, rows, checkedAt),
            );
            const fresh = new Map(rows.map((row) => [row.beatmapId, row.fileMd5]));
            // Files of removed beatmaps, and of beatmaps whose file changed, are stale.
            const stale = before.filter(
                (row) =>
                    removed.has(row.beatmapId) ||
                    (fresh.has(row.beatmapId) &&
                        fresh.get(row.beatmapId) !== row.fileMd5),
            );

            for (const row of stale) {
                this.beatmapService.evictBeatmap(row);
                await this.beatmapFileStore.delete(row.beatmapId);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(
                `Failed to store beatmapset ${beatmapsetId.toString()}:`,
                getErrorMessage(e),
            );
        }

        const entry = { rows: this.beatmapService.cacheBeatmaps(rows), lastChecked: checkedAt };

        this.beatmapsetCache.set(beatmapsetId, entry);

        return entry.rows;
    }

    private async drop(
        beatmapsetId: number,
        previous: readonly BeatmapRow[],
    ): Promise<void> {
        for (const row of previous) {
            this.beatmapService.evictBeatmap(row);
        }

        try {
            for (const id of await this.beatmapsetRepository.deleteBeatmapset(beatmapsetId)) {
                await this.beatmapFileStore.delete(id);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(
                `Failed to delete beatmapset ${beatmapsetId.toString()}:`,
                getErrorMessage(e),
            );
        }
    }

    private needsRecheck(set: CachedSet): boolean {
        return isRecheckDue(
            set.lastChecked,
            getBeatmapsetRecheckIntervalMs(
                set.rows.map((row) => row.approved),
                this.config.recheckMinutes,
            ),
        );
    }
}
```

```ts
     * The beatmap's `.osu` file, checked against its `fileMd5`: from the store, or downloaded and stored. `null` if

     *
```

```ts
const beatmap = buildBeatmapRow({ file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(file), max_combo: "314" });

function setup() {

        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup();

        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup();

        beatmapFileStore.files.set(75, file.subarray(0, 20));
        osuGateway.files.set(75, file);

        expect(await service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).toEqual(file);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.get(75)).toEqual(file);
    });

    it("answers null when osu! has no file", async () => {
        const { service } = setup();

        expect(await service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).toBeNull();
    });

    it("serves the file when storing it fails", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.files.set(75, file);
        beatmapFileStore.shouldFailWrites = true;

        expect(await service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).toEqual(file);
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it("downloads when the stored file cannot be read", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.files.set(75, file);
        beatmapFileStore.shouldFailReads = true;

        expect(await service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).toEqual(file);
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it("shares one download across lanes", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, service } = setup();
        let open = () => {};

        osuGateway.files.set(75, file);
        osuGateway.gate = new Promise((resolve) => (open = resolve));

        const downloads = [
            service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap),
            service.getBeatmapFile("bulk", beatmap),
        ];

        open();
        await Promise.all(downloads);

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual(["downloadFile priority 75"]);
    });

    it("refreshes the metadata once when the download matches a newer version", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, beatmapService, service } = setup();
        const newer = buildOsuFile(75, "Normal v2");

        osuGateway.files.set(75, newer);
        beatmapService.refreshBeatmap.mockResolvedValue(buildBeatmapRow({ file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(newer) }));

        expect(await service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).toEqual(newer);
        expect(beatmapService.refresh).toHaveBeenCalledWith("priority", 75);
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.get(75)).toEqual(newer);
    });

    it("answers unavailable when the download matches no metadata", async () => {
        silenceConsoleErrors();
        const { osuGateway, beatmapFileStore, beatmapService, service } = setup();

        osuGateway.files.set(75, buildOsuFile(75, "Something else"));
        beatmapService.refreshBeatmap.mockResolvedValue(beatmap);

        await expect(service.getBeatmapFile("priority", beatmap)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(
            OsuUnavailableError,
        );
        expect(beatmapFileStore.files.has(75)).toBe(false);
    });

    it("backfills a missing max combo from the file", async () => {
        const { beatmapService, service } = setup();

        service.backfillMaxCombo({ ...beatmap, maxCombo: null }, file);

        await vi.waitFor(() =>
            expect(beatmapService.setMaxCombo).toHaveBeenCalledWith(75, 2),
        );
    });

    it("leaves a known max combo alone", async () => {
        const { beatmapService, service } = setup();

        service.backfillMaxCombo(beatmap, file);
        await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 10));

        expect(beatmapService.setMaxCombo).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
});
```

`src/services/BeatmapFileService.ts`:

```ts
import { computeMD5 } from "@/beatmaps/md5";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { OsuUnavailableError, type Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import type { IBeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/IBeatmapFileStore";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { getErrorMessage } from "@/utils/getErrorMessage";
import { InFlight } from "@/utils/InFlight";
import { BeatmapDecoder } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IBeatmapFileService } from "./IBeatmapFileService";
import type { IBeatmapService } from "./IBeatmapService";

/**
 * `Retry-After` when osu! serves a file that matches no metadata: osu! is mid-update, so asking again soon is right.
 */
const inconsistentRetryAfterSeconds = 5;

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileService)
export class BeatmapFileService implements IBeatmapFileService {
    private readonly downloads = new InFlight<string, Buffer | null>();

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileStore)
        private readonly beatmapFileStore: IBeatmapFileStore,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.osuGateway)
        private readonly osuGateway: Pick<IOsuGateway, "downloadFile">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapService)
        private readonly beatmapService: Pick<IBeatmapService, "refreshBeatmap" | "setMaxCombo">,
    ) {}

    getBeatmapFile(lane: Lane, beatmap: BeatmapRow): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        // Shared regardless of lane: one download per beatmap version.
        return this.pendingDownloads.run(
            `${beatmap.beatmapId.toString()}:${beatmap.fileMd5}`,
            () => this.loadFile(lane, beatmap),
        );
    }

    backfillMaxCombo(beatmap: BeatmapRow, file: Buffer): void {
        if (beatmap.maxCombo !== null) {
            return;
        }

        // After the response: decoding takes a moment and must not delay it.
        setImmediate(() => {
            void (async () => {
                try {
                    const { maxCombo } = new BeatmapDecoder().decode(
                        file.toString(),
                    ).result;

                    await this.beatmapService.setMaxCombo(beatmap.beatmapId, maxCombo);
                } catch (e) {
                    console.error(
                        `Failed to compute the max combo of beatmap ${beatmap.beatmapId.toString()}:`,
                        getErrorMessage(e),
                    );
                }
            })();
        });
    }

    private async loadFile(lane: Lane, beatmap: BeatmapRow): Promise<Buffer | null> {
        const id = beatmap.beatmapId;
        const stored = await this.beatmapFileStore.read(id).catch((e: unknown) => {
            console.error(
                `Failed to read the file of beatmap ${id.toString()}:`,
                getErrorMessage(e),
            );

            return null;
        });

        if (stored) {
            if (computeMD5(stored) === beatmap.fileMd5) {
                return stored;
            }

            // Truncated, corrupt, or an older version.
            await this.beatmapFileStore.delete(id).catch(() => undefined);
        }

        const downloaded = await this.osuGateway.downloadFile(lane, id);

        if (!downloaded) {
            return null;
        }

        const hash = computeMD5(downloaded);

        if (hash !== beatmap.fileMd5) {
            // The beatmap may have been updated after its metadata was fetched.
            const current = await this.beatmapService.refreshBeatmap(lane, id);

            if (current?.fileMd5 !== hash) {
                console.error(
                    `osu! served a file for beatmap ${id.toString()} that matches neither its stored nor its current metadata`,
                );

                throw new OsuUnavailableError(inconsistentRetryAfterSeconds);
            }
        }

        try {
            await this.beatmapFileStore.write(id, downloaded);
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(
                `Failed to store the file of beatmap ${id.toString()}:`,
                getErrorMessage(e),
            );
        }

        return downloaded;
    }
}
```

```ts
// src/access/types.ts
export interface ApiKeyRecord {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly lane: Lane;
    /** `null` for unlimited. */
    readonly rateLimitPerMinute: number | null;
}
export interface StoredApiKey extends ApiKeyRecord { readonly keyHash: string; }
export interface KeyListing extends ApiKeyRecord { readonly createdAt: Date; readonly revokedAt: Date | null; }
export type Caller = { readonly kind: "key"; readonly key: ApiKeyRecord } | { readonly kind: "anonymous" };
export const anonymousCaller: Caller;
/** The lane a caller's requests use: a key's lane, or bulk for anonymous callers. */
export function getCallerLane(caller: Caller): Lane;

// src/access/keys.ts
export function generateApiKey(): string;   // 32 random bytes, base64url (43 characters)
export function hashApiKey(key: string): string; // SHA-256, hex

// src/access/names.ts
export function isValidKeyName(name: string): boolean; // /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/

// src/access/snapshot.ts
export type KeySnapshot = ReadonlyMap<string, ApiKeyRecord>; // by key hash
export const builtInKeyNames: { readonly priority: "built-in:priority"; readonly bulk: "built-in:bulk" };
export function buildKeySnapshot(stored: readonly StoredApiKey[], builtIn: Config["builtInKeys"]): KeySnapshot;

// src/access/IKeyService.ts
export interface IKeyService {
    /** Loads the keys. @throws If the database cannot be read. */
    load(): Promise<void>;
    /** Loads the keys; on failure logs and keeps the previous ones. @returns Whether it loaded. */
    reload(): Promise<boolean>;
    /** Reloads every `KEY_RELOAD_INTERVAL_SECONDS`; disposing stops it. */
    startReloading(): Disposable;
    authenticate(rawKey: string): ApiKeyRecord | undefined;
    describeKeys(): string;
}

// src/repositories/IKeyRepository.ts
export interface IKeyRepository {
    /** Unrevoked keys. */
    listActive(): Promise<StoredApiKey[]>;
    /** Every key, revoked ones included, by name. */
    list(): Promise<KeyListing[]>;
    findByName(name: string): Promise<KeyListing | null>;
    insert(key: { name: string; keyHash: string; lane: Lane; rateLimitPerMinute: number | null }): Promise<void>;
    update(name: string, changes: { lane?: Lane; rateLimitPerMinute?: number | null }): Promise<void>;
    /** @returns `false` if the key was already revoked. */
    revoke(name: string): Promise<boolean>;
}

// src/http/requestContext.ts
export function setCaller(res: Response, caller: Caller): void;
export function getCaller(res: Response): Caller; // throws if the auth middleware has not run
export function findCaller(res: Response): Caller | undefined;

// src/middleware/auth.ts
export function createAuthMiddleware(keys: Pick<IKeyService, "authenticate">, options: { readonly allowAnonymous: boolean }): RequestHandler;

// src/middleware/rateLimit.ts
export function createRateLimitMiddleware(options: { readonly anonymousLimit: number }): RequestHandler;
```

```ts
import { hashApiKey } from "@/access/keys";
import { buildKeySnapshot } from "@/access/snapshot";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("buildKeySnapshot", () => {
    it("holds the built-in keys and the stored keys by hash", () => {
        const snapshot = buildKeySnapshot(
            [
                {
                    name: "site",
                    lane: "bulk",
                    rateLimitPerMinute: 120,
                    keyHash: hashApiKey("site-key"),
                },
            ],
            { priority: "internal", bulk: "bulk" },
        );

        expect(snapshot.get(hashApiKey("internal"))).toEqual({
            name: "built-in:priority",
            lane: "priority",
            rateLimitPerMinute: null,
        });
        expect(snapshot.get(hashApiKey("bulk"))).toEqual({
            name: "built-in:bulk",
            lane: "bulk",
            rateLimitPerMinute: null,
        });
        expect(snapshot.get(hashApiKey("site-key"))).toEqual({
            name: "site",
            lane: "bulk",
            rateLimitPerMinute: 120,
        });
    });

    it("leaves out unset built-in keys, and lets a built-in key win a collision", () => {
        const snapshot = buildKeySnapshot(
            [
                {
                    name: "copy",
                    lane: "bulk",
                    rateLimitPerMinute: 1,
                    keyHash: hashApiKey("internal"),
                },
            ],
            { priority: "internal", bulk: undefined },
        );

        expect(snapshot.size).toBe(1);
        expect(snapshot.get(hashApiKey("internal"))?.name).toBe(
            "built-in:priority",
        );
    });
});
```

```ts
import { hashApiKey } from "@/access/keys";
import { KeyService } from "@/access/KeyService";
import type { StoredApiKey } from "@/access/types";
import { silenceConsoleErrors } from "@test/utils/silenceConsoleErrors";
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const stored = (name: string): StoredApiKey => ({
    name,
    lane: "bulk",
    rateLimitPerMinute: 60,
    keyHash: hashApiKey(`${name}-key`),
});

const config = {
    builtInKeys: { priority: "internal", bulk: undefined },
    keyReloadIntervalSeconds: 30,
};

afterEach(() => {
    vi.useRealTimers();
});

describe("KeyService", () => {
    it("authenticates built-in and stored keys after loading", async () => {
        const service = new KeyService(
            { listActive: () => Promise.resolve([stored("site")]) },
            config,
        );

        expect(service.authenticate("internal")).toBeUndefined();

        await service.load();

        expect(service.authenticate("internal")?.lane).toBe("priority");
        expect(service.authenticate("site-key")?.name).toBe("site");
        expect(service.authenticate("wrong")).toBeUndefined();
        expect(service.describeKeys()).toBe("2 keys (1 built in)");
    });

    it("keeps the previous keys when a reload fails", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        const listActive = vi
            .fn<() => Promise<StoredApiKey[]>>()
            .mockResolvedValueOnce([stored("site")])
            .mockRejectedValueOnce(new Error("database down"));
        const service = new KeyService({ listActive }, config);

        await service.load();

        expect(await service.reload()).toBe(false);
        expect(service.authenticate("site-key")?.name).toBe("site");
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });

    it("never lets an older load replace a newer one", async () => {
        let finishFirst = (_keys: StoredApiKey[]) => {};
        const listActive = vi
            .fn<() => Promise<StoredApiKey[]>>()
            .mockReturnValueOnce(new Promise((resolve) => (finishFirst = resolve)))
            .mockResolvedValueOnce([stored("newer")]);
        const service = new KeyService({ listActive }, config);

        const first = service.load();

        await service.load();
        finishFirst([stored("older")]);
        await first;

        expect(service.authenticate("newer-key")).toBeDefined();
        expect(service.authenticate("older-key")).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it("reloads on its interval until disposed", async () => {
        vi.useFakeTimers();

        const listActive = vi
            .fn<() => Promise<StoredApiKey[]>>()
            .mockResolvedValue([]);
        const service = new KeyService({ listActive }, config);
        const reloading = service.startReloading();

        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(30_000);

        expect(listActive).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);

        reloading[Symbol.dispose]();
        await vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(60_000);

        expect(listActive).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    });
});
```

`tests/unit/middleware/auth.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiKeyRecord } from "@/access/types";
import { findCaller } from "@/http/requestContext";
import { createAuthMiddleware } from "@/middleware/auth";
import express from "express";
import request from "supertest";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const bot: ApiKeyRecord = {
    name: "bot",
    lane: "priority",
    rateLimitPerMinute: null,
};

function createApp(allowAnonymous = true) {
    return express()
        .use(
            createAuthMiddleware(
                {
                    authenticate: (raw) =>
                        raw === "good-key" ? bot : undefined,
                },
                { allowAnonymous },
            ),
        )
        .get("/", (_req, res) => {
            res.json(findCaller(res));
        });
}

describe("auth middleware", () => {
    it("accepts a key in the header or the query", async () => {
        for (const send of [
            request(createApp()).get("/").set("X-Api-Key", "good-key"),
            request(createApp()).get("/?key=good-key"),
            request(createApp())
                .get("/?key=good-key")
                .set("X-Api-Key", "good-key"),
        ]) {
            const response = await send.expect(200);

            expect(response.body).toEqual({ kind: "key", key: bot });
        }
    });

    it("treats a request without a key as anonymous", async () => {
        const response = await request(createApp()).get("/").expect(200);

        expect(response.body).toEqual({ kind: "anonymous" });
    });

    it("requires a key when anonymous access is off", async () => {
        await request(createApp(false))
            .get("/")
            .expect(401, { error: "API key required" });
        await request(createApp(false)).get("/?key=good-key").expect(200);
    });

    it.each([
        ["/?key=wrong", undefined],
        ["/?key=", undefined],
        ["/?key=good-key&key=good-key", undefined],
        ["/", ""],
        ["/", "wrong"],
        ["/?key=good-key", "other"],
    ])("rejects %s with header %j", async (path, header) => {
        const send = request(createApp()).get(path);

        if (header !== undefined) {
            send.set("X-Api-Key", header);
        }

        await send.expect(401, { error: "Unauthorized" });
    });
});
```

`tests/unit/middleware/rateLimit.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { ApiKeyRecord, Caller } from "@/access/types";
import { anonymousCaller } from "@/access/types";
import { setCaller } from "@/http/requestContext";
import { createRateLimitMiddleware } from "@/middleware/rateLimit";
import express from "express";
import request from "supertest";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const key = (name: string, rateLimitPerMinute: number | null): ApiKeyRecord => ({
    name,
    lane: "bulk",
    rateLimitPerMinute,
});

const callers: Record<string, Caller> = {
    limited: { kind: "key", key: key("limited", 2) },
    other: { kind: "key", key: key("other", 2) },
    unlimited: { kind: "key", key: key("unlimited", null) },
};

function createApp() {
    return express()
        .use((req, res, next) => {
            const name = req.get("x-caller");

            setCaller(res, (name && callers[name]) || anonymousCaller);
            next();
        })
        .use(createRateLimitMiddleware({ anonymousLimit: 1 }))
        .get("/", (_req, res) => {
            res.json({ ok: true });
        });
}

describe("rate limit middleware", () => {
    it("limits each key by its own per-minute limit, with standard headers", async () => {
        const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
        const app = createApp();
        const send = (caller: string) => request(app).get("/").set("X-Caller", caller);

        expect((await send("limited")).headers["ratelimit-policy"]).toBeDefined();
        await send("limited").expect(200);
        await send("limited").expect(429, { error: "Rate limit exceeded" });
        await send("other").expect(200);
        expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Rate limit reached by key limited");
    });

    it("never limits unlimited keys", async () => {
        const app = createApp();

        for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
            const response = await request(app).get("/").set("X-Caller", "unlimited");

            expect(response.status).toBe(200);
            expect(response.headers["ratelimit-policy"]).toBeUndefined();
        }
    });

    it("limits anonymous callers per IP without logging each rejection", async () => {
        const warn = vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
        const app = createApp();

        await request(app).get("/").expect(200);
        await request(app).get("/").expect(429);
        expect(warn).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
});
```

`src/access/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { Lane } from "@/osu/errors";

/**
 * An API key as the service uses it.
 */
export interface ApiKeyRecord {
    readonly name: string;
    readonly lane: Lane;

    /**
     * Requests per minute, `null` for unlimited.
     */
    readonly rateLimitPerMinute: number | null;
}

/**
 * A stored key with the hash it is looked up by.
 */
export interface StoredApiKey extends ApiKeyRecord {
    readonly keyHash: string;
}

/**
 * A key as the CLI lists it.
 */
export interface KeyListing extends ApiKeyRecord {
    readonly createdAt: Date;
    readonly revokedAt: Date | null;
}

export type Caller =
    | { readonly kind: "key"; readonly key: ApiKeyRecord }
    | { readonly kind: "anonymous" };

export const anonymousCaller: Caller = { kind: "anonymous" };

/**
 * The lane a caller's requests use: a key's lane, or bulk for anonymous callers, so that public traffic never slows
 * down the game or the bot.
 */
export function getCallerLane(caller: Caller): Lane {
    return caller.kind === "key" ? caller.key.lane : "bulk";
}
```

`src/access/keys.ts`:

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

/**
 * A new API key: 32 random bytes, base64url-encoded (43 characters).
 */
export function generateApiKey(): string {
    return randomBytes(32).toString("base64url");
}

/**
 * The SHA-256 hash (hex) under which a key is stored and looked up.
 */
export function hashApiKey(key: string): string {
    return createHash("sha256").update(key).digest("hex");
}
```

`src/access/names.ts`:

```ts
const namePattern = /^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]*$/;

/**
 * Whether a key name is allowed: lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens, not starting with a hyphen. Built-in key
 * names contain a colon, so a stored key can never take one.
 */
export function isValidKeyName(name: string): boolean {
    return namePattern.test(name);
}
```

`src/access/snapshot.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import { hashApiKey } from "./keys";
import type { ApiKeyRecord, StoredApiKey } from "./types";

/**
 * Keys by their hash.
 */
export type KeySnapshot = ReadonlyMap<string, ApiKeyRecord>;

export const builtInKeyNames = {
    priority: "built-in:priority",
    bulk: "built-in:bulk",
} as const;

/**
 * The keys requests can use: the built-in keys from the environment (unlimited, one per lane), then the stored keys.
 * A stored key whose hash equals a built-in key's is ignored.
 */
export function buildKeySnapshot(
    stored: readonly StoredApiKey[],
    builtIn: Config["builtInKeys"],
): KeySnapshot {
    const keys = new Map<string, ApiKeyRecord>();

    if (builtIn.priority !== undefined) {
        keys.set(hashApiKey(builtIn.priority), {
            name: builtInKeyNames.priority,
            lane: "priority",
            rateLimitPerMinute: null,
        });
    }

    if (builtIn.bulk !== undefined) {
        keys.set(hashApiKey(builtIn.bulk), {
            name: builtInKeyNames.bulk,
            lane: "bulk",
            rateLimitPerMinute: null,
        });
    }

    for (const { keyHash, name, lane, rateLimitPerMinute } of stored) {
        if (!keys.has(keyHash)) {
            keys.set(keyHash, { name, lane, rateLimitPerMinute });
        }
    }

    return keys;
}
```

`src/access/KeyService.ts`:

```ts
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import type { IKeyRepository } from "@/repositories/IKeyRepository";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { getErrorMessage } from "@/utils/getErrorMessage";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IKeyService } from "./IKeyService";
import { hashApiKey } from "./keys";
import { buildKeySnapshot, type KeySnapshot } from "./snapshot";
import type { ApiKeyRecord } from "./types";

/**
 * The keys in memory, so that authenticating never touches the database (a database outage does not lock callers
 * out).
 */
@Injectable(dependencyTokens.keyService)
export class KeyService implements IKeyService {
    private snapshot: KeySnapshot = new Map();
    /** The generation of the newest load started. */
    private latestLoadGeneration = 0;
    /** The generation of the loaded snapshot; a load that finishes with an older one is stale. */
    private appliedLoadGeneration = 0;

    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.keyRepository)
        private readonly keyRepository: Pick<IKeyRepository, "listActive">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.config)
        private readonly config: Pick<
            Config,
            "builtInKeys" | "keyReloadIntervalSeconds"
        >,
    ) {}

    async load(): Promise<void> {
        // Loads can overlap (timer and SIGHUP); one that started earlier must not replace a newer snapshot.
        const generation = ++this.latestLoadGeneration;
        const stored = await this.keyRepository.listActive();

        if (generation < this.appliedLoadGeneration) {
            return;
        }

        this.appliedLoadGeneration = generation;
        this.snapshot = buildKeySnapshot(stored, this.config.builtInKeys);
    }

    async reload(): Promise<boolean> {
        try {
            await this.load();

            return true;
        } catch (e) {
            console.error(
                "Failed to reload the API keys; keeping the previous ones:",
                getErrorMessage(e),
            );

            return false;
        }
    }

    startReloading(): Disposable {
        const timer = setInterval(() => {
            void this.reload();
        }, this.config.keyReloadIntervalSeconds * 1000);

        timer.unref();

        return { [Symbol.dispose]: () => clearInterval(timer) };
    }

    authenticate(rawKey: string): ApiKeyRecord | undefined {
        // The map lookup compares hashes, not keys, so its timing reveals nothing usable about stored keys.
        return this.snapshot.get(hashApiKey(rawKey));
    }

    describeKeys(): string {
        const { builtInKeys } = this.config;
        const builtIn = [builtInKeys.priority, builtInKeys.bulk].filter(
            (key) => key !== undefined,
        ).length;

        return `${this.snapshot.size.toString()} keys (${builtIn.toString()} built in)`;
    }
}
```

`src/repositories/KeyRepository.ts`:

```ts
import type { KeyListing, StoredApiKey } from "@/access/types";
import { apiKeysTable } from "@/database/schema";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import type { Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import { Injectable } from "@/server";
import { and, asc, eq, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";
import type { IKeyRepository } from "./IKeyRepository";

const listing = {
    name: apiKeysTable.name,
    lane: apiKeysTable.lane,
    rateLimitPerMinute: apiKeysTable.rateLimitPerMinute,
    createdAt: apiKeysTable.createdAt,
    revokedAt: apiKeysTable.revokedAt,
};

@Injectable(dependencyTokens.keyRepository)
export class KeyRepository implements IKeyRepository {
    constructor(@inject(dependencyTokens.db) private readonly db: Database) {}

    listActive(): Promise<StoredApiKey[]> {
        return this.db
            .select({
                name: apiKeysTable.name,
                lane: apiKeysTable.lane,
                rateLimitPerMinute: apiKeysTable.rateLimitPerMinute,
                keyHash: apiKeysTable.keyHash,
            })
            .from(apiKeysTable)
            .where(isNull(apiKeysTable.revokedAt))
            .orderBy(asc(apiKeysTable.name));
    }

    list(): Promise<KeyListing[]> {
        return this.db
            .select(listing)
            .from(apiKeysTable)
            .orderBy(asc(apiKeysTable.name));
    }

    async findByName(name: string): Promise<KeyListing | null> {
        const [key] = await this.db
            .select(listing)
            .from(apiKeysTable)
            .where(eq(apiKeysTable.name, name))
            .limit(1);

        return key ?? null;
    }

    async insert(key: {
        name: string;
        keyHash: string;
        lane: Lane;
        rateLimitPerMinute: number | null;
    }): Promise<void> {
        await this.db.insert(apiKeysTable).values(key);
    }

    async update(
        name: string,
        changes: { lane?: Lane; rateLimitPerMinute?: number | null },
    ): Promise<void> {
        await this.db
            .update(apiKeysTable)
            .set(changes)
            .where(eq(apiKeysTable.name, name));
    }

    async revoke(name: string): Promise<boolean> {
        const revoked = await this.db
            .update(apiKeysTable)
            .set({ revokedAt: new Date() })
            .where(and(eq(apiKeysTable.name, name), isNull(apiKeysTable.revokedAt)))
            .returning({ name: apiKeysTable.name });

        return revoked.length > 0;
    }
}
```

`src/http/requestContext.ts`:

```ts
import type { Caller } from "@/access/types";
import type { Response } from "express";

// Keyed by response object, so per-request state stays typed instead of living in `res.locals`.
const callers = new WeakMap<Response, Caller>();

export function setCaller(res: Response, caller: Caller): void {
    callers.set(res, caller);
}

/**
 * The caller of a request that passed the auth middleware.
 *
 * @throws {Error} If the auth middleware has not run for this response.
 */
export function getCaller(res: Response): Caller {
    const caller = callers.get(res);

    if (!caller) {
        throw new Error("The auth middleware has not run for this request");
    }

    return caller;
}

/**
 * The caller, or `undefined` if the request was rejected before it was authenticated.
 */
export function findCaller(res: Response): Caller | undefined {
    return callers.get(res);
}
```

`src/middleware/auth.ts`:

```ts
import type { IKeyService } from "@/access/IKeyService";
import { anonymousCaller } from "@/access/types";
import { setCaller } from "@/http/requestContext";
import { UnauthorizedError } from "@/server";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";

/**
 * Identifies the caller from the `X-Api-Key` header or the `key` query parameter (which the game, the bot, and the
 * dpp processors send). No key means anonymous, unless anonymous access is off. An unknown, revoked, or empty key,
 * a repeated `key` parameter, or a header and a parameter that differ are rejected rather than treated as
 * anonymous, so a misconfigured client notices at once.
 */
export function createAuthMiddleware(
    keys: Pick<IKeyService, "authenticate">,
    options: { readonly allowAnonymous: boolean },
): RequestHandler {
    const reject = (res: Parameters<RequestHandler>[1], message?: string) => {
        const error = new UnauthorizedError(message);

        res.status(error.statusCode).json(error.toResponse());
    };

    return (req, res, next) => {
        const header = req.get("x-api-key");
        const query: unknown = req.query.key;

        if (query !== undefined && typeof query !== "string") {
            reject(res);

            return;
        }

        if (header !== undefined && query !== undefined && header !== query) {
            reject(res);

            return;
        }

        const raw = header ?? query;

        if (raw === undefined) {
            if (!options.allowAnonymous) {
                reject(res, "API key required");

                return;
            }

            setCaller(res, anonymousCaller);
            next();

            return;
        }

        const key = keys.authenticate(raw);

        if (!key) {
            reject(res);

            return;
        }

        setCaller(res, { kind: "key", key });
        next();
    };
}
```

`src/middleware/rateLimit.ts`:

```ts
import type { Caller } from "@/access/types";
import { getCaller } from "@/http/requestContext";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";
import { ipKeyGenerator, rateLimit } from "express-rate-limit";

/**
 * Per-minute limits on requests to this service: each key by its own limit (none if unlimited), anonymous callers
 * by IP. Counters live in memory, which is correct for a single process. The osu! budget behind the service is
 * limited separately, by lane.
 */
export function createRateLimitMiddleware(options: {
    readonly anonymousLimit: number;
}): RequestHandler {
    const limitOf = (caller: Caller): number | null =>
        caller.kind === "key"
            ? caller.key.rateLimitPerMinute
            : options.anonymousLimit;

    return rateLimit({
        windowMs: 60_000,
        // Unlimited callers are skipped before this runs; the fallback only satisfies the type.
        limit: (_req, res) => limitOf(getCaller(res)) ?? Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER,
        skip: (_req, res) => limitOf(getCaller(res)) === null,
        keyGenerator: (req, res) => {
            const caller = getCaller(res);

            return caller.kind === "key"
                ? `key:${caller.key.name}`
                : `ip:${ipKeyGenerator(req.ip ?? "")}`;
        },
        standardHeaders: "draft-8",
        legacyHeaders: false,
        handler: (_req, res) => {
            const caller = getCaller(res);

            if (caller.kind === "key") {
                console.warn(`Rate limit reached by key ${caller.key.name}`);
            }

            res.status(429).json({ error: "Rate limit exceeded" });
        },
    });
}
```

`tests/utils/app.ts`:

```ts
import { createApp } from "@/app";
import type { Config } from "@/config/config";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { createFakeOsuHandler, type FakeOsuState } from "@test/fixtures/osu/fakeOsu";
import { createGoldenOsuState } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { testConfig } from "@test/utils/config";
import { createTestContainer } from "@test/utils/container";
import { createTestDatabase, type TestDatabase } from "@test/utils/database";
import { startStubOsu, type StubOsu } from "@test/utils/stubOsu";
import type { Express } from "express";
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import type { DependencyContainer } from "tsyringe";

export interface TestApp extends AsyncDisposable {
    readonly app: Express;
    readonly container: DependencyContainer;
    readonly database: TestDatabase;
    readonly osuStub: StubOsu;
    readonly osuState: FakeOsuState;
    readonly beatmapDirectory: string;
}

/**
 * The real app over PGlite, a temporary beatmap directory, and a fake osu! server serving `osuState` (default: the
 * golden state). Keys are loaded.
 */
export async function startTestApp(
    options: { config?: Partial<Config>; osuState?: FakeOsuState } = {},
): Promise<TestApp> {
    const resources = new AsyncDisposableStack();
    const database = await createTestDatabase();

    // Tests may close the database early to simulate an outage; closing it twice must not fail the test.
    resources.defer(() =>
        database[Symbol.asyncDispose]().catch(() => undefined),
    );
    const osuStub = resources.use(await startStubOsu());
    const osuState = options.osuState ?? createGoldenOsuState();
    const beatmapDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "beatmaps-"));

    resources.defer(() => rm(beatmapDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true }));
    osuStub.handle(createFakeOsuHandler(osuState));

    const { container } = await createTestContainer({
        db: database.db,
        config: {
            ...testConfig,
            osu: {
                apiKey: "test-osu-key",
                apiBaseUrl: `${osuStub.url}/api`,
                fileBaseUrl: `${osuStub.url}/osu`,
            },
            beatmapFileDirectory: beatmapDirectory,
            ...options.config,
        },
    });

    await container.resolve(dependencyTokens.keyService).load();

    return {
        app: createApp(container),
        container,
        database,
        osuStub,
        osuState,
        beatmapDirectory,
        [Symbol.asyncDispose]: () => resources.disposeAsync(),
    };
}
```

```ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import type { GoldenCase, GoldenResponse } from "./cases";

export const recordedResponses = JSON.parse(
    await readFile(
        fileURLToPath(new URL("./responses.json", import.meta.url)),
        "utf8",
    ),
) as GoldenResponse[];

const json = { "content-type": "application/json; charset=utf-8" };

const error = (status: number, message: string) => ({
    status,
    headers: json,
    body: { error: message },
});

function findRecordedResponse(name: string): GoldenResponse {
    const response = recordedResponses.find((r) => r.name === name);

    if (!response) {
        throw new Error(`No recorded response named ${name}`);
    }

    return response;
}

/**
 * What the rewrite answers instead, for the cases with a `changeReason`.
 */
const changedAnswers: Record<string, () => Omit<GoldenResponse, "name">> = {
    "getbeatmapfile not found": () => error(404, "Beatmap not found"),
    "getbeatmapfile of a taiko beatmap": () => error(404, "Beatmap not found"),
    "getbeatmap without parameters": () => error(400, "Missing id or hash"),
    "getbeatmapfile without parameters": () => error(400, "Missing id or hash"),
    "getbeatmaps without parameters": () => error(400, "Missing id"),
    "no key": () => {
        const { status, headers, body } = findRecordedResponse("getbeatmap by id");

        return { status, headers, body };
    },
};

/**
 * The response the rewrite must give: the recorded one, or the listed change.
 */
export function buildExpectedResponse(golden: GoldenCase): Omit<GoldenResponse, "name"> {
    if (golden.changeReason) {
        const answer = changedAnswers[golden.name];

        if (!answer) {
            throw new Error(`No expected answer for the changed case ${golden.name}`);
        }

        return answer();
    }

    const { status, headers, body } = findRecordedResponse(golden.name);

    return { status, headers, body };
}

/**
 * The parts of a response the contract compares.
 */
export function normalizeResponse(
    status: number,
    header: (name: string) => string | undefined,
    text: string,
): Omit<GoldenResponse, "name"> {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = {};

    for (const name of ["content-type", "content-disposition", "retry-after"]) {
        const value = header(name);

        if (value !== undefined) {
            headers[name] = value;
        }
    }

    return {
        status,
        headers,
        body: headers["content-type"]?.startsWith("application/json")
            ? (JSON.parse(text) as unknown)
            : text,
    };
}
```

`tests/integration/contract.test.ts`:

```ts
import { bulkKey, goldenCases, priorityKey } from "@test/golden/cases";
import {
    normalizeResponse,
    buildExpectedResponse,
    recordedResponses,
} from "@test/golden/expected";
import { startTestApp } from "@test/utils/app";
import request from "supertest";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("the HTTP contract", () => {
    it("answers every recorded request as the pre-rewrite service did, except the listed changes", async () => {
        await using test = await startTestApp({
            config: { builtInKeys: { priority: priorityKey, bulk: bulkKey } },
        });

        expect(recordedResponses.map((r) => r.name)).toEqual(
            goldenCases.map((c) => c.name),
        );

        for (const golden of goldenCases) {
            test.osuState.rateLimited = golden.rateLimited ?? false;

            const response = await request(test.app).get(golden.path);

            expect(
                normalizeResponse(
                    response.status,
                    (name) => response.headers[name] as string | undefined,
                    response.text,
                ),
                golden.name,
            ).toEqual(buildExpectedResponse(golden));
        }
    });
});
```

```ts
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";

import { createApp } from "@/app";

describe("the app", () => {

                order.push("first");
                next();

            (_req, res) => {

                res.status(503).json({ error: "Shutting down" });

        vi.spyOn(console, "warn").mockImplementation(() => undefined);

        const keys = new KeyRepository(test.database.db);

            lane: "bulk",
            rateLimitPerMinute: 1,

        await request(test.app)

            .get(`/api/beatmap/getbeatmapfile?id=78&${key}`)

        expect(response.text).toBe(file.toString());

        const beatmaps = test.container.resolve(dependencyTokens.beatmapRepository);

        await vi.waitFor(async () =>
            expect((await beatmaps.findById(78))?.maxCombo).toBe(2),

        const again = await request(test.app)
            .get(`/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=78&${key}`)
            .expect(200);

        expect(again.body).toMatchObject({ max_combo: "2" });
        // Metadata once, file once; the second request is served from the caches.
        expect(test.osuStub.requests).toHaveLength(2);
    });

    it("answers 500 without details for an unexpected error", async () => {
        const log = silenceConsoleErrors();
        await using test = await startTestApp();

        vi.spyOn(
            test.container.resolve(dependencyTokens.beatmapService),

            .get(`/api/beatmap/getbeatmap?id=75&${key}`)
            .expect(500, { error: "Internal server error" });
        expect(log).toHaveBeenCalled();
    });
});
```

`src/controllers/BeatmapController.ts`:

```ts
import { getCallerLane } from "@/access/types";
import { convertRowToApiResponse } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { parseBeatmapReference, parseBeatmapsetId } from "@/beatmaps/validation";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { getCaller } from "@/http/requestContext";
import { OsuUnavailableError } from "@/osu/errors";
import { BaseController, Controller, Get } from "@/server";
import type { IBeatmapFileService } from "@/services/IBeatmapFileService";
import type { IBeatmapService } from "@/services/IBeatmapService";
import type { IBeatmapsetService } from "@/services/IBeatmapsetService";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import type { Request, Response } from "express";
import { inject } from "tsyringe";

type ErrorBody = { error: string };

/**
 * The pre-rewrite routes, mounted at `/api/beatmap`. A `404` with `{ error }` means only that osu! has no such
 * beatmap, set, or file: the dpp processors rely on it.
 */
@Controller("")
export class BeatmapController extends BaseController {
    constructor(
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapService)
        private readonly beatmapService: Pick<IBeatmapService, "getBeatmap">,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapsetService)
        private readonly beatmapsetService: IBeatmapsetService,
        @inject(dependencyTokens.beatmapFileService)
        private readonly beatmapFileService: IBeatmapFileService,
    ) {
        super();
    }

    @Get("/getbeatmap")
    async getBeatmap(
        req: Request,
        res: Response<OsuAPIResponse | ErrorBody>,
    ): Promise<void> {
        try {
            const beatmap = await this.beatmapService.getBeatmap(
                getCallerLane(getCaller(res)),
                parseBeatmapReference(req.query),
            );

            if (!beatmap) {
                res.status(404).json({ error: "Beatmap not found" });

                return;
            }

            res.json(convertRowToApiResponse(beatmap));
        } catch (e) {
            this.handleError(res, e);
        }
    }

    @Get("/getbeatmapfile")
    async getBeatmapFile(req: Request, res: Response): Promise<void> {
        try {
            const lane = getCallerLane(getCaller(res));
            // Metadata first, so that every served file is checked against its MD5 and is osu!standard.
            const beatmap = await this.beatmapService.getBeatmap(
                lane,
                parseBeatmapReference(req.query),
            );

            if (!beatmap) {
                res.status(404).json({ error: "Beatmap not found" });

                return;
            }

            const file = await this.beatmapFileService.getBeatmapFile(lane, beatmap);

            if (!file) {
                res.status(404).json({ error: "Beatmap file not found" });

                return;
            }

            res.setHeader(
                "Content-Disposition",
                `attachment; filename=${beatmap.beatmapId.toString()}.osu`,
            );
            res.setHeader("Content-Type", "text/plain");
            res.end(file);

            this.beatmapFileService.backfillMaxCombo(beatmap, file);
        } catch (e) {
            this.handleError(res, e);
        }
    }

    @Get("/getbeatmaps")
    async getBeatmaps(
        req: Request,
        res: Response<OsuAPIResponse[] | ErrorBody>,
    ): Promise<void> {
        try {
            const set = await this.beatmapsetService.getBeatmapset(
                getCallerLane(getCaller(res)),
                parseBeatmapsetId(req.query),
            );

            if (!set) {
                res.status(404).json({ error: "Beatmapset not found" });

                return;
            }

            res.json(set.map(convertRowToApiResponse));
        } catch (e) {
            this.handleError(res, e);
        }
    }

    protected override handleError(res: Response, error: unknown): void {
        if (error instanceof OsuUnavailableError) {
            res.status(503)
                .set("Retry-After", error.retryAfterSeconds.toString())
                .json({ error: error.message });

            return;
        }

        super.handleError(res, error);
    }
}
```

`src/controllers/health.ts`:

```ts
import { ServiceUnavailableError } from "@/server";
import type { RequestHandler } from "express";

/**
 * `GET /health`: `200 { status: "ok" }` when the database answers, otherwise `503`. Needs no key and is not rate
 * limited, so PM2 and monitoring can call it.
 */
export function createHealthHandler(
    ping: () => Promise<unknown>,
): RequestHandler {
    return async (_req, res) => {
        try {
            await ping();
            res.json({ status: "ok" });
        } catch {
            const error = new ServiceUnavailableError("Database unavailable", 5);

            res.status(error.statusCode).set(error.headers).json(error.toResponse());
        }
    };
}
```

```ts
import { createHealthHandler } from "@/controllers/health";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { createAuthMiddleware } from "@/middleware/auth";
import { createRateLimitMiddleware } from "@/middleware/rateLimit";
import { createRouter, errorHandler, getContainer } from "@/server";
import cors from "cors";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import express, { Router, type Express, type RequestHandler } from "express";
// Load controllers so their metadata is registered before the router is built.
import "./controllers";

/**
 * Creates the Express application. Everything lives under `/api/beatmap`. `/health` comes before authentication;
 * every other request is authenticated and rate limited first. Unknown routes fall through to Express's default
 * HTML 404, never a JSON `{ error }` 404 (see `errorHandlers.ts`).
 *
 * @param earlyHandlers Middleware to run before everything else, in order, such as `GracefulServer.middleware`.
 */
export function createApp(
    container = getContainer(),
    ...earlyHandlers: RequestHandler[]
): Express {
    const config = container.resolve(dependencyTokens.config);
    const keys = container.resolve(dependencyTokens.keyService);
    const db = container.resolve(dependencyTokens.db);

    const api = Router()
        .get(
            "/health",
            createHealthHandler(() => db.execute(sql`select 1`)),
        )
        .use(
            createAuthMiddleware(keys, {
                // ANON_RATE_LIMIT=0 turns anonymous access off.
                allowAnonymous: config.anonymousRateLimit > 0,
            }),
        )
        .use(
            createRateLimitMiddleware({
                anonymousLimit: config.anonymousRateLimit,
            }),
        )
        .use(createRouter(container));

    const app = express()
        .disable("x-powered-by")
        .set("trust proxy", config.trustProxy);

    // Express rejects `use()` with no handlers.
    if (earlyHandlers.length > 0) {
        app.use(...earlyHandlers);
    }

    return app
        .use(
            cors({
                exposedHeaders: [
                    "Content-Disposition",
                    "RateLimit",
                    "RateLimit-Policy",
                    "Retry-After",
                ],
            }),
        )
        .use("/api/beatmap", api)
        .use(errorHandler);
}
```

```ts
import { createFakeOsuHandler } from "@test/fixtures/osu/fakeOsu";
import { createGoldenOsuState } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { startStubOsu } from "@test/utils/stubOsu";
import { findFreePort, startProcess, startTestPostgres } from "@/server/testing";
import { mkdtemp, readFile, rm } from "node:fs/promises";

import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import pg from "pg";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const script = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../dist/index.mjs", import.meta.url));
const bulkKey = "e2e-bulk-key";

async function buildEnvironment(postgresPort: number, osuUrl: string, directory: string) {
    const port = await findFreePort();

    return {
        port,
        env: {
            PATH: process.env.PATH,
            // Skips `.env.*` files: the test passes every variable.
            CI: "1",
            EXPRESS_PORT: port.toString(),
            POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: "127.0.0.1",
            POSTGRES_PORT: postgresPort.toString(),
            POSTGRES_DB_NAME: "postgres",
            POSTGRES_USERNAME: "postgres",
            OSU_API_KEY: "e2e-osu-key",
            INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: "e2e-priority-key",
            BULK_SERVER_KEY: bulkKey,
            OSU_API_BASE_URL: `${osuUrl}/api`,
            OSU_FILE_BASE_URL: `${osuUrl}/osu`,
            BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY: directory,
        },
    };
}

describe("dist/index.mjs", () => {
    it("serves the dpp processors' contract and shuts down cleanly", async () => {
        await using postgres = await startTestPostgres();
        await using osu = await startStubOsu();
        const state = createGoldenOsuState();
        const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "e2e-beatmaps-"));

        osu.handle(createFakeOsuHandler(state));

        try {
            const { port, env } = await buildEnvironment(postgres.port, osu.url, directory);
            const base = `http://127.0.0.1:${port.toString()}/api/beatmap`;
            await using server = await startProcess({
                script,
                env,
                readyUrl: `${base}/health`,
            });

            const beatmap = await fetch(`${base}/getbeatmap?id=75&key=${bulkKey}`);

            expect(beatmap.status).toBe(200);
            expect(await beatmap.json()).toMatchObject({
                beatmap_id: "75",
                file_md5: state.beatmaps[0]?.file_md5,
            });

            const missing = await fetch(`${base}/getbeatmap?id=999&key=${bulkKey}`);

            expect(missing.status).toBe(404);
            expect(await missing.json()).toEqual({ error: "Beatmap not found" });

            const file = await fetch(`${base}/getbeatmapfile?id=75&key=${bulkKey}`);

            expect(await file.text()).toBe(state.files.get(75)?.toString());
            expect(await readFile(join(directory, "75.osu"))).toEqual(state.files.get(75));

            state.rateLimited = true;

            const limited = await fetch(`${base}/getbeatmap?id=77&key=${bulkKey}`);

            expect(limited.status).toBe(503);
            expect(limited.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("30");

            server.child.kill("SIGTERM");

            expect(await server.exited).toBe(0);
            expect(server.output()).toContain("[beatmap-service]");
        } finally {
            await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
        }
    });

    it("refuses to start on a pre-rewrite database that was never baselined", async () => {
        await using postgres = await startTestPostgres();
        await using osu = await startStubOsu();

            ).rejects.toThrow(/pnpm db:baseline/);
        } finally {
            await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
        }
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement the entry point**

`src/index.ts`:

```ts
import "./polyfills";
import type { IKeyService } from "@/access/IKeyService";
import { createApp } from "@/app";
import { ConfigError, loadConfig } from "@/config/config";
import { assertMigrationHistory, BaselineError } from "@/database/baseline";
import { schema } from "@/database/schema";
import { registerDependencies } from "@/dependencies/register";
import { dependencyTokens } from "@/dependencies/tokens";
import { createOsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/createOsuGatewayOptions";
import {
    createShutdownHandler,
    DatabaseConnection,
    getContainer,
    GracefulServer,
    installLogPrefix,
    loadEnvironmentVariables,
} from "@/server";
import { sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

async function main(): Promise<void> {
    loadEnvironmentVariables();

    const config = loadConfig(process.env);

    // Kept for the process's whole lifetime, so never disposed.
    installLogPrefix("beatmap-service");

    // Installed first so a SIGHUP during startup does not hit Node's default action (exit); it is ignored until
    // the keys have loaded.
    let keys: IKeyService | undefined;

    process.on("SIGHUP", () => {
        const loaded = keys;

        if (!loaded) {
            return;
        }

        void loaded.reload().then((reloaded) => {
            if (reloaded) {
                console.log(`Reloaded API keys: ${loaded.describeKeys()}`);
            }
        });
    });

    // Released in reverse order on shutdown: server, key reloading, then database.
    const resources = new AsyncDisposableStack();
    const container = getContainer();

    try {
        const database = resources.use(
            new DatabaseConnection(config.database, schema),
        );

        await database.db.execute(sql`select 1`);
        await assertMigrationHistory(database.db);
        await database.migrate(
            fileURLToPath(new URL("./drizzle", import.meta.url)),
        );

        registerDependencies(container, {
            config,
            db: database.db,
            osuGatewayOptions: createOsuGatewayOptions(config),
        });

        const keyService = container.resolve(dependencyTokens.keyService);

        await keyService.load();
        keys = keyService;
        console.log(`Loaded API keys: ${keyService.describeKeys()}`);
        resources.use(keyService.startReloading());

        const server = resources.use(new GracefulServer());
        const port = await server.listen(
            createApp(container, server.middleware),
            config.port,
        );

        console.log(`Listening on port ${port.toString()}`);
    } catch (e) {
        await resources.disposeAsync().catch((disposeError: unknown) => {
            console.error(
                "Failed to release resources after a startup failure:",
                disposeError,
            );
        });

        throw e;
    }

    const shutdown = createShutdownHandler({
        resources,
        // In-flight requests may wait for the osu! budget and then for osu! itself (10 s timeout).
        timeoutMs:
            Math.max(config.maxWaitMs.priority, config.maxWaitMs.bulk) + 15_000,
    });

    process.once("SIGINT", shutdown);
    process.once("SIGTERM", shutdown);
}

main().catch((e: unknown) => {
    console.error(
        "Startup failed:",
        e instanceof ConfigError || e instanceof BaselineError ? e.message : e,
    );
    process.exit(1);
});
```

```ts
// src/cli/types.ts
export interface CliOutput { info(line: string): void; error(line: string): void; }
export interface CliDependencies {
    readonly output: CliOutput;
    readonly db: Database;
    readonly migrationsFolder: string;
    /** The full configuration; only commands that call osu! or write files read it. @throws {ConfigError} */
    readonly loadConfig: () => Config;
    /** A gateway with its own rate limiter. Call it once per command. */
    readonly createOsuGateway: (config: Config) => Pick<IOsuGateway, "getBeatmapsSince">;
    readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
}
export type CommandAction = (deps: CliDependencies) => Promise<void>;
/** Checks a command's arguments without touching anything. @throws {CliError} */
export type Command = (args: string[]) => CommandAction;
export interface CommandDefinition {
    readonly buildAction: Command;
    /** Apply pending migrations before running (every command but `db-baseline`). */
    readonly requiresMigrations: boolean;
}

// src/cli/run.ts
export const commands: Map<string, CommandDefinition>;
export const usage: string;
export function parseCli(argv: readonly string[], output: CliOutput): { action: CommandAction; requiresMigrations: boolean } | undefined;
export function runAction(action: CommandAction, deps: CliDependencies): Promise<number>; // 0, or 1 for a CliError
export function runCli(argv: readonly string[], deps: CliDependencies): Promise<number>;

// src/cli/arguments.ts
export function parseArguments(args, options) /* util.parseArgs, strict, positionals allowed; errors → CliError */;
export function expectPositionals(positionals: string[], names: readonly string[]): string[];
export function requireValidName(name: string): void;
export function parseLimit(limit: string | undefined, unlimited: boolean): number | null | undefined;
export function parseLane(lane: string | undefined): Lane | undefined;

// src/cli/table.ts

// src/cli/output.ts
export const consoleOutput: CliOutput;
export const reloadReminder: string;

// tests/utils/cli.ts
export interface CapturedOutput extends CliOutput { readonly lines: string[]; readonly errors: string[]; }
export function captureOutput(): CapturedOutput;
export function createCliDependencies(db: Database, overrides?: Partial<CliDependencies>): CliDependencies & { output: CapturedOutput };
```

```ts
import type { CliDependencies, CliOutput } from "@/cli/types";
import type { Database } from "@/database/types";
import { testConfig } from "@test/utils/config";
import { migrationsFolder } from "@test/utils/database";

export interface CapturedOutput extends CliOutput {
    readonly lines: string[];
    readonly errors: string[];
}

export function captureOutput(): CapturedOutput {
    const lines: string[] = [];
    const errors: string[] = [];

    return {
        lines,
        errors,
        info: (line) => lines.push(line),
        error: (line) => errors.push(line),
    };
}

/**
 * CLI dependencies over a test database. osu! is unreachable unless `createOsuGateway` is overridden, and sleeping
 * returns at once.
 */
export function createCliDependencies(
    db: Database,
    overrides: Partial<CliDependencies> = {},
): CliDependencies & { output: CapturedOutput } {
    return {
        db,
        migrationsFolder,
        loadConfig: () => testConfig,
        createOsuGateway: () => {
            throw new Error("This test does not use osu!");
        },
        sleep: () => Promise.resolve(),
        ...overrides,
        output: captureOutput(),
    };
}
```

```ts
import {
    expectPositionals,
    parseArguments,
    parseLane,
    parseLimit,
    requireValidName,
} from "@/cli/arguments";
import { CliError } from "@/cli/errors";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("CLI arguments", () => {
    it("parses options strictly", () => {
        expect(
            parseArguments(["site", "--limit", "5"], { limit: { type: "string" } }),
        ).toMatchObject({ values: { limit: "5" }, positionals: ["site"] });
        expect(() => parseArguments(["--nope"], {})).toThrow(CliError);
    });

    it("checks positionals", () => {
        expect(expectPositionals(["a"], ["name"])).toEqual(["a"]);
        expect(() => expectPositionals([], ["name"])).toThrow("Expected <name>");
        expect(() => expectPositionals(["a"], [])).toThrow("Expected no arguments");
    });

    it("checks names", () => {
        expect(() => requireValidName("site")).not.toThrow();
        expect(() => requireValidName("Site")).toThrow(/Invalid name "Site"/);
    });

    it("reads limits", () => {
        expect(parseLimit("120", false)).toBe(120);
        expect(parseLimit(undefined, true)).toBeNull();
        expect(parseLimit(undefined, false)).toBeUndefined();
        expect(() => parseLimit("0", false)).toThrow(CliError);
        expect(() => parseLimit("1.5", false)).toThrow(CliError);
        expect(() => parseLimit("5", true)).toThrow(

});
```

```ts
import { hashApiKey } from "@/access/keys";
import { runCli } from "@/cli/run";
import { KeyRepository } from "@/repositories/KeyRepository";
import { createCliDependencies } from "@test/utils/cli";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@test/utils/database";

describe("key commands", () => {

        expect(
            await runCli(["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "bulk", "--limit", "120"], deps),
        ).toBe(0);

        const key = printed?.slice("Key: ".length) ?? "";

        expect(key).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
        expect(deps.output.lines).toContain("Store it now; it cannot be shown again.");
        expect(await new KeyRepository(database.db).listActive()).toEqual([
            { name: "site", lane: "bulk", rateLimitPerMinute: 120, keyHash: hashApiKey(key) },
        ]);
    });

    it.each([
        [["key", "create", "Site", "--lane", "bulk", "--unlimited"], /Invalid name "Site"/],
        [["key", "create", "site", "--unlimited"], /Pass --lane priority or --lane bulk/],
        [["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "fast", "--unlimited"], /Invalid lane "fast"/],
        [["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "bulk"], /Pass --limit <per-minute> or --unlimited/],
        [["key", "update", "site"], /Nothing to update/],
        [["key", "nope"], /Usage:/],
    ])("rejects %j", async (argv, message) => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const deps = createCliDependencies(database.db);

        expect(await runCli(argv, deps)).toBe(1);
        expect(deps.output.errors.join("\n")).toMatch(message);
    });

    it("refuses a duplicate name", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const deps = createCliDependencies(database.db);

        await runCli(["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "bulk", "--unlimited"], deps);

        expect(
            await runCli(["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "bulk", "--unlimited"], deps),
        ).toBe(1);
        expect(deps.output.errors).toContain('A key named "site" already exists');
    });

    it("updates, shows, lists, and revokes keys", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const deps = createCliDependencies(database.db);

        await runCli(["key", "create", "site", "--lane", "bulk", "--limit", "60"], deps);
        await runCli(["key", "create", "bot", "--lane", "priority", "--unlimited"], deps);

        expect(await runCli(["key", "update", "site", "--lane", "priority", "--unlimited"], deps)).toBe(0);

        deps.output.lines.length = 0;
        await runCli(["key", "show", "site"], deps);

        expect(deps.output.lines.slice(0, 3)).toEqual([
            "Name:     site",
            "Lane:     priority",
            "Limit:    unlimited",
        ]);

        expect(await runCli(["key", "revoke", "site"], deps)).toBe(0);
        expect(await runCli(["key", "revoke", "site"], deps)).toBe(1);
        expect(deps.output.errors).toContain("Key site is already revoked");
        expect(await runCli(["key", "update", "site", "--unlimited"], deps)).toBe(1);

        deps.output.lines.length = 0;
        await runCli(["key", "list"], deps);

        expect(deps.output.lines[0]).toMatch(/^NAME\s+LANE\s+LIMIT\s+CREATED\s+REVOKED$/);
        expect(deps.output.lines[1]).toMatch(/^bot\s+priority\s+unlimited\s+\S+\s+-$/);
        expect(deps.output.lines[2]).toMatch(/^site\s+priority\s+unlimited\s+\S+\s+\d{4}-/);
    });
});
```

`src/cli/arguments.ts`:

```ts
import { isValidKeyName } from "@/access/names";
import type { Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import { parseArgs, type ParseArgsOptionsConfig } from "node:util";
import { CliError } from "./errors";

/**
 * Parses a command's arguments with `util.parseArgs`: unknown options are errors, and positionals are allowed.
 * Argument errors (unknown option, missing value) become {@link CliError}s.
 */
export function parseArguments<const T extends ParseArgsOptionsConfig>(
    args: string[],
    options: T,
) {
    try {
        return parseArgs<{
            args: string[];
            options: T;
            allowPositionals: true;
            strict: true;
        }>({ args, options, allowPositionals: true, strict: true });
    } catch (e) {
        if (
            e instanceof TypeError &&
            "code" in e &&
            typeof e.code === "string" &&
            e.code.startsWith("ERR_PARSE_ARGS")
        ) {
            throw new CliError(e.message);
        }

        throw e;
    }
}

/**
 * Checks that exactly the named positional arguments were given.
 */
export function expectPositionals(
    positionals: string[],
    names: readonly string[],
): string[] {
    if (positionals.length !== names.length) {
        throw new CliError(
            names.length === 0
                ? "Expected no arguments"
                : `Expected ${names.map((name) => `<${name}>`).join(" ")}`,
        );
    }

    return positionals;
}

export function requireValidName(name: string): void {
    if (!isValidKeyName(name)) {
        throw new CliError(
            `Invalid name "${name}": use lowercase letters, digits, and hyphens, not starting with a hyphen`,
        );
    }
}

/**
 * Reads `--limit`/`--unlimited`: a positive whole number, `null` for unlimited, or `undefined` if neither was given.
 */
export function parseLimit(
    limit: string | undefined,
    unlimited: boolean,
): number | null | undefined {
    if (limit !== undefined && unlimited) {
        throw new CliError("Pass only one of --limit or --unlimited");
    }

    if (unlimited) {
        return null;
    }

    if (limit === undefined) {
        return undefined;
    }

    const value = Number(limit);

    if (!Number.isInteger(value) || value < 1) {
        throw new CliError(
            `Invalid limit "${limit}": expected a whole number of requests per minute`,
        );
    }

    return value;
}

/**
 * Reads `--lane`: `priority`, `bulk`, or `undefined` if it was not given.
 */
export function parseLane(lane: string | undefined): Lane | undefined {
    if (lane === undefined || lane === "priority" || lane === "bulk") {
        return lane;
    }

    throw new CliError(`Invalid lane "${lane}": use priority or bulk`);
}
```
