`src/cli/commands/key.ts`:

```ts
import { generateApiKey, hashApiKey } from "@/access/keys";
import type { KeyListing } from "@/access/types";
import {
    expectPositionals,
    parseArguments,
    parseLane,
    parseLimit,
    requireValidName,
} from "@/cli/arguments";
import { CliError } from "@/cli/errors";
import { reloadReminder } from "@/cli/output";
import { formatTable } from "@/cli/table";
import type { Command } from "@/cli/types";
import type { Lane } from "@/osu/errors";
import { KeyRepository } from "@/repositories/KeyRepository";

async function requireActiveKey(
    repository: KeyRepository,
    name: string,
): Promise<KeyListing> {
    const key = await repository.findByName(name);

    if (!key) {
        throw new CliError(`No key named "${name}"`);
    }

    if (key.revokedAt) {
        throw new CliError(`Key ${name} is revoked`);
    }

    return key;
}

function describeLimit(key: Pick<KeyListing, "rateLimitPerMinute">): string {
    return key.rateLimitPerMinute === null
        ? "unlimited"
        : `${key.rateLimitPerMinute.toString()}/min`;
}

export const createKey: Command = (args) => {
    const { values, positionals } = parseArguments(args, {
        lane: { type: "string" },
        limit: { type: "string" },
        unlimited: { type: "boolean", default: false },
    });
    const [name = ""] = expectPositionals(positionals, ["name"]);

    requireValidName(name);

    const lane = parseLane(values.lane);

    if (lane === undefined) {
        throw new CliError("Pass --lane priority or --lane bulk");
    }

    const rateLimitPerMinute = parseLimit(values.limit, values.unlimited);

    if (rateLimitPerMinute === undefined) {
        throw new CliError("Pass --limit <per-minute> or --unlimited");
    }

    return async ({ db, output }) => {
        const repository = new KeyRepository(db);

        if (await repository.findByName(name)) {
            throw new CliError(`A key named "${name}" already exists`);
        }

        const key = generateApiKey();

        await repository.insert({
            name,
            keyHash: hashApiKey(key),
            lane,
            rateLimitPerMinute,
        });

        output.info(
            `Created key ${name} (${lane} lane, ${describeLimit({ rateLimitPerMinute })}).`,
        );
        output.info(`Key: ${key}`);
        output.info("Store it now; it cannot be shown again.");
        output.info(reloadReminder);
    };
};

export const updateKey: Command = (args) => {
    const { values, positionals } = parseArguments(args, {
        lane: { type: "string" },
        limit: { type: "string" },
        unlimited: { type: "boolean", default: false },
    });
    const [name = ""] = expectPositionals(positionals, ["name"]);
    const changes: { lane?: Lane; rateLimitPerMinute?: number | null } = {};
    const lane = parseLane(values.lane);
    const rateLimitPerMinute = parseLimit(values.limit, values.unlimited);

    if (lane !== undefined) {
        changes.lane = lane;
    }

    if (rateLimitPerMinute !== undefined) {
        changes.rateLimitPerMinute = rateLimitPerMinute;
    }

    if (Object.keys(changes).length === 0) {
        throw new CliError(
            "Nothing to update; pass --lane, --limit, or --unlimited",
        );
    }

    return async ({ db, output }) => {
        const repository = new KeyRepository(db);

        await requireActiveKey(repository, name);
        await repository.update(name, changes);

        output.info(`Updated key ${name}.`);
        output.info(reloadReminder);
    };
};

export const revokeKey: Command = (args) => {
    const { positionals } = parseArguments(args, {});
    const [name = ""] = expectPositionals(positionals, ["name"]);

    return async ({ db, output }) => {
        const repository = new KeyRepository(db);

        if (!(await repository.findByName(name))) {
            throw new CliError(`No key named "${name}"`);
        }

        if (!(await repository.revoke(name))) {
            throw new CliError(`Key ${name} is already revoked`);
        }

        output.info(`Revoked key ${name}.`);
        output.info(reloadReminder);
    };
};

export const listKeys: Command = (args) => {
    const { positionals } = parseArguments(args, {});

    expectPositionals(positionals, []);

    return async ({ db, output }) => {
        const keys = await new KeyRepository(db).list();

        if (keys.length === 0) {
            output.info("No keys.");

            return;
        }

        for (const line of formatTable(
            ["NAME", "LANE", "LIMIT", "CREATED", "REVOKED"],
            keys.map((key) => [
                key.name,
                key.lane,
                describeLimit(key),
                key.createdAt.toISOString(),
                key.revokedAt?.toISOString() ?? "-",
            ]),
        )) {
            output.info(line);
        }
    };
};

export const showKey: Command = (args) => {
    const { positionals } = parseArguments(args, {});
    const [name = ""] = expectPositionals(positionals, ["name"]);

    return async ({ db, output }) => {
        const key = await new KeyRepository(db).findByName(name);

        if (!key) {
            throw new CliError(`No key named "${name}"`);
        }

        output.info(`Name:     ${key.name}`);
        output.info(`Lane:     ${key.lane}`);
        output.info(`Limit:    ${describeLimit(key)}`);
        output.info(`Created:  ${key.createdAt.toISOString()}`);
        output.info(`Revoked:  ${key.revokedAt?.toISOString() ?? "-"}`);
    };
};
```

`src/cli/commands/dbBaseline.ts`:

```ts
import { expectPositionals, parseArguments } from "@/cli/arguments";
import { CliError } from "@/cli/errors";
import type { Command } from "@/cli/types";
import { BaselineError, baselineDatabase } from "@/database/baseline";

/**
 * Records the pre-rewrite schema as migrated, once, before the rewrite first starts against an existing database.
 */
export const dbBaseline: Command = (args) => {
    const { positionals } = parseArguments(args, {});

    expectPositionals(positionals, []);

    return async ({ db, migrationsFolder, output }) => {
        try {
            await baselineDatabase(db, migrationsFolder);
        } catch (e) {
            if (e instanceof BaselineError) {
                throw new CliError(e.message);
            }

            throw e;
        }

        output.info(
            "Recorded the baseline migration as applied. Start the service (or run any other command) to apply the remaining migrations.",
        );
    };
};
```

`src/cli/run.ts`:

```ts
import { dbBaseline } from "./commands/dbBaseline";
import {
    createKey,
    listKeys,
    revokeKey,
    showKey,
    updateKey,
} from "./commands/key";
import { CliError } from "./errors";
import type {
    CliDependencies,
    CliOutput,
    CommandAction,
    CommandDefinition,
} from "./types";

export const commands = new Map<string, CommandDefinition>([
    ["key create", { buildAction: createKey, requiresMigrations: true }],
    ["key update", { buildAction: updateKey, requiresMigrations: true }],
    ["key revoke", { buildAction: revokeKey, requiresMigrations: true }],
    ["key list", { buildAction: listKeys, requiresMigrations: true }],
    ["key show", { buildAction: showKey, requiresMigrations: true }],
    // Runs before any migration: it is what lets migrations run on a pre-rewrite database.
    ["db-baseline", { buildAction: dbBaseline, requiresMigrations: false }],
]);

export const usage = `Usage: node dist/cli.mjs <command>

  key create <name> --lane <priority|bulk> (--limit <per-minute> | --unlimited)
  key update <name> [--lane <priority|bulk>] [--limit <per-minute> | --unlimited]
  key revoke <name>
  key list
  key show <name>

  db-baseline
  populate-cache [--files <dump directory>] [--move] [--restart]
  fix-dates [--restart]`;

/**
 * Checks the command name and its arguments. Needs no configuration or database, so a mistyped command reports usage
 * before anything is opened.
 *
 * @returns The action to run, or `undefined` after printing usage or the argument error.
 */
export function parseCli(
    argv: readonly string[],
    output: CliOutput,
): { readonly action: CommandAction; readonly requiresMigrations: boolean } | undefined {
    const [first = "", second = ""] = argv;
    const twoWordCommand = commands.get(`${first} ${second}`);
    const command = twoWordCommand ?? commands.get(first);

    if (!command) {
        output.error(usage);

        return undefined;
    }

    try {
        return {
            action: command.buildAction(argv.slice(twoWordCommand ? 2 : 1)),
            requiresMigrations: command.requiresMigrations,
        };
    } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof CliError) {
            output.error(e.message);

            return undefined;
        }

        throw e;
    }
}

/**
 * Runs a parsed command.
 *
 * @returns The exit code: 0 on success, 1 for a {@link CliError}.
 */
export async function runAction(
    action: CommandAction,
    deps: CliDependencies,
): Promise<number> {
    try {
        await action(deps);

        return 0;
    } catch (e) {
        if (e instanceof CliError) {
            deps.output.error(e.message);

            return 1;
        }

        throw e;
    }
}

/**
 * Parses and runs one CLI command, without migrating (tests pass migrated databases).
 *
 * @returns The exit code: 0 on success, 1 for an unknown command or a {@link CliError}.
 */
export async function runCli(
    argv: readonly string[],
    deps: CliDependencies,
): Promise<number> {
    const parsed = parseCli(argv, deps.output);

    return parsed ? await runAction(parsed.action, deps) : 1;
}
```

`src/cli.ts` (replaces the placeholder):

```ts
import "./polyfills";
import { consoleOutput } from "@/cli/output";
import { parseCli, runAction } from "@/cli/run";
import { ConfigError, loadConfig, loadDatabaseConfig } from "@/config/config";
import { assertMigrationHistory, BaselineError } from "@/database/baseline";
import { schema } from "@/database/schema";
import { createOsuGatewayOptions } from "@/osu/createOsuGatewayOptions";
import { OsuGateway } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import { DatabaseConnection, loadEnvironmentVariables } from "@/server";
import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";

/**
 * `node dist/cli.mjs <command>`. The arguments are checked before any configuration is read or the database is
 * opened. Every command but `db-baseline` applies pending migrations first, so it works before the service has ever
 * started.
 */
async function main(): Promise<number> {
    const parsed = parseCli(process.argv.slice(2), consoleOutput);

    if (!parsed) {
        return 1;
    }

    loadEnvironmentVariables(true);

    await using database = new DatabaseConnection(
        loadDatabaseConfig(process.env),
        schema,
    );
    const migrationsFolder = fileURLToPath(
        new URL("./drizzle", import.meta.url),
    );

    if (parsed.requiresMigrations) {
        await assertMigrationHistory(database.db);
        await database.migrate(migrationsFolder);
    }

    // Awaited so the connection stays open until the command finishes.
    return await runAction(parsed.action, {
        output: consoleOutput,
        db: database.db,
        migrationsFolder,
        loadConfig: () => loadConfig(process.env),
        createOsuGateway: (config) =>
            new OsuGateway(createOsuGatewayOptions(config)),
        sleep: (ms) => sleep(ms),
    });
}

main().then(
    (code) => {
        process.exitCode = code;
    },
    (e: unknown) => {
        console.error(
            e instanceof ConfigError || e instanceof BaselineError ? e.message : e,
        );
        process.exitCode = 1;
    },
);
```

```ts
export const walkStart: Date; // 2007-01-01T00:00:00Z
export interface SinceWalkDependencies {
    readonly osuGateway: Pick<IOsuGateway, "getBeatmapsSince">;
    readonly cursorRepository: ICursorRepository;
    readonly output: CliOutput;
    readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
}
export interface SinceWalkOptions {
    /** The cursor's name, e.g. "populate-cache". */
    readonly command: string;
    /** Start from `walkStart` instead of the saved cursor. */
    readonly shouldRestart: boolean;
    /** Handles one page; the cursor moves past the page only after this resolves. */
    readonly processPage: (page: readonly OsuAPIResponse[]) => Promise<void>;
}
/** @returns The number of pages and beatmaps handled. */
export function walkSince(deps: SinceWalkDependencies, options: SinceWalkOptions): Promise<{ pages: number; beatmaps: number }>;
/** One second before `lastApproved`, or, if that does not move past `cursor`, the later of `lastApproved` and `cursor` + 1 s. */
export function computeNextCursor(cursor: Date, lastApproved: Date): { next: Date; skipped: boolean };
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Check the live osu! API (3 requests)**

The walker and the file service rely on three facts about osu! that the fake cannot prove. `scripts/check-osu-api.ts`:

```ts
// Checks the osu! API v1 behaviour the rewrite relies on (Task 18 of the rewrite plan). Makes three requests to
// osu!. Run with: node --env-file=.env scripts/check-osu-api.ts
import { createHash } from "node:crypto";

interface Entry {
    readonly beatmap_id: string;
    readonly approved_date: string | null;
    readonly mode: string;
    readonly file_md5: string;
}

const key = process.env.OSU_API_KEY;

if (!key) {
    throw new Error("OSU_API_KEY is not set");
}

const api = "https://osu.ppy.sh/api/get_beatmaps";
const since = "2007-10-01 00:00:00";
const page = (await (
    await fetch(`${api}?k=${key}&since=${encodeURIComponent(since)}&m=0&limit=5`)
).json()) as Entry[];

console.log(
    "since page:",
    page.map((b) => `${b.beatmap_id} ${b.approved_date ?? "null"} mode ${b.mode}`),
);
console.log(
    "ordered by approval date:",
    page.every((b, i) => i === 0 || (page[i - 1]?.approved_date ?? "") <= (b.approved_date ?? "")),
);
console.log("all approved on or after since:", page.every((b) => (b.approved_date ?? "") >= since));
console.log("all osu!standard:", page.every((b) => b.mode === "0"));

const [meta] = (await (await fetch(`${api}?k=${key}&b=75`)).json()) as Entry[];
const file = Buffer.from(await (await fetch("https://osu.ppy.sh/osu/75")).arrayBuffer());
const hash = createHash("md5").update(file).digest("hex");

console.log(`beatmap 75: file_md5 ${meta?.file_md5 ?? "missing"}, downloaded ${hash}, match: ${String(meta?.file_md5 === hash)}`);
```

Run: `node --env-file=.env scripts/check-osu-api.ts` (the repository's `.env` holds the development `OSU_API_KEY`; if it is missing, ask the user for a key and pass it as `OSU_API_KEY=… node scripts/check-osu-api.ts`).

Expected: five beatmaps; `ordered by approval date: true`, `all approved on or after since: true`, `all osu!standard: true`, and `match: true`. If anything is `false` or the request fails, **stop and report it to the user** before continuing: the walker's paging (ordering, date format) or the file service's MD5 check (a mismatch would turn every download into a 503) would be wrong.

`tests/fakes/FakeCursorRepository.ts`:

```ts
import type { ICursorRepository } from "@/repositories/ICursorRepository";

/**
 * CLI cursorRepository in memory.
 */
export class FakeCursorRepository implements ICursorRepository {
    readonly cursorsByCommand = new Map<string, Date>();

    async getCursor(command: string): Promise<Date | null> {
        return this.cursorsByCommand.get(command) ?? null;
    }

    async setCursor(command: string, cursor: Date): Promise<void> {
        this.cursorsByCommand.set(command, cursor);
    }

    async clearCursor(command: string): Promise<void> {
        this.cursorsByCommand.delete(command);
    }
}
```

```ts
import { formatOsuDate } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { computeNextCursor, walkSince, walkStart } from "@/cli/SinceWalker";
import { OsuUnavailableError } from "@/osu/errors";
import { buildApiBeatmap } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { captureOutput } from "@test/utils/cli";
import { FakeCursorRepository } from "@test/fakes/FakeCursorRepository";
import { FakeOsuGateway } from "@test/fakes/FakeOsuGateway";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

const base = Date.parse("2010-01-01T00:00:00Z");

/**
 * `count` ranked beatmaps approved one minute apart (or all at once with `sameDate`).
 */
function buildRankedBeatmaps(count: number, sameDate = false): OsuAPIResponse[] {
    return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, i) =>
        buildApiBeatmap({
            beatmap_id: (i + 1).toString(),
            beatmapset_id: (i + 1).toString(),
            approved_date: formatOsuDate(new Date(base + (sameDate ? 0 : i * 60_000))),
            file_md5: (i + 1).toString(16).padStart(32, "0"),
        }),
    );
}

function setup(...responses: OsuAPIResponse[]) {
    const osuGateway = new FakeOsuGateway().addBeatmaps(...responses);
    const cursorRepository = new FakeCursorRepository();
    const output = captureOutput();
    const sleep = vi.fn((_ms: number) => Promise.resolve());

    return { osuGateway, cursorRepository, output, sleep, deps: { osuGateway, cursorRepository, output, sleep } };
}

describe("computeNextCursor", () => {
    const cursor = new Date("2010-01-01T00:00:00Z");

    it("steps back one second from the last approval date", () => {
        expect(computeNextCursor(cursor, new Date("2010-01-02T00:00:00Z"))).toEqual({
            next: new Date("2010-01-01T23:59:59Z"),
            skipped: false,
        });
    });

    it("always moves forward, reporting a possible skip", () => {
        expect(computeNextCursor(cursor, cursor)).toEqual({
            next: new Date("2010-01-01T00:00:01Z"),
            skipped: true,
        });
        expect(computeNextCursor(cursor, new Date("2010-01-01T00:00:01Z"))).toEqual({
            next: new Date("2010-01-01T00:00:01Z"),
            skipped: true,
        });
    });
});

describe("walkSince", () => {
    it("walks every page with overlap and stops on a short page", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, cursorRepository, deps } = setup(...buildRankedBeatmaps(1200));
        const seen = new Set<string>();

        const result = await walkSince(deps, {
            command: "test",
            shouldRestart: false,
            processPage: async (page) => {
                for (const beatmap of page) {
                    seen.add(beatmap.beatmap_id);
                }
            },
        });

        expect(seen.size).toBe(1200);
        expect(result.pages).toBe(3);
        expect(osuGateway.calls[0]).toBe(`getBeatmapsSince bulk ${formatOsuDate(walkStart)}`);
        // Resumes one second before the last beatmap, so a later run sees maps approved since.
        expect(await cursorRepository.getCursor("test")).toEqual(new Date(base + 1199 * 60_000 - 1000));
    });

    it("resumes from the saved cursor, or from the start with restart", async () => {
        const { osuGateway, cursorRepository, deps } = setup(...buildRankedBeatmaps(3));
        const saved = new Date(base + 60_000);

        await cursorRepository.setCursor("test", saved);
        await walkSince(deps, { command: "test", shouldRestart: false, processPage: async () => {} });

        expect(osuGateway.calls).toEqual([`getBeatmapsSince bulk ${formatOsuDate(saved)}`]);

        await walkSince(deps, { command: "test", shouldRestart: true, processPage: async () => {} });

        expect(osuGateway.calls[1]).toBe(`getBeatmapsSince bulk ${formatOsuDate(walkStart)}`);
    });

    it("waits out an unavailable osu! and asks for the same page again", async () => {
        const { cursorRepository, output, sleep } = setup();
        const getBeatmapsSince = vi
            .fn<(lane: string, since: Date) => Promise<OsuAPIResponse[]>>()
            .mockRejectedValueOnce(new OsuUnavailableError(7))
            .mockResolvedValueOnce(buildRankedBeatmaps(2));
        const pages: number[] = [];

        await walkSince(
            { osuGateway: { getBeatmapsSince }, cursorRepository, output, sleep },
            { command: "test", shouldRestart: false, processPage: async (page) => void pages.push(page.length) },
        );

        expect(sleep).toHaveBeenCalledWith(7000);
        expect(getBeatmapsSince.mock.calls.map(([, since]) => since)).toEqual([walkStart, walkStart]);
        expect(pages).toEqual([2]);
    });

    it("moves past 500 beatmaps approved in the same second, and says so", async () => {
        const { output, deps } = setup(...buildRankedBeatmaps(600, true));
        const seen = new Set<string>();

        await walkSince(deps, {
            command: "test",
            shouldRestart: false,
            processPage: async (page) => {
                for (const beatmap of page) {
                    seen.add(beatmap.beatmap_id);
                }
            },
        });

        // osu! only ever lists the first 500 of that second; the walk ends instead of looping.
        expect(seen.size).toBe(500);
        expect(output.lines.join("\n")).toMatch(/may have been skipped/);
    });

    it("keeps the cursor when a page fails, so a rerun repeats it", async () => {
        const { cursorRepository, deps } = setup(...buildRankedBeatmaps(3));

        await expect(
            walkSince(deps, {
                command: "test",
                shouldRestart: false,
                processPage: () => Promise.reject(new Error("database down")),
            }),
        ).rejects.toThrow("database down");
        expect(await cursorRepository.getCursor("test")).toBeNull();
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement**

`src/cli/SinceWalker.ts`:

```ts
import { formatOsuDate, parseOsuDate } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { OsuUnavailableError } from "@/osu/errors";
import type { IOsuGateway } from "@/osu/IOsuGateway";
import { sincePageSize } from "@/osu/OsuGateway";
import type { ICursorRepository } from "@/repositories/ICursorRepository";
import type { OsuAPIResponse } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import type { CliOutput } from "./types";

/**
 * Before the first ranked beatmap.
 */
export const walkStart = new Date("2007-01-01T00:00:00Z");

export interface SinceWalkDependencies {
    readonly osuGateway: Pick<IOsuGateway, "getBeatmapsSince">;
    readonly cursorRepository: ICursorRepository;
    readonly output: CliOutput;
    readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>;
}

export interface SinceWalkOptions {
    /** The cursor's name, e.g. "populate-cache". */
    readonly command: string;
    /** Start from `walkStart` instead of the saved cursor. */
    readonly shouldRestart: boolean;
    /** Handles one page; the cursor moves past the page only after this resolves. */
    readonly processPage: (page: readonly OsuAPIResponse[]) => Promise<void>;
}

/**
 * Where the next page starts: one second before the page's last approval date, so that pages overlap by their last
 * second and maps tied at a boundary are not lost whether osu! compares `since` inclusively or exclusively. If that
 * would not move past `cursor` (a whole page within one second), the later of `lastApproved` and `cursor` + 1 s,
 * which may skip maps.
 */
export function computeNextCursor(
    cursor: Date,
    lastApproved: Date,
): { next: Date; skipped: boolean } {
    const overlap = new Date(lastApproved.getTime() - 1000);

    if (overlap > cursor) {
        return { next: overlap, skipped: false };
    }

    return {
        next: new Date(Math.max(lastApproved.getTime(), cursor.getTime() + 1000)),
        skipped: true,
    };
}

/**
 * Walks every osu!standard beatmap approved since the saved cursor (ranked, approved, loved, and qualified), 500 per
 * osu! call on the bulk lane. A refused or rate-limited call waits out its `Retry-After` and is repeated; any other
 * failure stops the walk, and a rerun resumes from the saved cursor.
 */
export async function walkSince(
    deps: SinceWalkDependencies,
    options: SinceWalkOptions,
): Promise<{ pages: number; beatmaps: number }> {
    if (options.shouldRestart) {
        await deps.cursorRepository.clearCursor(options.command);
    }

    let cursor = (await deps.cursorRepository.getCursor(options.command)) ?? walkStart;
    let pages = 0;
    let beatmaps = 0;

    for (;;) {
        let page: OsuAPIResponse[];

        try {
            page = await deps.osuGateway.getBeatmapsSince("bulk", cursor);
        } catch (e) {
            if (e instanceof OsuUnavailableError) {
                deps.output.info(
                    `osu! is unavailable; retrying in ${e.retryAfterSeconds.toString()} s`,
                );
                await deps.sleep(e.retryAfterSeconds * 1000);

                continue;
            }

            throw e;
        }

        await options.processPage(page);
        pages++;
        beatmaps += page.length;

        const last = page.at(-1);

        if (last) {
            if (last.approved_date === null) {
                throw new Error(
                    `osu! listed beatmap ${last.beatmap_id} without an approval date`,
                );
            }

            const { next, skipped } = computeNextCursor(
                cursor,
                parseOsuDate(last.approved_date),
            );

            if (skipped) {
                deps.output.info(
                    `More than ${sincePageSize.toString()} beatmaps were approved at ${formatOsuDate(cursor)}; some may have been skipped`,
                );
            }

            cursor = next;
            await deps.cursorRepository.setCursor(options.command, cursor);
        }

        if (page.length < sincePageSize) {
            return { pages, beatmaps };
        }
    }
}
```

```ts
import { runCli } from "@/cli/run";
import { BeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/BeatmapRepository";
import { CursorRepository } from "@/repositories/CursorRepository";
import { buildApiBeatmap, computeFixtureMD5, buildOsuFile } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { createCliDependencies } from "@test/utils/cli";
import { testConfig } from "@test/utils/config";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@test/utils/database";
import { FakeOsuGateway } from "@test/fakes/FakeOsuGateway";
import { mkdtemp, readdir, readFile, rm, writeFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("populate-cache", () => {
    let dump: string;
    let beatmapDirectory: string;
    const files = {
        ranked: buildOsuFile(1, "Ranked"),
        approved: buildOsuFile(2, "Approved"),
        missing: buildOsuFile(3, "Missing"),
        mismatched: buildOsuFile(4, "Mismatched"),
    };

    function osuGateway() {
        return new FakeOsuGateway().addBeatmaps(
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "1", approved: "1", approved_date: "2008-01-01 00:00:00", file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(files.ranked) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "2", approved: "2", approved_date: "2008-01-02 00:00:00", file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(files.approved) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "3", approved: "1", approved_date: "2008-01-03 00:00:00", file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(files.missing) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "4", approved: "1", approved_date: "2008-01-04 00:00:00", file_md5: computeFixtureMD5(files.mismatched) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "5", approved: "4", approved_date: "2008-01-05 00:00:00", file_md5: "5".repeat(32) }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "6", approved: "3", approved_date: "2008-01-06 00:00:00", file_md5: "6".repeat(32) }),
        );
    }

    beforeEach(async () => {
        dump = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "dump-"));
        beatmapDirectory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "beatmaps-"));
        await writeFile(join(dump, "1.osu"), files.ranked);
        await writeFile(join(dump, "2.osu"), files.approved);
        await writeFile(join(dump, "4.osu"), Buffer.from("not the same file"));
    });

    afterEach(async () => {
        await rm(dump, { recursive: true, force: true });
        await rm(beatmapDirectory, { recursive: true, force: true });
    });

    function dependencies(db: Parameters<typeof createCliDependencies>[0], gateway = osuGateway()) {
        return createCliDependencies(db, {
            loadConfig: () => ({ ...testConfig, beatmapFileDirectory: beatmapDirectory }),
            createOsuGateway: () => gateway,
        });
    }

    it("stores ranked and approved beatmaps and copies their matching dump files", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const deps = dependencies(database.db);

        expect(await runCli(["populate-cache", "--files", dump], deps)).toBe(0);

        const beatmaps = new BeatmapRepository(database.db);

        expect((await beatmaps.findByIds([1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6])).map((r) => r.beatmapId).sort()).toEqual([1, 2, 3, 4]);
        expect((await readdir(beatmapDirectory)).sort()).toEqual(["1.osu", "2.osu"]);
        expect(await readFile(join(beatmapDirectory, "1.osu"))).toEqual(files.ranked);
        // Copying leaves the dump as it was.
        expect((await readdir(dump)).sort()).toEqual(["1.osu", "2.osu", "4.osu"]);
        expect(deps.output.lines.at(-1)).toBe(
            "Done: 4 beatmaps stored, 2 files copied, 1 missing, 1 mismatched",
        );
    });

    it("moves files with --move", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();

        await runCli(["populate-cache", "--files", dump, "--move"], dependencies(database.db));

        expect(await readdir(dump)).toEqual(["4.osu"]);
        expect((await readdir(beatmapDirectory)).sort()).toEqual(["1.osu", "2.osu"]);
    });

    it("stores metadata only without --files, and resumes from its cursor", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const gateway = osuGateway();

        await runCli(["populate-cache"], dependencies(database.db, gateway));

        expect(await readdir(beatmapDirectory)).toEqual([]);
        expect(await new CursorRepository(database.db).get("populate-cache")).toEqual(
            new Date("2008-01-05T23:59:59Z"),
        );

        await runCli(["populate-cache"], dependencies(database.db, gateway));

        expect(gateway.calls.at(-1)).toBe("getBeatmapsSince bulk 2008-01-05 23:59:59");
    });

    it.each([
        [["populate-cache", "--move"], "--move needs --files <dump directory>"],
        [["populate-cache", "--files", "/nonexistent/dump"], "/nonexistent/dump is not a directory"],
    ])("rejects %j", async (argv, message) => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const deps = dependencies(database.db);

        expect(await runCli(argv, deps)).toBe(1);
        expect(deps.output.errors).toContain(message);
    });
});
```

```ts
import { runCli } from "@/cli/run";
import { BeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/BeatmapRepository";
import { buildApiBeatmap } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { createCliDependencies } from "@test/utils/cli";
import { createTestDatabase } from "@test/utils/database";
import { FakeOsuGateway } from "@test/fakes/FakeOsuGateway";
import { buildBeatmapRow } from "@test/utils/rows";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const plus8 = (date: Date) => new Date(date.getTime() + 8 * 3_600_000);

describe("fix-dates", () => {
    it("corrects the dates of stored ranked, approved, and loved beatmaps and inserts nothing", async () => {
        await using database = await createTestDatabase();
        const beatmaps = new BeatmapRepository(database.db);
        const shifted = (id: string, approved: string) => {
            const correct = buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: id, approved });

            return {
                ...correct,
                submitDate: plus8(correct.submitDate),
                approvedDate: plus8(correct.approvedDate!),
                lastUpdate: plus8(correct.lastUpdate),
            };
        };

        await beatmaps.upsert([
            shifted("1", "1"),
            shifted("2", "4"),
            buildBeatmapRow({ beatmap_id: "3", approved: "2" }),
        ]);

        const osuGateway = new FakeOsuGateway().addBeatmaps(
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "1", approved: "1" }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "2", approved: "4" }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "3", approved: "2" }),
            buildApiBeatmap({ beatmap_id: "4", approved: "1" }),
        );
        const deps = createCliDependencies(database.db, { createOsuGateway: () => osuGateway });

        expect(await runCli(["fix-dates"], deps)).toBe(0);

        for (const id of [1, 2, 3]) {
            expect((await beatmaps.findById(id))?.submitDate).toEqual(
                new Date("2007-10-06T17:46:31Z"),
            );
        }

        expect(await beatmaps.findById(4)).toBeNull();
        expect(deps.output.lines.at(-1)).toBe("Done: 3 stored beatmaps checked, 2 corrected");
    });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`src/cli/commands/populateCache.ts`:

```ts
import { convertApiResponseToRow } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { computeMD5 } from "@/beatmaps/md5";
import { expectPositionals, parseArguments } from "@/cli/arguments";
import { CliError } from "@/cli/errors";
import { walkSince } from "@/cli/SinceWalker";
import type { Command } from "@/cli/types";
import type { BeatmapRow } from "@/database/types";
import { BeatmapFileStore } from "@/repositories/BeatmapFileStore";
import { BeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/BeatmapRepository";
import { CursorRepository } from "@/repositories/CursorRepository";
import { RankedStatus } from "@rian8337/osu-base";
import { readFile, rm, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import { join } from "node:path";

type FileOutcome = "copied" | "missing" | "mismatched";

function isMissing(error: unknown): boolean {
    return (
        typeof error === "object" &&
        error !== null &&
        "code" in error &&
        error.code === "ENOENT"
    );
}

/**
 * Copies (or moves) a beatmap's file from an extracted osu! file dump when its MD5 matches.
 */
async function importFile(
    store: BeatmapFileStore,
    directory: string,
    row: BeatmapRow,
    move: boolean,
): Promise<FileOutcome> {
    const source = join(directory, `${row.beatmapId.toString()}.osu`);
    let data: Buffer;

    try {
        data = await readFile(source);
    } catch (e) {
        if (isMissing(e)) {
            return "missing";
        }

        throw e;
    }

    if (computeMD5(data) !== row.fileMd5) {
        return "mismatched";
    }

    await store.write(row.beatmapId, data);

    if (move) {
        await rm(source);
    }

    return "copied";
}

/**
 * Stores every ranked and approved osu!standard beatmap from osu!'s approval-date listing (about 500 osu! calls),
 * and with `--files`, takes their `.osu` files from an extracted osu! file dump instead of downloading them. Files
 * that are missing from the dump or do not match are downloaded on demand later.
 */
export const populateCache: Command = (args) => {
    const { values, positionals } = parseArguments(args, {
        files: { type: "string" },
        move: { type: "boolean", default: false },
        restart: { type: "boolean", default: false },
    });

    expectPositionals(positionals, []);

    const { files: dump, move, restart } = values;

    if (move && dump === undefined) {
        throw new CliError("--move needs --files <dump directory>");
    }

    return async (deps) => {
        if (
            dump !== undefined &&
            !(await stat(dump).then((s) => s.isDirectory(), () => false))
        ) {
            throw new CliError(`${dump} is not a directory`);
        }

        const config = deps.loadConfig();
        const beatmaps = new BeatmapRepository(deps.db);
        const store = new BeatmapFileStore(config);
        const totals = { stored: 0, copied: 0, missing: 0, mismatched: 0 };
        const describeTotals = () =>
            `${totals.stored.toString()} beatmaps stored, ${totals.copied.toString()} files copied, ${totals.missing.toString()} missing, ${totals.mismatched.toString()} mismatched`;

        await walkSince(
            {
                osuGateway: deps.createOsuGateway(config),
                cursorRepository: new CursorRepository(deps.db),
                output: deps.output,
                sleep: deps.sleep,
            },
            {
                command: "populate-cache",
                shouldRestart: restart,
                processPage: async (page) => {
                    const rows = page
                        .filter((response) => {
                            const status = Number(response.approved);

                            return (
                                status === RankedStatus.Ranked ||
                                status === RankedStatus.Approved
                            );
                        })
                        .map((response) => convertApiResponseToRow(response));

                    await beatmaps.upsert(rows);
                    totals.stored += rows.length;

                    if (dump !== undefined) {
                        for (const row of rows) {
                            totals[await importFile(store, dump, row, move)]++;
                        }
                    }

                    const last = page.at(-1);

                    if (last) {
                        deps.output.info(
                            `Up to ${last.approved_date ?? "?"}: ${describeTotals()}`,
                        );
                    }
                },
            },
        );

        deps.output.info(`Done: ${describeTotals()}`);
    };
};
```

`src/cli/commands/fixDates.ts`:

```ts
import { convertApiResponseToRow } from "@/beatmaps/converter";
import { expectPositionals, parseArguments } from "@/cli/arguments";
import { walkSince } from "@/cli/SinceWalker";
import type { Command } from "@/cli/types";
import { BeatmapRepository } from "@/repositories/BeatmapRepository";
import { CursorRepository } from "@/repositories/CursorRepository";
import { RankedStatus } from "@rian8337/osu-base";

const neverRechecked = new Set<number>([
    RankedStatus.Ranked,
    RankedStatus.Approved,
    RankedStatus.Loved,
]);

const sameTime = (a: Date | null, b: Date | null) =>
    a?.getTime() === b?.getTime();

/**
 * Overwrites the dates and status of stored ranked, approved, and loved beatmaps with osu!'s (UTC) values. These rows
 * are never re-checked, so rows written in UTC+8 before the rewrite keep their wrong dates otherwise. Inserts
 * nothing; other rows heal at their next re-check.
 */
export const fixDates: Command = (args) => {
    const { values, positionals } = parseArguments(args, {
        restart: { type: "boolean", default: false },
    });

    expectPositionals(positionals, []);

    return async (deps) => {
        const config = deps.loadConfig();
        const beatmaps = new BeatmapRepository(deps.db);
        const totals = { checked: 0, corrected: 0 };

        await walkSince(
            {
                osuGateway: deps.createOsuGateway(config),
                cursorRepository: new CursorRepository(deps.db),
                output: deps.output,
                sleep: deps.sleep,
            },
            {
                command: "fix-dates",
                shouldRestart: values.restart,
                processPage: async (page) => {
                    const fresh = page
                        .filter((response) =>
                            neverRechecked.has(Number(response.approved)),
                        )
                        .map((response) => convertApiResponseToRow(response));
                    const stored = new Map(
                        (
                            await beatmaps.findByIds(
                                fresh.map((row) => row.beatmapId),
                            )
                        ).map((row) => [row.beatmapId, row]),
                    );

                    for (const row of fresh) {
                        const old = stored.get(row.beatmapId);

                        if (!old) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        totals.checked++;

                        if (
                            old.approved === row.approved &&
                            sameTime(old.submitDate, row.submitDate) &&
                            sameTime(old.approvedDate, row.approvedDate) &&
                            sameTime(old.lastUpdate, row.lastUpdate)
                        ) {
                            continue;
                        }

                        await beatmaps.update(row.beatmapId, {
                            approved: row.approved,
                            submitDate: row.submitDate,
                            approvedDate: row.approvedDate,
                            lastUpdate: row.lastUpdate,
                            lastChecked: new Date(),
                        });
                        totals.corrected++;
                    }

                    const last = page.at(-1);

                    if (last) {
                        deps.output.info(
                            `Up to ${last.approved_date ?? "?"}: ${totals.checked.toString()} checked, ${totals.corrected.toString()} corrected`,
                        );
                    }
                },
            },
        );

        deps.output.info(
            `Done: ${totals.checked.toString()} stored beatmaps checked, ${totals.corrected.toString()} corrected`,
        );
    };
};
```

Register both in `src/cli/run.ts`:

```ts

import { populateCache } from "./commands/populateCache";
```

```ts
    ["fix-dates", { buildAction: fixDates, requiresMigrations: true }],
```

```ts
import { createGoldenOsuState } from "@test/fixtures/osu/beatmaps";
import { createFakeOsuHandler } from "@test/fixtures/osu/fakeOsu";
import { bulkKey, goldenCases, priorityKey } from "@test/golden/cases";
import { normalizeResponse, buildExpectedResponse } from "@test/golden/expected";
import { startStubOsu } from "@test/utils/stubOsu";
import { findFreePort, startProcess, startTestPostgres } from "@/server/testing";
import { mkdtemp, rm } from "node:fs/promises";
import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

describe("the built server", () => {
    it("answers the recorded requests", async () => {
        await using postgres = await startTestPostgres();
        await using osu = await startStubOsu();
        const state = createGoldenOsuState();
        const directory = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), "e2e-contract-"));
        const port = await findFreePort();

        osu.handle(createFakeOsuHandler(state));

        try {
            await using _server = await startProcess({
                script: fileURLToPath(new URL("../../dist/index.mjs", import.meta.url)),
                env: {
                    PATH: process.env.PATH,
                    CI: "1",
                    EXPRESS_PORT: port.toString(),
                    POSTGRES_HOSTNAME: "127.0.0.1",
                    POSTGRES_PORT: postgres.port.toString(),
                    POSTGRES_DB_NAME: "postgres",
                    POSTGRES_USERNAME: "postgres",
                    OSU_API_KEY: "e2e-osu-key",
                    INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY: priorityKey,
                    BULK_SERVER_KEY: bulkKey,
                    OSU_API_BASE_URL: `${osu.url}/api`,
                    OSU_FILE_BASE_URL: `${osu.url}/osu`,
                    BEATMAP_FILE_DIRECTORY: directory,
                },
                readyUrl: `http://127.0.0.1:${port.toString()}/api/beatmap/health`,
            });

            for (const golden of goldenCases) {
                state.rateLimited = golden.rateLimited ?? false;

                const response = await fetch(`http://127.0.0.1:${port.toString()}${golden.path}`);

                expect(
                    normalizeResponse(
                        response.status,
                        (name) => response.headers.get(name) ?? undefined,
                        await response.text(),
                    ),
                    golden.name,
                ).toEqual(buildExpectedResponse(golden));
            }
        } finally {
            await rm(directory, { recursive: true, force: true });
        }
    });
});
```

2. **Routes**: the four routes under `/api/beatmap` with parameters, the `404`/`503`/`400`/`401`/`429` meanings from the spec's error table, and the sentence "A `404` with an `{ error }` body always means osu! has no such beatmap; anything temporary is a `503` with `Retry-After`."
3. **Access**: keys in `X-Api-Key` or `?key=`; built-in keys (`INTERNAL_SERVER_KEY` → priority, `BULK_SERVER_KEY` → bulk, unlimited); database keys via the CLI; anonymous callers (bulk lane, `ANON_RATE_LIMIT` per IP, `0` requires a key); `TRUST_PROXY` (`loopback` behind nginx on the same machine).
